📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

乙型肝炎病毒本期文献呈现多方向研究进展

本期重点文献按发表日期、相关性、证据等级和研究质量综合排序，研究结果与综述证据分别核验后形成以下进展。

  • 乙型肝炎知晓率汇总为 75.3%(95% CI:61.5%-86.9%),不同地区和人群存在显着异质性(I2=99.24%),乌干达中部和医护人员的知晓率明显高于其他地区和群体。 [paper-2fedcc97b9dc78ee]
  • 然而,其临床应用因显着的细胞毒性、诱导细胞凋亡、半衰期短和体内不良反应而受到严重限制。 [paper-2189495d0f9c5a69]
  • IL-17F 主要由 CD4+ T 细胞在 HBcAg 刺激后分泌,并在功能上促进纤维化相关细胞因子和标志物的表达。 [paper-fdbe5860b11012f0]
  • TDF 与小风险的肾毒性和骨毒性相关,主要发生在老年和高风险患者中。 [paper-66619f3ed7138565]
  • 血清学确诊的抗-HBs阳性率为51.10%(1,208/2,364),远低于世界卫生组织建议的70%阈值。空间分析显示,地理异质性显着(χ2 = 74.38,P < 0.001),免疫力较高的地区集中在规模较大的工业带(67.33%),免疫力较低的地区集中在分散的服务业区(36.81%)。SEM 结果表明,虽然外部线索强烈预测 HBV 知识(β = 0.894),但知识对预防意图(β = 0.192)只有边际影响,而预防意图又显着预测实际行为(β = 0.655)。 [paper-eb67546d253ee1df]

本期研究方向以入选文献实际覆盖的临床、流行病学、宿主生态、诊断或分子监测主题为准。

证据提醒：部分证据来自摘要、观察性研究或叙述性综述，结论应结合研究设计和证据等级理解。

This literature brief prioritizes publications in the active reporting window and ranks them by relevance, evidence availability, study quality, recency and source convergence.

  • The pooled prevalence of HBV awareness was 75.3% (95% CI: 61.5%-86.9%), with substantial heterogeneity across regions and population groups (I2=99.24%), and awareness was significantly higher in Central Uganda and among healthcare workers compared to other regions and groups. [paper-2fedcc97b9dc78ee]
  • However, its clinical application is severely limited by significant cytotoxicity, the induction of apoptosis, a short half-life, and adverse in vivo effects. [paper-2189495d0f9c5a69]
  • IL-17F was primarily secreted by CD4+ T cells upon HBcAg stimulation and functionally promoted the expression of fibrosis-related cytokines and markers. [paper-fdbe5860b11012f0]
  • TDF is associated with a small risk renal and bone toxicity, primarily in older and higher risk patients. [paper-66619f3ed7138565]
  • The serologically confirmed anti-HBs positive rate was 51.10% (1,208/2,364), substantially lower than the 70% threshold recommended by the World Health Organization. [paper-eb67546d253ee1df]

The direction of this week's research is defined by the clinical, epidemiological, ecological, diagnostic and molecular topics actually represented by the eligible publications.

  • ETSU 研究人员寻求乙型肝炎治愈方法 - 东田纳西州立大学。 [news-fc07d105b6e41b89]

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Antiviral research  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-13  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.antiviral.2026.106486  |  Citation: 2026, 106486
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

重组多克隆抗体 GIGA-2339 可有效、全基因型中和乙型肝炎病毒。

Recombinant polyclonal antibody GIGA-2339 potently and pan-genotypically neutralizes hepatitis B virus.
作者： Sheila M Keating, Brett W Higgins, Rena A Mizrahi, Ariel R Niedecken, Michael A Asensio, Peyton Witte, Ashley Gras, Ilana Segal, Sabrina M Nelson, Yao Chiang
摘要中文翻译现有的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)治疗可以控制慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者的病毒复制,但无法阻止乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)的产生;过量的 HBsAg 产生会导致 HBV 特异性免疫耐受。GIGA-2339 是一种一流的重组多克隆抗体 (pAb) 治疗药物,由 1,000 多种针对 HBsAg 表位的人 IgG1/IgK 抗体组成,专为治疗慢性 HBV 感染而开发。由于丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 利用 HBV 产生的 HBsAg 进行病毒侵入,因此 GIGA-2339 也可能为 HDV/HBV 共感染患者带来益处。使用 GigaGen 的单细胞微流控抗体捕获平台,分离了来自 HBV 疫苗接种者的记忆 B 细胞,并富集了 HBsAg 特异性抗体序列。使用靶向整合中国仓鼠卵巢 (CHO) 细胞系克隆并集体转染针对 HBsAg 的全长抗体序列,然后进行批量表达和纯化。在体外,GIGA-2339 结合 HBsAg 并中和活 HBV,其效力比基于血浆的乙型肝炎免疫球蛋白超免疫 (HyperHEP B) 高 2,000 倍,但比 HBsAg 单克隆抗体 (mAb) 的效力略低。GIGA-2339 中和全基因型 HBsAg (A-H) 包膜病毒颗粒,结合具有临床意义的 HBsAg 逃逸突变体,并与免疫细胞上的人类 Fc 受体结合。体内研究表明,与 HyperHEP B 相比,慢性阶段循环 HBsAg 的减少有所改善。新型多克隆抗体治疗剂 GIGA-2339 有效结合并中和 HBsAg,以预防病毒感染并减少循环抗原。GIGA-2339代表了慢性乙型肝炎治疗领域的一种重要的新治疗方式,目前正处于临床开发阶段。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
现有的乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)治疗可以控制慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)患者的病毒复制,但无法阻止乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)的产生;过量的 HBsAg 产生会导致 HBV 特异性免疫耐受。
设计与对象
由于丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 利用 HBV 产生的 HBsAg 进行病毒侵入,因此 GIGA-2339 也可能为 HDV/HBV 共感染患者带来益处。
核心方法
使用靶向整合中国仓鼠卵巢 (CHO) 细胞系克隆并集体转染针对 HBsAg 的全长抗体序列,然后进行批量表达和纯化。
主要结果
体内研究表明,与 HyperHEP B 相比,慢性阶段循环 HBsAg 的减少有所改善。
解释与创新
新型多克隆抗体治疗剂 GIGA-2339 可有效结合并中和 HBsAg,以预防病毒感染并减少循环抗原。
科研与公卫意义
GIGA-2339代表了慢性乙型肝炎治疗领域的一种重要的新治疗方式,目前正处于临床开发阶段。
局限与证据强度
GIGA-2339 中和全基因型 HBsAg (A-H) 包膜病毒颗粒,结合具有临床意义的 HBsAg 逃逸突变体,并与免疫细胞上的人类 Fc 受体结合。

Recombinant polyclonal antibody GIGA-2339 potently and pan-genotypically neutralizes hepatitis B virus.

Authors: Sheila M Keating, Brett W Higgins, Rena A Mizrahi, Ariel R Niedecken, Michael A Asensio, Peyton Witte, Ashley Gras, Ilana Segal, Sabrina M Nelson, Yao Chiang
Original Abstract
Existing hepatitis B virus (HBV) treatments control viral replication in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients but fail to prevent hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) production; excessive HBsAg production leads to HBV-specific immune tolerance. GIGA-2339 is a first-in-class, recombinant polyclonal antibody (pAb) therapeutic consisting of over 1,000 human IgG1/IgK antibodies targeting HBsAg epitopes developed for the treatment of chronic HBV infection. As Hepatitis D virus (HDV) utilizes HBsAg produced by HBV for viral entry, GIGA-2339 may also provide benefit to HDV/HBV co-infected patients. Memory B cells from HBV vaccinated donors were isolated and HBsAg-specific antibody sequences were enriched using GigaGen's single cell microfluidic antibody capture platform. Full-length antibody sequences targeting HBsAg were cloned and transfected en masse using a targeted integration Chinese hamster ovary (CHO) cell line, followed by bulk expression and purification. In vitro, GIGA-2339 bound HBsAg and neutralized live HBV with over 2,000-fold greater potency than a plasma-based hepatitis B immunoglobulin hyperimmune (HyperHEP B) and slightly lower potency than a HBsAg monoclonal antibody (mAb). GIGA-2339 neutralized pan-genotypic HBsAg (A-H) enveloped viral particles, bound clinically significant HBsAg escape mutants, and engaged human Fc receptors on immune cells. In vivo studies showed improved reduction of circulating HBsAg in a chronic stage as compared to HyperHEP B. The novel polyclonal antibody therapeutic GIGA-2339 potently binds and neutralizes HBsAg to prevent viral infection and to reduce circulating antigen. GIGA-2339 represents an important new therapeutic modality for the CHB treatment landscape and is currently in clinical development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Existing hepatitis B virus (HBV) treatments control viral replication in chronic hepatitis B (CHB) patients but fail to prevent hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) production; excessive HBsAg production leads to HBV-specific immune tolerance.
Design and population
As Hepatitis D virus (HDV) utilizes HBsAg produced by HBV for viral entry, GIGA-2339 may also provide benefit to HDV/HBV co-infected patients.
Core methods
Full-length antibody sequences targeting HBsAg were cloned and transfected en masse using a targeted integration Chinese hamster ovary (CHO) cell line, followed by bulk expression and purification.
Main results
In vivo studies showed improved reduction of circulating HBsAg in a chronic stage as compared to HyperHEP B.
Interpretation and novelty
The novel polyclonal antibody therapeutic GIGA-2339 potently binds and neutralizes HBsAg to prevent viral infection and to reduce circulating antigen.
Scientific and public-health significance
GIGA-2339 represents an important new therapeutic modality for the CHB treatment landscape and is currently in clinical development.
Limitations and evidence strength
GIGA-2339 neutralized pan-genotypic HBsAg (A-H) enveloped viral particles, bound clinically significant HBsAg escape mutants, and engaged human Fc receptors on immune cells.

Journal: BMC infectious diseases  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-07-18  |  Date basis: online_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  DOI: 10.1186/s12879-026-14019-6  |  Citation: 2026
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

乌干达乙型肝炎病毒认识与疾病特定知识的相关性:对 2018 年至 2025 年发表的研究进行荟萃分析和荟萃回归。

Correlates of hepatitis B virus awareness and disease specific knowledge in Uganda: a meta-analysis and meta-regression of studies published between 2018 and 2025.
作者： Hussein Mukasa Kafeero, Hakim Sendagire, Dorothy Ndagire, Abdul Walusansa, Ali Kudamba, Fahad Muwanda, Mariam Namusoke, Ponsiano Ocama
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)仍然是乌干达的一个主要公共卫生挑战,尽管国家采取了疫苗接种和筛查举措,但其患病率因地区和人群而异。了解意识和知识水平对于指导乙肝预防和控制策略至关重要。这项研究综合了乌干达不同人群对乙肝病毒的认识和疾病具体知识之间的相关性的现有证据。方法:我们系统地检索了 PubMed、非洲期刊在线 (AJOL) 和 Google Scholar,查找 2018 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的关于乌干达 HBV 认知或特定疾病知识的横断面研究。研究选择、数据提取和质量评估由两名评审员使用 Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) 分析横断面研究批判评估清单独立进行。最终综合报告中纳入了十八项符合资格标准的研究。使用 MedCalc 软件(版本 10.10)中的随机效应模型计算 HBV 认知度和特定疾病知识相关性的汇总估计,并使用 I2 统计量量化异质性。通过亚组分析和敏感性分析探讨异质性的来源。进行元回归以检查按地区、出版年份和研究人群划分的 HBV 认知趋势。使用 Begg 和 Egger 测试评估发表偏倚。统计显着性设置为 p < 0.05,所有估计值均以 95% 置信区间报告。结果:十八项研究符合纳入标准。乙型肝炎知晓率汇总率为 75.3%(95% CI:61.5%-86.9%),不同地区和人群之间存在显着异质性(I2=99.24%)。亚组分析显示,与北部、东部和西部地区相比,乌干达中部地区的知晓率显着较高(p < 0.05),与一般人群和产前护理人员相比,医护人员的知晓率显着较高(p < 0.05)。对特定 HBV 领域的了解差异很大:与先前没有 HBV 知识的参与者相比,知识丰富的参与者对病原体、筛查服务和疫苗接种可用性的认识显着较高 (p < 0.05),而与至少接受部分疫苗接种的参与者相比,对暴露后预防和完成完整 HBV 疫苗接种计划的认识仍然较低 (p < 0.05)。尽管对通过受感染体液传播的了解仍然很少,但对性接触、共用针头和母婴传播等关键传播途径的了解程度较低。与教育水平较低的参与者相比,受教育程度较高的参与者更有可能接种疫苗(p < 0.05),对疫苗有效性持积极态度的参与者比持消极态度的参与者更有可能接种疫苗(p < 0.05)。荟萃回归分析进一步揭示了研究期间乙肝病毒认知度整体下降的矛盾现象。结论:对乙肝病毒的认识和关键知识领域仍然存在严重差距,这突出表明在传播健康信息时需要针对当代社会动态制定替代策略。临床试验编号:不适用。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
尽管有国家疫苗接种和筛查举措,乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)仍然是乌干达的一个主要公共卫生挑战,了解意识和特定疾病的知识水平对于指导预防和控制策略至关重要。
设计与对象
本研究通过对 2018 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月期间发表的研究进行系统回顾和荟萃分析,综合了乌干达 HBV 认识和特定疾病知识的横断面证据。
核心方法
两名评审员独立选择研究、提取数据并使用 Joanna Briggs Institute 分析横断面研究关键评估清单评估质量,并使用 MedCalc 软件中的随机效应模型计算汇总估计值,并使用 I2 统计量量化异质性。
主要结果
乙型肝炎知晓率汇总为 75.3%(95% CI:61.5%-86.9%),不同地区和人群存在显着异质性(I2=99.24%),乌干达中部和医护人员的知晓率明显高于其他地区和群体。
解释与创新
该研究发现了乙型肝炎意识和特定知识领域的关键差距,包括对暴露后预防和通过受感染体液传播的意识较低,并强调了研究期间乙型肝炎意识的整体下降。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要制定有针对性的、针对区域的健康传播战略,以解决乙型肝炎预防和控制方面持续存在的知识差距和意识下降的趋势。
局限与证据强度
尽管有严格的系统评价方法和亚组分析,但显着的异质性(I2=99.24%)和观察到的意识随着时间的推移而下降限制了证据的强度。

Correlates of hepatitis B virus awareness and disease specific knowledge in Uganda: a meta-analysis and meta-regression of studies published between 2018 and 2025.

Authors: Hussein Mukasa Kafeero, Hakim Sendagire, Dorothy Ndagire, Abdul Walusansa, Ali Kudamba, Fahad Muwanda, Mariam Namusoke, Ponsiano Ocama
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major public health challenge in Uganda, where prevalence varies by region and population group despite national vaccination and screening initiatives. Understanding awareness and knowledge levels is essential for guiding HBV prevention and control strategies. This study synthesized available evidence on the correlates of HBV awareness and disease specific knowledge among different Ugandan populations. METHODS: We systematically searched PubMed, African Journals Online (AJOL), and Google Scholar for cross-sectional studies reporting on HBV awareness or disease-specific knowledge in Uganda, published between January 2018 and December 2025. Study selection, data extraction, and quality appraisal were conducted independently by two reviewers using the Joanna Briggs Institute (JBI) Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical Cross-Sectional Studies. Eighteen studies meeting the eligibility criteria were included in the final synthesis. Pooled estimates of HBV awareness and correlates of disease-specific knowledge were computed using a random-effects model in MedCalc software (version 10.10), with heterogeneity quantified using the I2 statistic. Sources of heterogeneity were explored through subgroup analysis and sensitivity analysis. Meta-regression was performed to examine trends in HBV awareness by region, year of publication, and study population. Publication bias was assessed using Begg's and Egger's tests. Statistical significance was set at p < 0.05, and all estimates are reported with 95% confidence intervals. RESULTS: Eighteen studies met the inclusion criteria. The pooled prevalence of HBV awareness was 75.3% (95% CI: 61.5%-86.9%), with substantial heterogeneity observed across regions and population groups (I2=99.24%). Subgroup analysis revealed that awareness was significantly higher in Central Uganda compared to the northern, eastern, and western regions (p < 0.05), and among healthcare workers compared to the general population and antenatal care attendees (p < 0.05). Knowledge of specific HBV domains varied considerably: awareness of the causative agent, screening services, and vaccination availability was significantly higher among knowledgeable participants compared to those with no prior HBV knowledge (p < 0.05), whereas awareness of post-exposure prophylaxis and completion of the full HBV vaccination schedule remained low compared to those who had received at least partial vaccination (p < 0.05). Awareness of key transmission routes including sexual contact, needle sharing, and mother-to-child transmission was moderate, though knowledge of transmission through infected body fluids remained poor. Participants who were of higher education level were more likely to take up vaccination compared to lower education level (p < 0.05), and participants with a positive attitude toward vaccine effectiveness were significantly more likely to be vaccinated compared to those with a negative attitude (p < 0.05). The meta-regression analysis further revealed a paradoxical overall decline in HBV awareness over the study period. CONCLUSION: Critical gaps still persist in HBV awareness and key knowledge areas highlighting the need for alternative strategies tailored to the social dynamics of the current generation during the delivery of health messages. CLINICAL TRIAL NUMBER: Not applicable.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) remains a major public health challenge in Uganda despite national vaccination and screening initiatives, and understanding awareness and disease-specific knowledge levels is essential for guiding prevention and control strategies.
Design and population
This study synthesized cross-sectional evidence on HBV awareness and disease-specific knowledge in Uganda through a systematic review and meta-analysis of studies published between January 2018 and December 2025.
Core methods
Two reviewers independently selected studies, extracted data, and appraised quality using the Joanna Briggs Institute Critical Appraisal Checklist for Analytical Cross-Sectional Studies, with pooled estimates computed using a random-effects model in MedCalc software and heterogeneity quantified using the I2 statistic.
Main results
The pooled prevalence of HBV awareness was 75.3% (95% CI: 61.5%-86.9%), with substantial heterogeneity across regions and population groups (I2=99.24%), and awareness was significantly higher in Central Uganda and among healthcare workers compared to other regions and groups.
Interpretation and novelty
The study identified critical gaps in HBV awareness and specific knowledge domains, including low awareness of post-exposure prophylaxis and transmission through infected body fluids, and highlighted a paradoxical overall decline in HBV awareness over the study period.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for targeted, regionally tailored health communication strategies to address persistent knowledge gaps and declining awareness trends in HBV prevention and control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The substantial heterogeneity (I2=99.24%) and the observed decline in awareness over time limit the strength of the evidence, despite rigorous systematic review methods and subgroup analyses.

澳大利亚慢性乙型肝炎护理和治疗依从性的纵向模式:国家关联数据队列研究。

Longitudinal Patterns of Chronic Hepatitis B Care and Treatment Adherence in Australia: A National Linked Data Cohort Study.
作者： Jennifer H MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Stacey L Rowe, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C Cowie
摘要中文翻译目前澳大利亚慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 治疗和护理的接受率远低于目标水平,导致可预防的肝病和癌症的持续负担。了解个人层面的护理参与轨迹和社会人口因素的变化对于指导改善护理获取和保留的努力至关重要。这项研究分析了澳大利亚国家健康保险系统提供的乙型肝炎病毒载量(VL)检测和抗病毒治疗的记录,这些记录与来自各个政府数据集的移民、死亡和社会人口统计数据相关。对不同人群的治疗和 VL 测试进行了评估,并使用慢性乙型肝炎患者人数的模型估计值来计算摄取量。还评估了基于分发的脚本的 VL 测试频率和治疗依从性。2023 年,澳大利亚估计有 219,800 名慢性乙型肝炎患者,其中 118,334 人 (53.8%) 在 2014 年至 2023 年期间接受了 VL 检测或治疗。原住民和托雷斯海峡岛民对 VL 检测和治疗的估计接受率较低,并且因出生地区而异。在接受任何 VL 检测的 81,360 人中,只有 17,352 人 (21.3%) 定期接受检测,而 31,715 人 (39.0%) 有先前监测的证据,但自 2019 年以来没有进行 VL 检测。大多数开始治疗的人继续治疗,并获得足够的供应以坚持治疗。这项研究表明,澳大利亚在慢性乙型肝炎护理方面存在巨大差距,特别是在对未接受治疗的患者进行持续监测方面。优先人群的吸收差异很明显。应优先考虑重新参与那些失去随访的人。

Longitudinal Patterns of Chronic Hepatitis B Care and Treatment Adherence in Australia: A National Linked Data Cohort Study.

Authors: Jennifer H MacLachlan, Nicole Romero, Stacey L Rowe, Nicole Allard, Benjamin C Cowie
Original Abstract
Current uptake of chronic hepatitis B (CHB) treatment and care in Australia is well below target levels, leading to an ongoing burden of preventable liver disease and cancer. Understanding individual-level trajectories of care engagement and variation by sociodemographic factors is essential to guide efforts to improve access to and retention in care. This study analysed records of hepatitis B viral load (VL) testing and antiviral treatment provided through Australia's national health insurance system, which were linked to migration, death, and sociodemographic data from various government datasets. Treatment and VL testing by population group were assessed, and uptake was calculated using modelled estimates of the number living with CHB. VL testing frequency and treatment adherence based on scripts dispensed were also assessed. Of the estimated 219,800 people living with CHB in Australia in 2023, 118,334 (53.8%) received either VL testing or treatment during 2014-2023. Estimated uptake of VL testing and treatment uptake were lower among Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander people and also varied by region of birth. Of the 81,360 who received any VL testing, only 17,352 (21.3%) received it regularly, while 31,715 (39.0%) had evidence of prior monitoring but no VL testing since 2019. The majority of those who initiated treatment continued therapy and received supply sufficient for adherence. This study shows that substantial gaps exist in CHB care in Australia, particularly in continued monitoring among those not on treatment. Disparities in uptake are evident by priority population. Re-engaging those lost to follow-up should be prioritised.

乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)的耐药突变影响人类丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)病毒粒子的产量及其传染性

Drug resistance mutations of hepatitis B virus (HBV) influence the yield of the virions of the human hepatitis delta virus (HDV) and their infectivity
作者： Oleksandra Chazova, Igor Zaiets, Severin O. Gudima
摘要中文翻译未标记:这项工作分析了乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 表面抗原(即 HBsAg)中的 30 种耐药突变 (DRM),这些突变是由 FDA 批准的几种抗 HBV 核苷类似物选择的。所有携带 DRM 的 HBsAg 变体都支持丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 的形成和分泌,只有 13/30 DRM 显着降低了分泌的 HDV 病毒粒子的产量。然而,大多数DRM(23/30)大大降低了HDV的感染性,其中四种甚至导致感染性完全丧失,这是通过分析感染细胞中HDV RNA基因组的水平以及HDV感染细胞的比例来确定的。观察到位于大胞质环 (LCL)、主要抗原环 (MAL)、HDV 结合位点 (HDV-BS) 和 HBsAg 跨膜结构域 II、III 和 IV 中的 DRM 具有抑制 HDV 感染性的能力,这表明 DRM 通过直接和间接机制介导其抑制影响,这些机制可能改变病毒颗粒的附着/进入、进入后运输和/或随后的脱壳。该报告还确立了跨膜结构域 II、III 和 IV 作为 HDV 病毒粒子整体感染性的关键调节因子。很明显,在自然 HBV/HDV 感染的情况下,DRM 可以大大减少 HDV 的传播/重复感染,并缩小慢性感染肝脏中 HDV 储存库的大小。总体而言,我们的研究结果加深了对抗乙型肝炎药物治疗如何影响丁型肝炎病毒生命周期、乙型肝炎病毒包膜蛋白在促进丁型肝炎病毒组装和感染性中的作用以及复杂的乙型肝炎病毒-丁型肝炎病毒相互作用机制的理解。重要性:研究表明,乙型肝炎病毒(HBsAg)表面抗原的耐药突变(DRM)可以大大降低丁型肝炎病毒(HDV)的组装和传染性。由于大多数测试的 DRM 显着降低了丁型肝炎病毒的感染性,其中四种导致感染性完全丧失,因此这种作用可以抑制丁型肝炎病毒传播/重复感染,并减少受感染肝脏中丁型肝炎病毒的储存量。如果对感染性最有害的 DRM 存在于大多数 HBsAg 分子上,这可能有助于显着抑制慢性 HDV 感染,并可能减少 HDV 相关的肝脏发病机制。还确定位于 HBsAg 跨膜域 II、III 和 IV 中的 DRM 可以严重改变 HDV 感染性。因此,我们的研究结果表明,不同的 HBsAg 区域在分子内协调作用中发挥作用,促进病毒体的组装和感染性,并且 HBsAg 不同区域之间发生某些短距离和长距离相互作用。

Drug resistance mutations of hepatitis B virus (HBV) influence the yield of the virions of the human hepatitis delta virus (HDV) and their infectivity

Authors: Oleksandra Chazova, Igor Zaiets, Severin O. Gudima
Original Abstract
UNLABELLED: This work analyzed 30 drug resistance mutations (DRMs) in the surface antigen of hepatitis B virus (HBV) (i.e., HBsAg) selected by several FDA-approved anti-HBV nucleos(t)ide analogs. All DRM-bearing HBsAg variants supported the formation and secretion of hepatitis delta virus (HDV), and only 13/30 DRMs considerably decreased the yield of secreted HDV virions. However, the majority of DRMs (23/30) greatly reduced HDV infectivity, and four of them even caused the complete loss of infectivity, which was determined by analyzing the levels of HDV RNA genomes in the infected cells, and the fraction of HDV-infected cells. The ability to inhibit HDV infectivity was observed for DRMs located in the large cytosolic loop (LCL), major antigenic loop (MAL), HDV-binding site (HDV-BS), and the transmembrane domains II, III, and IV of HBsAg, which suggested that DRMs mediate their inhibitory influence by both direct and indirect mechanisms that could alter the attachment/entry, post-entry trafficking, and/or subsequent uncoating of the virions. This report also established the transmembrane domains II, III, and IV as critical regulators of the overall infectivity of HDV virions. It became apparent that in the settings of natural HBV/HDV infection, DRMs can therefore greatly reduce the spread/superinfection of HDV and diminish the size of the HDV reservoir in chronically infected livers. Overall, our findings advanced the understanding of how anti-HBV drug treatments influence the HDV life cycle, the roles of HBV envelope proteins in facilitating the assembly and infectivity of HDV, and the mechanism of complex HBV-HDV interactions. IMPORTANCE: The study showed that the drug resistance mutations (DRMs) in the surface antigen of hepatitis B virus (HBsAg) can greatly reduce hepatitis delta virus (HDV) assembly and infectivity. Since most of the tested DRMs considerably reduced HDV infectivity, and four of them resulted in a complete loss of the infectivity, such effects can lead to suppression of HDV spread/superinfection and reduction of the HDV reservoir in the infected liver. If most detrimental for infectivity DRMs are present on the majority of HBsAg molecules, this could facilitate substantial suppression of chronic HDV infection and possibly reduce HDV-associated liver pathogenesis. It was also determined that DRMs located in the transmembrane domains II, III, and IV of HBsAg could critically alter HDV infectivity. Our findings therefore suggest that different HBsAg regions work in the intramolecular coordinating concert, facilitating the assembly and infectivity of the virions, and that certain short- and long-distance interactions between different regions of HBsAg take place.

慢性乙型肝炎患者维生素D缺乏症引起的继发性甲状旁腺功能亢进症并伴有股骨骨折:病例报告。

Hypovitaminosis D-Induced Secondary Hyperparathyroidism With Concomitant Femoral Fracture in a Chronic Hepatitis B Patient: A Case Report.
作者： Walter Appati, Kojo Awotwi Hutton-Mensah, Landrop Aidoo, Naa Akweley Suma Otchwemah
摘要中文翻译维生素 D 缺乏和继发性甲状旁腺功能亢进 (SHPT) 是代谢性骨病的可预防原因,但它们在临床上可能保持沉默,直到发生灾难性骨骼事件。即使没有肝硬化,慢性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染也越来越多地与骨和矿物质代谢紊乱相关。我们报告了一名患有慢性乙型肝炎病毒感染的 39 岁男性,他患有低创伤性股骨粗隆下骨折。他没有饮酒或吸烟史,最近才开始抗病毒治疗。生化评估显示维生素 D 严重缺乏、甲状旁腺激素水平显着升高、低磷血症和碱性磷酸酶升高,这与 SHPT 和代谢性骨病一致。没有肝硬化的临床或放射学证据。该患者接受了骨折手术固定,补充维生素 D,并开始服用替诺福韦艾拉酚胺,临床恢复良好。

Hypovitaminosis D-Induced Secondary Hyperparathyroidism With Concomitant Femoral Fracture in a Chronic Hepatitis B Patient: A Case Report.

Authors: Walter Appati, Kojo Awotwi Hutton-Mensah, Landrop Aidoo, Naa Akweley Suma Otchwemah
Original Abstract
Vitamin D deficiency and secondary hyperparathyroidism (SHPT) are preventable causes of metabolic bone disease, yet they may remain clinically silent until catastrophic skeletal events occur. Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection has been increasingly linked to disordered bone and mineral metabolism, even in the absence of cirrhosis. We report a 39-year-old man with chronic HBV infection who presented with a low-trauma subtrochanteric femoral fracture. He had no history of alcohol or tobacco use and had only recently commenced antiviral therapy. Biochemical evaluation revealed severe vitamin D deficiency, markedly elevated parathyroid hormone levels, hypophosphatemia, and raised alkaline phosphatase, consistent with SHPT and metabolic bone disease. There was no clinical or radiological evidence of liver cirrhosis. The patient underwent surgical fixation of the fracture, received vitamin D supplementation, and was initiated on Tenofovir Alafenamide, with good clinical recovery.

新诊断癌症患者隐匿性乙型肝炎病毒感染的患病率和血清学特征。

Prevalence and Serological Profile of Occult Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Newly Diagnosed Cancer Patients.
作者： Subhashree Sahu, Shilpee Kumar, Deepthi Nair, Deepthi Nairr
摘要中文翻译背景技术隐匿性乙型肝炎病毒感染(OBI)代表了诊断和临床挑战,特别是在免疫抑制治疗期间面临病毒再激活风险的癌症患者中。印度关于恶性肿瘤中 OBI 的患病率和特征的数据有限。本研究旨在确定三级医院新诊断癌症患者中 OBI 的患病率并描述其血清学特征。材料与方法 2023 年进行了一项横断面研究,包括 100 名化疗或放疗前新诊断的癌症患者。使用定量实时 PCR 对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阴性的血清样本进行 HBV DNA 筛查。对 OBI 阳性病例进行 IgM 抗 HBc、总抗 HBc 和抗 HBs 抗体的血清学检测。对人口统计学和临床​​变量进行了描述性分析。结果 7% (7/100) 的患者检测到 OBI,HBV DNA 水平范围为 79 至 64,533 IU/mL(平均值:10,823 IU/mL;中位数:519 IU/mL)。在 OBI 阳性个体中,42.8% 为血清阳性(抗 HBc 和/或抗 HBs 阳性),57.1% 为血清阴性。结论 在初治癌症患者中观察到 OBI 患病率为 7%,与现有数据相比,其 HBV DNA 水平明显较高。OBI 在接种疫苗和血清阴性个体中的存在凸显了基于血清学的筛查的不足。强烈建议将 HBV DNA 检测纳入常规化疗前和放疗筛查中,以防止病毒再激活并提高癌症治疗的安全性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
隐匿性乙型肝炎病毒感染 (OBI) 提出了诊断和临床挑战,特别是对于在免疫抑制治疗期间面临病毒再激活风险的癌症患者。本研究旨在确定三级医院新诊断癌症患者中 OBI 的患病率并描述其血清学特征。
设计与对象
2023 年进行了一项横断面研究,纳入了 100 名接受化疗或放疗前新诊断的癌症患者。
核心方法
使用定量实时 PCR 对乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 阴性的血清样本进行 HBV DNA 筛查。对 OBI 阳性病例进行 IgM 抗 HBc、总抗 HBc 和抗 HBs 抗体的血清学检测。
主要结果
7% (7/100) 的患者检测到 OBI,HBV DNA 水平范围为 79 至 64,533 IU/mL(平均值:10,823 IU/mL;中位数:519 IU/mL)。在 OBI 阳性个体中,42.8% 为血清阳性(抗 HBc 和/或抗 HBs 阳性),57.1% 为血清阴性。
解释与创新
在未经治疗的癌症患者中观察到 OBI 患病率为 7%,与现有数据相比,其 HBV DNA 水平明显较高。
科研与公卫意义
OBI 在接种疫苗和血清阴性个体中的存在凸显了基于血清学的筛查的不足。强烈建议将 HBV DNA 检测纳入常规化疗前和放疗筛查中,以防止病毒再激活并提高癌症治疗的安全性。

所提供的证据未报告任何限制或证据强度。

Prevalence and Serological Profile of Occult Hepatitis B Virus Infection in Newly Diagnosed Cancer Patients.

Authors: Subhashree Sahu, Shilpee Kumar, Deepthi Nair, Deepthi Nairr
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND Occult hepatitis B virus infection (OBI) represents a diagnostic and clinical challenge, particularly among cancer patients who are at risk of viral reactivation during immunosuppressive therapy. Limited data are available from India on the prevalence and characteristics of OBI in malignancy. This study aimed to determine the prevalence and describe the serological profile of OBI among newly diagnosed cancer patients at a tertiary care hospital. MATERIALS & METHODS A cross-sectional study was conducted in 2023, including 100 newly diagnosed cancer patients prior to chemotherapy or radiotherapy. Serum samples negative for Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) were screened for HBV DNA using quantitative real-time PCR. Serological testing for IgM anti-HBc, Total anti-HBc and anti-HBs antibodies was performed on OBI-positive cases. Demographic and clinical variables were analyzed descriptively. RESULTS OBI was detected in 7% (7/100) of patients, with HBV DNA levels ranging from 79 to 64,533 IU/mL (mean: 10,823 IU/mL; median: 519 IU/mL). Among OBI-positive individuals, 42.8% were seropositive (anti-HBc and/or anti-HBs positive) and 57.1% were seronegative. CONCLUSION A 7% prevalence of OBI was observed among treatment-naïve cancer patients, with notably high HBV DNA levels compared to existing data. The presence of OBI in both vaccinated and seronegative individuals highlights the inadequacy of serology-based screening. Incorporating HBV DNA testing into routine pre-chemotherapy and radiation therapy screening is strongly recommended to prevent reactivation and improve safety in cancer management.

HBcAg 诱导的 IL-17F 在隐匿性 HBV 感染中导致肝纤维化

HBcAg-Induced IL-17F drives liver fibrosis in occult HBV infection
作者： Bochao Liu, Jieting Huang, Hualong Yang, Qiao Liao, Min Wang, R. Xu, Zhengang Shan, Huishan Zhong, Fengfang Liao, Tingting Li
摘要中文翻译摘要 虽然隐匿性乙型肝炎病毒感染是进行性肝病的已知危险因素,但人们对这种关联的机制基础知之甚少。为了调查这一点,我们进行了一项比较研究,涉及 40 名 OBI 献血者和 100 名健康献血者,以评估肝纤维化的临床参数,包括血清生物标志物、FIB-4 指数和肝脏硬度测量。对这些供体的外周血单核细胞进行转录组测序,发现 OBI 组中 IL-17F 显着上调。从机制上讲,我们发现 IL-17F 主要由 CD4 + T 细胞在 HBcAg 刺激后分泌,并在功能上促进关键纤维化相关细胞因子和标志物的表达。总的来说,我们的研究揭示了一种新途径,其中 HBcAg 诱导 CD4 + T 细胞分泌 IL-17F,进而加速肝纤维形成,为 OBI 相关肝病提供机制基础。重要性 这项研究首次揭示了 OBI 驱动肝纤维化的新机制,证明 HBcAg 刺激 CD4+T 细胞分泌 IL-17F,进而激活巨噬细胞和肝星状细胞,从而促进肝脏炎症和纤维化。这一发现不仅阐明了OBI相关肝病进展的关键分子通路,而且为开发IL-17F靶向诊断方法和抗纤维化治疗提供了重要的理论基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
隐匿性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染与进行性肝病相关,但这种联系的机制基础仍不清楚,促使该研究调查其潜在途径。
设计与对象
对 40 名隐匿性 HBV 感染 (OBI) 献血者和 100 名健康献血者进行了比较研究,以评估肝纤维化的临床参数。
核心方法
对血清生物标志物、FIB-4 指数和肝脏硬度测量进行了评估,外周血单核细胞的转录组测序发现 IL-17F 在 OBI 组中显着上调。
主要结果
IL-17F 主要由 CD4+ T 细胞在 HBcAg 刺激后分泌,并在功能上促进纤维化相关细胞因子和标志物的表达。
解释与创新
该研究揭示了 HBcAg 诱导 CD4+ T 细胞分泌 IL-17F 的新途径,从而加速肝纤维化,为 OBI 相关肝病提供了机制基础。
科研与公卫意义
这一发现阐明了 OBI 相关肝病进展的关键分子途径,并支持开发针对 IL-17F 的诊断和抗纤维化治疗策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据没有报告研究局限性、样本量合理性或统计功效,这限制了人们对研究结果的信心。

HBcAg-Induced IL-17F drives liver fibrosis in occult HBV infection

Authors: Bochao Liu, Jieting Huang, Hualong Yang, Qiao Liao, Min Wang, R. Xu, Zhengang Shan, Huishan Zhong, Fengfang Liao, Tingting Li
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT While occult hepatitis B virus infection is a known risk factor for progressive liver diseases, the mechanistic basis for this association is poorly understood. To investigate this, we conducted a comparative study involving 40 OBI blood donors and 100 healthy blood donors to assess clinical parameters of liver fibrosis, including serum biomarkers, the FIB-4 index, and liver stiffness measurement. Transcriptome sequencing of peripheral blood mononuclear cells from these donors identified IL-17F as significantly upregulated in the OBI group. Mechanistically, we found that IL-17F was chiefly secreted by CD4 + T cells upon HBcAg stimulation and functionally promoted the expression of key fibrosis-related cytokines and markers. Collectively, our study unveils a novel pathway wherein HBcAg induces CD4 + T cells to secrete IL-17F, which in turn accelerates liver fibrogenesis, providing a mechanistic basis for OBI-associated liver disease. IMPORTANCE This study reveals, for the first time, a novel mechanism by which OBI drives liver fibrosis, demonstrating that HBcAg stimulates CD4 + T cells to secrete IL-17F, which in turn activates macrophages and hepatic stellate cells, thereby promoting liver inflammation and fibrosis. This discovery not only elucidates a key molecular pathway underlying OBI-related liver disease progression, but also provides an important theoretical foundation for developing IL-17F-targeted diagnostic approaches and anti-fibrotic therapies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Occult hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is associated with progressive liver diseases, but the mechanistic basis for this link remains unclear, prompting the study to investigate the underlying pathways.
Design and population
A comparative study was conducted involving 40 occult HBV infection (OBI) blood donors and 100 healthy blood donors to evaluate clinical parameters of liver fibrosis.
Core methods
Serum biomarkers, the FIB-4 index, and liver stiffness measurement were assessed, and transcriptome sequencing of peripheral blood mononuclear cells identified IL-17F as significantly upregulated in the OBI group.
Main results
IL-17F was primarily secreted by CD4+ T cells upon HBcAg stimulation and functionally promoted the expression of fibrosis-related cytokines and markers.
Interpretation and novelty
The study reveals a novel pathway where HBcAg induces CD4+ T cells to secrete IL-17F, which accelerates liver fibrogenesis, providing a mechanistic basis for OBI-associated liver disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
This discovery elucidates a key molecular pathway underlying OBI-related liver disease progression and supports the development of IL-17F-targeted diagnostic and anti-fibrotic therapeutic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study limitations, sample size justification, or statistical power, which constrains confidence in the findings.

乙型肝炎表面抗原血清清除后肝细胞癌的长期发病率。

Long-term incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma after hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance.
作者： Soleiman El-Debeiky, Tony Hiu-Fung Lam, Mandy Sze-Man Lai, Yee-Kit Tse, Vicki Wing-Ki Hui, Jimmy Che-To Lai, Henry Lik-Yuen Chan, Vincent Wai-Sun Wong, Grace Lai-Hung Wong, Terry Cheuk-Fung Yip
摘要中文翻译背景和目的:乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)血清清除后,肝细胞癌(HCC)的风险降低。我们比较了清除 HBsAg 的慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 患者与普通人群的 HCC 发病率。方法:使用香港全港数据库对 2000 年至 2022 年期间清除 HBsAg 的所有慢性乙型肝炎成人进行识别。估算年度 HCC 发病率并与一般人群进行比较。特定年龄和性别的人口统计数据取自香港癌症登记处和政府统计处。结果:在 13,379 名 HBsAg 血清清除患者中(平均年龄 59.7±13.7 岁,59.8% 男性,14.6% 肝硬化),274 名 (2.0%) 患者在中位 5.6 (IQR 3.1-9.8) 岁时发展为 HCC。HBsAg 血清清除时年龄<50 岁和≥50 岁的肝硬化患者的年 HCC 发病率 (95% CI) 分别为 0.43% (0.22%-0.73%) 和 0.62% (0.50%-0.76%)。在 50 岁之前清除 HBsAg 的无肝硬化患者中,年 HCC 发病率为 0.03%(95% CI 0.01%-0.06%),与普通人群的 0.03% 相当。HBsAg 检测结果显示,年龄 <40 岁和 40-49 岁的无肝硬化男性患者的 HCC 年发病率分别为 0.02%(95% CI 0.002%-0.08%)和 0.09%(0.03%-0.19%)。对 1,317 名年龄 <50 岁且 HBsAg 消失的无肝硬化女性患者进行 15 年随访期间,没有发生 HCC 病例;50-59岁人群中HCC年发病率为0.06%(95% CI 0.02%-0.13%)。结论:在较年轻时清除 HBsAg 的慢性乙型肝炎患者,特别是没有肝硬化或其他危险因素的 40 岁以下男性和 50 岁以下女性,HCC 风险非常低,可能不需要常规监测。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 仍然是肝细胞癌 (HCC) 的主要原因,但乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 血清清除后的长期风险尚不确定。该研究探讨了 HBsAg 血清清除是否会降低 HCC 发病率,并确定了可能不需要监测的患者亚组。
设计与对象
这项在香港进行的全港队列研究利用全港数据库确定了 2000 年至 2022 年间所有实现 HBsAg 血清清除的慢性乙型肝炎成人患者。该队列包括 13,379 名患者(平均年龄 59.7±13.7 岁,59.8% 为男性,14.6% 患有肝硬化),并将 HCC 发病率与特定年龄和性别的人口统计数据进行了比较。
核心方法
每年估计 HCC 发病率,并按年龄、性别、肝硬化状态和 HBsAg 血清清除时的年龄进行分层。从香港癌症登记处和政府统计处检索特定年龄和性别的人口数据进行比较。
主要结果
在 13,379 名患者中,274 名 (2.0%) 患者在中位 5.6 年 (IQR 3.1-9.8) 时发展为 HCC。年龄<50岁和≥50岁的肝硬化患者的HCC年发病率分别为0.43%(95% CI 0.22%–0.73%)和0.62%(95% CI 0.50%–0.76%)。在无肝硬化的患者中,50 岁以下的患者的年发病率为 0.03%(95% CI 0.01%–0.06%),与一般人群相当。
解释与创新
该研究表明,年轻时的 HBsAg 血清清除率,特别是在 40 岁以下的男性和 50 岁以下且无肝硬化的女性中,与极低的 HCC 风险相关。这对这些亚组中进行常规 HCC 监测的需求提出了挑战,凸显了临床决策的新分层。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,对于年轻时清除 HBsAg 而没有肝硬化的患者,可以安全地推迟 HCC 监测,从而可能减轻医疗负担并提高生活质量。结果支持血清清除后 CHB 管理中的风险分层监测策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计、对全港数据库的依赖以及血清清除前抗病毒治疗数据的缺乏。由于潜在的不可测量的混杂因素和仅限于香港人口的普遍性,证据强度中等。

Long-term incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma after hepatitis B surface antigen seroclearance.

Authors: Soleiman El-Debeiky, Tony Hiu-Fung Lam, Mandy Sze-Man Lai, Yee-Kit Tse, Vicki Wing-Ki Hui, Jimmy Che-To Lai, Henry Lik-Yuen Chan, Vincent Wai-Sun Wong, Grace Lai-Hung Wong, Terry Cheuk-Fung Yip
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND AND AIMS: Risk of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) decreases following hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance. We examined HCC incidence in patients with chronic hepatitis B (CHB) who cleared HBsAg compared to general population. METHODS: All adults with CHB who cleared HBsAg between 2000-2022 were identified using a territory-wide database in Hong Kong. Annual HCC incidences were estimated and compared to general population. Age- and sex-specific population statistics were retrieved from the Hong Kong Cancer Registry and Census and Statistics Department. RESULTS: Of 13,379 patients with HBsAg seroclearance (mean age 59.7±13.7 years, 59.8% males, 14.6% cirrhosis), 274 (2.0%) developed HCC at a median of 5.6 (IQR 3.1-9.8) years. Annual HCC incidence (95% CI) was 0.43% (0.22%-0.73%) and 0.62% (0.50%-0.76%) among patients with cirrhosis aged <50 years and ≥50 years at HBsAg seroclearance, respectively. In patients without cirrhosis who cleared HBsAg before age 50, the annual HCC incidence was 0.03% (95% CI 0.01%-0.06%), comparable to 0.03% in general population. Annual HCC incidence of male patients without cirrhosis aged <40 and 40-49 years at HBsAg was 0.02% (95% CI 0.002%-0.08%) and 0.09% (0.03%-0.19%), respectively. No HCC cases occurred during 15 years of follow-up among 1,317 female patients without cirrhosis aged <50 years at HBsAg loss; the annual HCC incidence was 0.06% (95% CI 0.02%-0.13%) among those aged 50-59 years. CONCLUSIONS: Patients with CHB who clear HBsAg at younger ages, particularly men <40 years and women <50 years without cirrhosis or additional risk factors, have very low HCC risk and may not require routine surveillance.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) remains a leading cause of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC), but the long-term risk after hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) seroclearance is uncertain. The study addressed whether HBsAg seroclearance reduces HCC incidence and identified patient subgroups where surveillance may be unnecessary.
Design and population
This territory-wide cohort study in Hong Kong identified all adults with CHB who achieved HBsAg seroclearance between 2000 and 2022 using a territory-wide database. The cohort included 13,379 patients (mean age 59.7±13.7 years, 59.8% male, 14.6% with cirrhosis) and compared HCC incidence to age- and sex-specific population statistics.
Core methods
HCC incidence was estimated annually and stratified by age, sex, cirrhosis status, and age at HBsAg seroclearance. Age- and sex-specific population data were retrieved from the Hong Kong Cancer Registry and Census and Statistics Department for comparison.
Main results
Among 13,379 patients, 274 (2.0%) developed HCC at a median of 5.6 years (IQR 3.1–9.8). Annual HCC incidence was 0.43% (95% CI 0.22%–0.73%) and 0.62% (95% CI 0.50%–0.76%) in patients with cirrhosis aged <50 and ≥50 years, respectively. In patients without cirrhosis, annual incidence was 0.03% (95% CI 0.01%–0.06%) for those <50 years, comparable to the general population.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that HBsAg seroclearance at younger ages, particularly in men <40 years and women <50 years without cirrhosis, is associated with very low HCC risk. This challenges the need for routine HCC surveillance in these subgroups, highlighting a novel stratification for clinical decision-making.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that HCC surveillance may be safely deferred in patients who clear HBsAg at a young age without cirrhosis, potentially reducing healthcare burden and improving quality of life. The results support risk-stratified surveillance strategies in post-seroclearance CHB management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design, reliance on a territory-wide database, and lack of data on antiviral therapy prior to seroclearance. The evidence strength is moderate due to potential unmeasured confounding and generalizability limited to the Hong Kong population.

在没有急性乙型肝炎的情况下孤立的 HBc IgM 假反应:病例报告

Isolated HBc IgM false reactivity in the absence of acute Hepatitis B: a case report
作者： Giuseppe Di Pietra, Michele Borghi, Maira Zoppelletto, Mariela Marinova
摘要中文翻译摘要 血清学标志物及其可能的组合仍然用于诊断、区分急性和慢性感染以及评估与分子乙型肝炎病毒血症水平相关的治疗效果。孤立的 HBc IgM 血清阳性是一种独特的血清学模式,值得进一步研究。我们介绍了一名 28 岁男性病例,最近被诊断为多发性硬化症,并对其进行了深入分析。该患者的实验室检测仅显示 HBc IgM 呈阳性。因此,进行了完整的乙型肝炎血清学检测,证实了第一个意想不到的结果。乙型肝炎病毒血症未检测到。还使用另一种商业化验方法对样本进行了分析,结果呈阴性 HBc IgM。怀疑存在异嗜性抗体;因此,使用了异嗜性阻塞管。血清处理后的结果证实,最初的 HBc IgM 阳性是假阳性结果,归因于嗜异性抗体。本病例报告强调了乙型肝炎标志物的临床和实验室解释对于准确诊断的重要性。

Isolated HBc IgM false reactivity in the absence of acute Hepatitis B: a case report

Authors: Giuseppe Di Pietra, Michele Borghi, Maira Zoppelletto, Mariela Marinova
Original Abstract
Abstract Serological markers and their possible combinations are still used for diagnosis, to distinguish between acute and chronic infection, and to assess therapy efficacy, in association with molecular hepatitis B viremia levels. Isolated HBc IgM seropositivity is a distinctive serological pattern that warrants further investigation. We present a 28-year-old male case with a recent diagnosis of multiple sclerosis, for which in-depth analyses have been carried out. The patient’s laboratory testing showed positivity only for HBc IgM. Therefore, a complete hepatitis B serological panel was performed, confirming the first unexpected result. Hepatitis B viremia was undetectable. The sample was also analyzed using another commercial assay, which yielded a negative HBc IgM result. The presence of heterophile antibodies was suspected; therefore, a heterophilic blocking tube was used. The results after the serum’s treatment confirmed that the initial HBc IgM positivity was a false-positive result, attributed to heterophile antibodies. This case report highlights the importance of clinical and laboratory interpretation of hepatitis B markers for accurate diagnosis.

Abstract Serological markers and their possible combinations are still used for diagnosis, to distinguish between acute and chronic infection, and to assess therapy efficacy, in association with molecular hepatitis B viremia levels.
Design and population
This case report highlights the importance of clinical and laboratory interpretation of hepatitis B markers for accurate diagnosis.
Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength
The presence of heterophile antibodies was suspected; therefore, a heterophilic blocking tube was used.

乌兹别克斯坦慢性丁型肝炎病毒感染患者的晚期慢性肝病发病率很高。

High Rates of Advanced Chronic Liver Disease in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection in Uzbekistan.
作者： Malika E. Khodjaeva, Aziza S. Khikmatullaeva, Nargiz S. Ibadullaeva, Gulnara K. Khudaykulova, Munisa M. Ilyasova, Kamila U. Eshbayeva, Dildorakhon N. Abdurakhmanova, Helenie Kefalakes, Petra Huber, Michael J. Gebel
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景和目的 慢性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染在乌兹别克斯坦流行。先前的数据显示,HBsAg 阳性个体中抗丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 抗体的患病率很高。关于乌兹别克斯坦慢性 HDV 感染患者的疾病严重程度、治疗可用性和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患病率的数据有限。方法 病毒学科学研究所 (SRIOV) 是乌兹别克斯坦领先的病毒性肝炎医学和科学参考中心。在这项回顾性研究中,收集并分析了 2023 年 6 月至 2024 年 5 月期间所有就诊于 SRIoV 的 HBsAg 阳性患者的流行病学、临床和实验室数据。比较了 HDV 合并感染和 HBV 单一感染患者的数据。结果 纳入 1393 名患者的数据。HDV 合并感染比 HBV 单一感染更为普遍 (73.9% [ n = 1030/1393]) vs. 26.1% [ n = 363/1393]。HDV 感染患者明显更年轻(41.9 vs. 45.4,p p n = 4/1030)。连续地,78.9% (812/1023) 的患者可检测到 HDV RNA。HDV RNA 定量与 ALT 升高>正常上限独立相关。由于筛查措施不足,两个患者组的 HCC 检出率均较低(HDV:n = 7/1030,HBV:n = 11/363)。结论 在这个由 1393 名患者组成的回顾性队列中,HDV 合并感染的患病率和疾病严重程度超过了 HBV 单一感染。大多数丁型肝炎病毒感染患者均患有晚期肝病,目前缺乏针对丁型肝炎病毒的治疗。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在乙型肝炎病毒流行的乌兹别克斯坦,慢性丁型肝炎病毒 (HDV) 感染是一个被忽视的公共卫生问题,但 HDV 合并感染患者的疾病严重程度、治疗可及性和肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患病率仍然缺乏记录。
设计与对象
乌兹别克斯坦国家病毒性肝炎参考中心病毒学科学研究所 (SRIOV) 进行了一项回顾性队列研究,纳入了 2023 年 6 月至 2024 年 5 月期间出现的所有 HBsAg 阳性患者。
核心方法
收集并分析流行病学、临床和实验室数据;对 HDV 合并感染和 HBV 单一感染的患者进行比较,并对 HDV RNA 进行定量,并与评估的 ALT 升高相关。
主要结果
在 1393 名患者中,HDV 合并感染比 HBV 单一感染更为普遍(73.9% [1030/1393] vs 26.1% [363/1393]);HDV 感染患者较年轻(41.9 岁 vs 45.4 岁),78.9% (812/1023) 的 HDV RNA 可检测到,HDV RNA 与 ALT 升高 > ULN 独立相关。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,在乌兹别克斯坦,丁型肝炎病毒合并感染的患病率和疾病严重程度超过了乙型肝炎病毒单一感染,这突显了目前出现的晚期肝病和缺乏针对丁型肝炎病毒的治疗方法是新发现。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调乌兹别克斯坦迫切需要改进丁型肝炎病毒筛查、肝癌监测和获得丁型肝炎病毒特异性抗病毒治疗。
局限与证据强度
回顾性单中心设计、由于筛查不足和缺乏 HDV 导向治疗数据导致 HCC 检出率低,限制了因果推断和普遍性,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

High Rates of Advanced Chronic Liver Disease in Patients With Chronic Hepatitis D Virus Infection in Uzbekistan.

Authors: Malika E. Khodjaeva, Aziza S. Khikmatullaeva, Nargiz S. Ibadullaeva, Gulnara K. Khudaykulova, Munisa M. Ilyasova, Kamila U. Eshbayeva, Dildorakhon N. Abdurakhmanova, Helenie Kefalakes, Petra Huber, Michael J. Gebel
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT Background and Aims Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection is endemic in Uzbekistan. Previous data showed a high prevalence of anti‐hepatitis D virus (HDV) antibodies in HBsAg‐positive individuals. Limited data are available on disease severity, treatment availability and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) prevalence in patients with chronic HDV infection in Uzbekistan. Methods The Scientific Research Institute of Virology (SRIoV) is the leading medical and scientific reference centre for viral hepatitis in Uzbekistan. In this retrospective study, epidemiological, clinical and laboratory data were collected and analysed from all HBsAg‐positive patients presenting to the SRIoV between June 2023 and May 2024. Data from HDV‐coinfected and HBV‐monoinfected patients were compared. Results Data of 1393 individual patients were included. HDV coinfection was more prevalent than HBV‐monoinfection (73.9% [ n = 1030/1393]) vs. 26.1% [ n = 363/1393]. HDV‐infected patients were significantly younger (41.9 vs. 45.4, p p n = 4/1030). Consecutively, HDV RNA was detectable in 78.9% (812/1023) of patients. Quantitative HDV RNA was independently associated with ALT elevation > upper limit of normal. The HCC detection rate was low in both patient groups due to insufficient screening measures (HDV: n = 7/1030, HBV: n = 11/363). Conclusions In this retrospective cohort of 1393 individual patients, HDV coinfection exceeds HBV monoinfection in prevalence and disease severity. Most HDV‐infected patients present with advanced liver disease and currently lack access to HDV‐directed therapy.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis D virus (HDV) infection is a neglected public-health problem in Uzbekistan where HBV is endemic, yet disease severity, treatment access and hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) prevalence in HDV-coinfected patients remain poorly documented.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted at the Scientific Research Institute of Virology (SRIoV), the national reference centre for viral hepatitis in Uzbekistan, enrolling all HBsAg-positive patients presenting between June 2023 and May 2024.
Core methods
Epidemiological, clinical and laboratory data were collected and analysed; HDV-coinfected and HBV-monoinfected patients were compared, and HDV RNA was quantified with association to ALT elevation assessed.
Main results
Among 1393 patients, HDV coinfection was more prevalent than HBV monoinfection (73.9% [1030/1393] vs 26.1% [363/1393]); HDV-infected patients were younger (41.9 vs 45.4 years), HDV RNA was detectable in 78.9% (812/1023), and HDV RNA was independently associated with ALT elevation > ULN.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that HDV coinfection exceeds HBV monoinfection in prevalence and disease severity in Uzbekistan, highlighting advanced liver disease at presentation and lack of HDV-directed therapy access as novel findings.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings underscore an urgent need for improved HDV screening, HCC surveillance and access to HDV-specific antiviral therapy in Uzbekistan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The retrospective single-centre design, low HCC detection due to insufficient screening and absence of HDV-directed therapy data limit causal inference and generalisability, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

NGS 诊断的乙型肝炎脑炎伴孤立性左顶叶受累:病例报告。

NGS-diagnosed HBV encephalitis with isolated left parietal lobe involvement: a case report.
作者： Zhangning Zhou, Longqi Zhang
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景病毒性脑炎是指病毒感染引起的脑实质炎症,通常表现为发热、神志不清、癫痫发作和脑膜刺激症状。临床表现的非特异性,加上病史不完整,常常导致漏诊和不适当的治疗。病例介绍 我们介绍一例乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 脑炎病例,表现为孤立的左顶叶受累。患者为慢性乙型肝炎病毒携带者,未接受抗病毒治疗。发病时主要症状为意识不清、发烧和癫痫发作。通过脑脊液(CSF)的下一代测序(NGS)检测,他被诊断患有乙型肝炎脑炎。经过恩替卡韦抗病毒治疗和一般对症治疗后,患者的临床症状消失,后续脑部MRI显示病变几乎完全消退。结论 HBV 引起的中枢神经系统 (CNS) 感染在临床实践中极为罕见。该病例凸显乙型肝炎脑炎的临床表现非特异性且容易被漏诊,而神经影像学往往显示孤立性病变。早期应用NGS进行准确的病原诊断对于减少误诊至关重要。

问题与背景
临床表现的非特异性,加上病史不完整,常常导致漏诊和不适当的治疗。
设计与对象
病例介绍 我们介绍一例乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 脑炎病例,表现为孤立的左顶叶受累。
核心方法
通过脑脊液(CSF)的下一代测序(NGS)检测,他被诊断患有乙型肝炎脑炎。
主要结果

解释与创新
结论 HBV 引起的中枢神经系统 (CNS) 感染在临床实践中极为罕见。
科研与公卫意义
早期应用NGS进行准确的病原诊断对于减少误诊至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该病例凸显乙型肝炎脑炎的临床表现非特异性且容易被漏诊,而神经影像学往往显示孤立性病变。

NGS-diagnosed HBV encephalitis with isolated left parietal lobe involvement: a case report.

Authors: Zhangning Zhou, Longqi Zhang
Original Abstract
Abstract Background Viral encephalitis refers to the inflammation of the brain parenchyma caused by viral infection, usually manifested as fever, unconsciousness, seizures, and meningeal irritation symptoms. The non-specific clinical manifestations, combined with incomplete medical history, often lead to missed diagnoses and inappropriate treatments. Case presentation We present a case of Hepatitis B virus (HBV) encephalitis, manifesting as isolated left parietal lobe involvement. The patient is a chronic hepatitis B virus carrier who did not receive antiviral therapy. Upon onset, the main symptoms were unconsciousness, fever, and seizures. He was diagnosed with HBV encephalitis thru next-generation sequencing (NGS) testing of the cerebrospinal fluid (CSF). After antiviral treatment with entecavir and general symptomatic treatment, the patient’s clinical symptoms disappeared, and a follow-up brain MRI showed that the lesion had almost completely resolved. Conclusions Central nervous system (CNS) infections caused by HBV are incredibly uncommon in clinical practice. This case highlights that the clinical manifestations of HBV encephalitis are nonspecific and prone to being missed, while neuroimaging often reveals an isolated lesion. Early application of NGS for accurate pathogen diagnosis is crucial to reduce misdiagnosis.

The non-specific clinical manifestations, combined with incomplete medical history, often lead to missed diagnoses and inappropriate treatments.
Design and population

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

衣康酸通过重新编程线粒体功能障碍的 NK 细胞代谢来缓解急性肝炎

Itaconic acid alleviates acute hepatitis by reprogramming NK cell metabolism with mitochondrial dysfunction
作者： Ying Sun, Mingyue Duan, Xi Zhang, Xiaoguai Liu, Jun She, Yi Lv, Hong Lei, Sun Y, Duan M, Zhang X
摘要中文翻译<h4>背景</h4>免疫代谢为炎症性疾病的发病机制和治疗提供了重要的见解。衣康酸 (ITA) 是一种源自三羧酸循环的关键免疫调节代谢物,已在巨噬细胞中得到广泛表征;然而,其对 NK 细胞和 NK 细胞介导的肝脏病理学的直接影响仍不清楚。<h4>方法</h4>使用 SCENITH 和 Seahorse 分析,我们通过多组学评估了 ITA 对 NK 细胞代谢的影响,并研究了其潜在机制。然后在多聚 (I:C) 诱导的肝损伤小鼠模型中评估其治疗潜力。<h4>结果</h4>我们表明,ITA 重新编程 NK 细胞代谢,抑制其增殖和杀伤能力,同时抑制氧化磷酸化,同时诱导补偿性糖酵解转变。多组学整合表明,这种代谢变化与糖酵解基因网络的上调和显着的线粒体损伤相对应,在机制上与琥珀酸脱氢酶(SDH)活性的抑制和 NRF1/TFAM 线粒体生物发生途径的下调有关。ITA 还会损害线粒体并损害自噬活性,导致 NK 细胞中功能失调的线粒体积聚。所有这些改变都会导致 NK 细胞增殖和细胞毒功能受到严重抑制。对患有聚(I:C)诱导的肝损伤的小鼠施用ITA可显着减轻肝损伤并抑制肝脏的过度炎症。这种保护作用与抑制 NK 和 T 细胞的肝脏浸润和功能有关。<h4>结论</h4>我们的研究结果扩展了对 ITA 对 NK 细胞的免疫调节和代谢重编程的理解,表明 ITA 抑制 SDH 活性,通过 NK 细胞中的 NRF1/TFAM 下调线粒体生物合成,从而限制细胞毒功能,这表明 ITA 在 NK 细胞介导的肝脏病理中的应用具有进一步的治疗潜力。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
衣康酸 (ITA) 是一种源自三羧酸循环的关键免疫调节代谢物,已在巨噬细胞中得到广泛表征;然而,其对 NK 细胞和 NK 细胞介导的肝脏病理学的直接影响仍不清楚。<h4>方法</h4>使用 SCENITH 和 Seahorse 分析,我们通过多组学评估了 ITA 对 NK 细胞代谢的影响,并研究了其潜在机制。
设计与对象
提供的证据没有明确说明研究设计、设置或研究人群。
核心方法
然后在多聚 (I:C) 诱导的肝损伤小鼠模型中评估其治疗潜力。<h4>结果</h4>我们表明,ITA 重新编程 NK 细胞代谢,抑制其增殖和杀伤能力,同时抑制氧化磷酸化,同时诱导补偿性糖酵解转变。
主要结果
多组学整合表明,这种代谢变化与糖酵解基因网络的上调和显着的线粒体损伤相对应,在机制上与琥珀酸脱氢酶(SDH)活性的抑制和 NRF1/TFAM 线粒体生物发生途径的下调有关。
解释与创新
这种保护作用与抑制 NK 和 T 细胞的肝脏浸润和功能有关。<h4>结论</h4>我们的研究结果扩展了对 ITA 对 NK 细胞的免疫调节和代谢重编程的理解,表明 ITA 抑制 SDH 活性,通过 NK 细胞中的 NRF1/TFAM 下调线粒体生物合成,从而限制细胞毒功能,这表明 ITA 在 NK 细胞介导的肝脏病理中的应用具有进一步的治疗潜力。
科研与公卫意义
<h4>背景</h4>免疫代谢为炎症性疾病的发病机制和治疗提供了重要的见解。
局限与证据强度
对患有聚(I:C)诱导的肝损伤的小鼠施用ITA可显着减轻肝损伤并抑制肝脏的过度炎症。

Itaconic acid alleviates acute hepatitis by reprogramming NK cell metabolism with mitochondrial dysfunction

Authors: Ying Sun, Mingyue Duan, Xi Zhang, Xiaoguai Liu, Jun She, Yi Lv, Hong Lei, Sun Y, Duan M, Zhang X
Original Abstract
<h4>Background</h4>Immunometabolism provides critical insights into the pathogenesis and treatment of inflammatory diseases. Itaconic acid (ITA), a key immunomodulatory metabolite derived from the tricarboxylic acid cycle, has been extensively characterized in macrophages; however, its direct effects on NK cells and NK cell-mediated liver pathology remain poorly defined.<h4>Methods</h4>Using SCENITH and Seahorse analyses, we assessed ITA's impact on NK cell metabolism via multi-omics, and investigated its underlying mechanisms. The therapeutic potential was then evaluated in a mouse model of poly (I:C)-induced liver injury.<h4>Results</h4>We show that ITA reprograms NK cell metabolism to suppress their proliferation and killing capacity, simultaneously suppressing oxidative phosphorylation while inducing a compensatory glycolytic shift. Multi-omics integration revealed that this metabolic shift corresponds with the upregulation of glycolytic gene networks and significant mitochondrial impairment, mechanistically linked to the inhibition of succinate dehydrogenase (SDH) activity and the downregulation of the NRF1/TFAM mitochondrial biogenesis pathway. ITA also damages mitochondria and impairs autophagic activity, resulting in accumulation of dysfunctional mitochondria in NK cells. All these alterations lead to profound suppression of NK cell proliferation and cytotoxic function. Administration of ITA in mice with poly (I:C) -induced liver injury substantially attenuated hepatic damage and suppressed over-inflammation in the liver. This protective effect was associated with inhibited hepatic infiltration and function of both NK and T cells.<h4>Conclusion</h4>Our findings extend the understanding of the immune regulation and metabolic reprogramming of ITA to NK cells, showing that ITA inhibits SDH activity to down-regulate mitochondrial biogenesis through NRF1/TFAM in NK cells with consequent limitation of cytotoxic function, suggesting the further therapeutic potential of ITA application in NK cells-mediated liver pathologies.

石蒜碱与甘草酸超分子自组装成纳米药物,用于安全、持续的抗乙肝治疗。

Supramolecular Self-Assembly of Lycorine with Glycyrrhizic Acid into a Nanodrug for Safe and Sustained Anti-HBV Therapy.
作者： Hang Sun, Zi-Dong He, Bei-Bei Jia, Bo Yuan, Ling-Chuan Kong, Rui-Xian Xu, Qiong Wang, Wen-Jing Pan, Hao Chen, Shao-Xing Dai
摘要中文翻译实现慢性乙型肝炎的功能性治愈仍然是一个重大挑战,因为传统疗法难以消除乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)并随后恢复适应性抗病毒免疫。Lycorine (LY) 是一种具有广谱抗病毒活性的化合物,在抑制 HBsAg 分泌方面表现出特别的前景。然而,其临床应用因显着的细胞毒性、诱导细胞凋亡、半衰期短和体内不良反应而受到严重限制。为了克服这些障碍并增强其治疗潜力,我们通过将 LY 与天然抗 HBV 药物甘草酸共组装来设计了一种自组装纳米药物 (LGN)。这种策略不仅可以保护 LY 免于过早降解并使其能够在结肠中持续释放,而且还可以显着减弱 LY 引起的全身毒性。值得注意的是,LGN 有效抑制 HBsAg 分泌 (IC50 = 2.15 ± 0.09 μM),其选择性指数 (SI > 93) 比单独 LY (SI = 39.26) 高出两倍多。此外,LGN 同时抑制 HBV DNA 复制、pgRNA 转录以及病毒蛋白 HBc 和 HBx 的表达,表明与核苷(酸)类似物不同的多靶点、宿主依赖性机制。在 HDI 模型中使用这种纳米药物进行治疗进一步证明了其针对 HBV 的治疗潜力且具有明显的安全性。进一步的转录组学研究证实,LGN 诱导独特的宿主转录程序,破坏 HBV 生命周期的多个关键阶段,包括病毒进入、共价闭合环状 DNA 维持、基因转录和免疫逃避。总的来说,这些发现为利用 LY 等天然产物进行抗病毒治疗提供了宝贵的见解,并强调了自组装技术在改善药物安全性和药代动力学特征方面的潜力。

Supramolecular Self-Assembly of Lycorine with Glycyrrhizic Acid into a Nanodrug for Safe and Sustained Anti-HBV Therapy.

Authors: Hang Sun, Zi-Dong He, Bei-Bei Jia, Bo Yuan, Ling-Chuan Kong, Rui-Xian Xu, Qiong Wang, Wen-Jing Pan, Hao Chen, Shao-Xing Dai
Original Abstract
Achieving a functional cure for chronic hepatitis B remains a major challenge, as conventional therapies struggle to eliminate the hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) and subsequently restore adaptive antiviral immunity. Lycorine (LY), a compound with broad-spectrum antiviral activity, has shown particular promise inhibiting HBsAg secretion. However, its clinical application is severely limited by significant cytotoxicity, the induction of apoptosis, a short half-life, and adverse in vivo effects. To overcome these barriers and enhance its therapeutic potential, we designed a self-assembled nanomedicine (LGN) by co-assembling LY with glycyrrhizic acid, a natural anti-HBV agent. This strategy not only protected LY from premature degradation and enabled its sustained release in the colon but also markedly attenuated LY-induced systemic toxicity. Notably, LGN effectively suppressed HBsAg secretion (IC50 = 2.15 ± 0.09 μM) with a selectivity index (SI > 93) over two-fold higher than that of LY alone (SI = 39.26). Furthermore, LGN concurrently inhibited HBV DNA replication, pgRNA transcription, and the expression of viral protein HBc and HBx, indicating a multitargeted, host-dependent mechanism distinct from nucleos(t)ide analogs. Treatment with this nanomedicine in the HDI model further demonstrated its therapeutic potential against HBV with apparent safety. Further transcriptomic study confirmed that LGN induces a distinct host transcriptional program that disrupts multiple critical stages of the HBV life cycle including viral entry, covalently closed circular DNA maintenance, gene transcription, and immune evasion. Collectively, these findings provide valuable insights into exploiting natural products like LY for antiviral therapy and underscore the potential of self-assembly technology in improving drug safety and pharmacokinetic profiles.

HIV-1 和乙型肝炎患者的 B/F/TAF:ALLIANCE 开放标签扩展阶段的结果。

B/F/TAF in people with both HIV-1 and hepatitis B: outcomes from the ALLIANCE open-label extension phase.
作者： Anchalee Avihingsanon, Chee Loon Leong, Chien-Ching Hung, Sasisopin Kiertiburanakul, Man-Po Lee, Michelle L. D’Antoni, Sharline Madera, Hongyuan Wang, Jason T. Hindman, Taisheng Li
摘要中文翻译背景:关于比克替拉韦/恩曲他滨/替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (B/F/TAF) 在 HIV-1 和 HBV 感染者中的数据有限,特别是对于有治疗经验的人。方法:ALLIANCE 是一项随机、双盲、阳性对照 3 期研究,针对 HIV-1 RNA ≥ 500 拷贝/mL 且 HBV DNA ≥ 2000 IU/mL 的初治成人,进行 B/F/TAF 与多替拉韦 (DTG) + 恩曲他滨/富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (F/TDF) 的比较。参与者接受连续 B/F/TAF 至 144 周(≥ 96 周的盲法治疗加上 48 周可选的开放标签延长 [OLE]),或在 ≥ 96 周的 DTG + F/TDF 至 48 周的 OLE 后转为 B/F/TAF。我们在这里报告 OLE 的结果。结果:121名参与者被纳入连续B/F/TAF组;开关组89。B/F/TAF 暴露中位时间分别为 186.4 周和 48 周。两组均维持了 HIV-1 和 HBV 抑制率(缺失 = 排除)(B/F/TAF 第 144 周:99.0% 和 80.2%;转换 OLE 第 48 周:分别为 95.4% 和 86.6%)。两组的丙氨酸转氨酶正常化程度都有所改善。接受连续 B/F/TAF(第 144 周)的参与者中,HBV 包膜抗原丢失和血清转化的发生率分别为 44.8% 和 29.3%,而在转换组(OLE 第 48 周)中,分别为 17.0% 和 12.8%。HBV 表面抗原丧失和血清转化发生率分别为 22.5% 和 15.5%(连续 B/F/TAF)以及 4.3% 和 0%(转换组)。一名参与者(连续 B/F/TAF)由于与研究药物无关的治疗出现的不良事件而中断。没有报告出现治疗耐药。结论:B/F/TAF 可有效抑制 HIV-1 和 HBV,并且对于未接受治疗的个体在 ≤ 3 年和从基于 TDF 的方案转换后 ≤ 1 年内具有良好的耐受性。试验注册 ClinicalTrials.gov NCT03547908(首次注册 2018-05-24)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
关于比克替拉韦/恩曲他滨/替诺福韦艾拉酚胺 (B/F/TAF) 在 HIV-1 和乙型肝炎患者中的数据有限,特别是对于有治疗经验的患者。
设计与对象
ALLIANCE 是一项随机、双盲、阳性对照 3 期研究,针对 HIV-1 RNA ≥ 500 拷贝/mL 且 HBV DNA ≥ 2000 IU/mL 的初治成人,进行 B/F/TAF 与多替拉韦 (DTG) + 恩曲他滨/富马酸替诺福韦二吡呋酯 (F/TDF) 的比较。参与者在 144 周内接受连续 B/F/TAF,或者在 ≥ 96 周的 DTG + F/TDF 后通过 48 周的开放标签延伸转为 B/F/TAF。
核心方法
报告了开放标签延伸 (OLE) 的结果,连续暴露 B/F/TAF 186.4 周,转换组暴露 48 周。两组的 HIV-1 和 HBV 抑制率(缺失 = 排除)均得以维持,并且两组均观察到丙氨酸转氨酶正常化。

121名参与者被纳入连续B/F/TAF组;开关组89。HIV-1 和 HBV 抑制率保持不变(B/F/TAF 第 144 周:99.0% 和 80.2%;转换 OLE 第 48 周:95.4% 和 86.6%)。HBV 包膜抗原丢失和血清转化发生率分别为 44.8% 和 29.3%(连续 B/F/TAF)以及 17.0% 和 12.8%(转换组)。HBV 表面抗原丢失和血清转化发生率分别为 22.5% 和 15.5%(连续 B/F/TAF)以及 4.3% 和 0%(转换组)。没有报告出现治疗耐药。
解释与创新
B/F/TAF 可有效抑制 HIV-1 和 HBV,并且在未接受治疗的个体中耐受性良好,耐受性良好,持续时间为 ≤ 3 年,从基于 TDF 的方案转换后≤ 1 年。该研究提供了有关该人群中 HBV 包膜和表面抗原丢失以及血清转化的新数据。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持使用 B/F/TAF 来维持 HIV-1 和 HBV 抑制并改善初治和既往治疗个体的肝酶正常化。乙型肝炎病毒抗原丢失和血清转化的数据可以为合并感染患者的长期管理策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于开放标签扩展,样本量相对较小(210 名受试者),并且转换组的随访期较短(48 周)。由于缺乏长期数据且 OLE 阶段缺乏对照组,证据强度中等。

B/F/TAF in people with both HIV-1 and hepatitis B: outcomes from the ALLIANCE open-label extension phase.

Authors: Anchalee Avihingsanon, Chee Loon Leong, Chien-Ching Hung, Sasisopin Kiertiburanakul, Man-Po Lee, Michelle L. D’Antoni, Sharline Madera, Hongyuan Wang, Jason T. Hindman, Taisheng Li
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Data on bictegravir/emtricitabine/tenofovir alafenamide (B/F/TAF) in people with both HIV-1 and HBV are limited, particularly for treatment-experienced people. METHODS: ALLIANCE was a randomized, double-blind, active-controlled, phase 3 study of B/F/TAF versus dolutegravir (DTG) + emtricitabine/tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (F/TDF) in treatment-naïve adults with HIV-1 RNA ≥ 500 copies/mL and HBV DNA ≥ 2000 IU/mL. Participants received continuous B/F/TAF through 144 weeks (≥ 96 weeks of blinded treatment plus 48-week optional open-label extension [OLE]) or switched to B/F/TAF after ≥ 96 weeks of DTG + F/TDF through 48 weeks of OLE. Here we report outcomes from the OLE. RESULTS: 121 participants were included in the continuous B/F/TAF group; 89 in the switch group. Median B/F/TAF exposure was 186.4 and 48 weeks, respectively. HIV-1 and HBV suppression rates (missing = excluded) were maintained in both groups (B/F/TAF Week 144: 99.0% and 80.2%; switch OLE Week 48: 95.4% and 86.6%, respectively). Both groups had improved alanine aminotransferase normalization. HBV envelope antigen loss and seroconversion occurred in 44.8% and 29.3%, respectively, of participants receiving continuous B/F/TAF (Week 144) and 17.0% and 12.8% in the switch group (OLE Week 48). HBV surface antigen loss and seroconversion occurred in 22.5% and 15.5% (continuous B/F/TAF), and 4.3% and 0% (switch group) of participants, respectively. One participant (continuous B/F/TAF) discontinued due to study drug-unrelated treatment-emergent adverse event. No treatment-emergent resistance was reported. CONCLUSION: B/F/TAF was effective in HIV-1 and HBV suppression and well tolerated in treatment-naïve individuals for ≤ 3 years and ≤ 1 year after switching from a TDF-based regimen. Trial registration ClinicalTrials.gov NCT03547908 (first registration 2018-05-24).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Data on bictegravir/emtricitabine/tenofovir alafenamide (B/F/TAF) in people with both HIV-1 and hepatitis B are limited, particularly for treatment-experienced people.
Design and population
ALLIANCE was a randomized, double-blind, active-controlled, phase 3 study of B/F/TAF versus dolutegravir (DTG) + emtricitabine/tenofovir disoproxil fumarate (F/TDF) in treatment-naïve adults with HIV-1 RNA ≥ 500 copies/mL and HBV DNA ≥ 2000 IU/mL. Participants received continuous B/F/TAF through 144 weeks or switched to B/F/TAF after ≥ 96 weeks of DTG + F/TDF through 48 weeks of open-label extension.
Core methods
Outcomes from the open-label extension (OLE) were reported, with continuous B/F/TAF exposure for 186.4 weeks and 48 weeks for the switch group. HIV-1 and HBV suppression rates (missing = excluded) were maintained in both groups, and alanine aminotransferase normalization was observed in both groups.
Main results
121 participants were included in the continuous B/F/TAF group; 89 in the switch group. HIV-1 and HBV suppression rates were maintained (B/F/TAF Week 144: 99.0% and 80.2%; switch OLE Week 48: 95.4% and 86.6%). HBV envelope antigen loss and seroconversion occurred in 44.8% and 29.3% (continuous B/F/TAF) and 17.0% and 12.8% (switch group). HBV surface antigen loss and seroconversion occurred in 22.5% and 15.5% (continuous B/F/TAF) and 4.3% and 0% (switch group). No treatment-emergent resistance was reported.
Interpretation and novelty
B/F/TAF was effective in HIV-1 and HBV suppression and well tolerated in treatment-naïve individuals for ≤ 3 years and ≤ 1 year after switching from a TDF-based regimen. The study provides new data on HBV envelope and surface antigen loss and seroconversion in this population.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support the use of B/F/TAF for maintaining HIV-1 and HBV suppression and improving liver enzyme normalization in both treatment-naïve and previously treated individuals. The data on HBV antigen loss and seroconversion may inform long-term management strategies for co-infected patients.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an open-label extension with a relatively small sample size (210 participants) and a short follow-up period for the switch group (48 weeks). The evidence strength is moderate due to the lack of long-term data and the absence of a control group in the OLE phase.

AURKA 通过调节 TLR3 的 K48 泛素化和蛋白酶体降解来调节 HBV 诱导的肝星状细胞炎症和肝纤维化

AURKA regulates HBV-induced hepatic stellate cell inflammation and liver fibrosis by modulating K48-linked ubiquitination and proteasomal degradation of TLR3
作者： Hongjie Wu, Tianbao Wang, Fang Yang, Mingqiang Yue, Aihui Li, Changyun Si, 王天宝, Si Cw
摘要中文翻译由持续乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染引起的慢性乙型肝炎(CHB)继续对全球健康构成重大威胁。极光激酶 A (AURKA) 与纤维化有关,但其在 HBV 诱导的肝纤维化中的作用仍不清楚。本研究探讨了 AURKA 在 CHB 期间肝星状细胞 (HSC) 激活和纤维形成中的功能和机制。对 GSE83148 数据集进行生物信息学筛选,并在人 CHB 肝脏标本、用来自 HBV 复制细胞的条件培养基 (CM) 处理的 LX-2 HSC 以及通过尾静脉注射 Ad/rcccDNA 建立的持久性 HBV 重组 cccDNA (rcccDNA) 小鼠模型中进行验证。分别通过定量PCR和免疫印迹分析基因和蛋白质表达。通过ELISA测量细胞因子水平。通过 ROS 和 JC-1 测定评估线粒体功能。通过免疫共沉淀 (Co-IP) 和蛋白质稳定性测定评估蛋白质相互作用和泛素化。在其他实验中,用纯化的 HBV 病毒粒子以不同的 MOI 刺激 LX-2 细胞,以评估直接病毒效应。AURKA 在 CHB 患者和 HBV-CM 治疗的 LX-2 HSC 中显着上调。在 LX-2 HSC 中,AURKA 敲低减弱了 HBV-CM 诱导的炎症细胞因子分泌(IL-1β、IL-6)、线粒体功能障碍(线粒体 ROS、线粒体膜电位)、HSC 增殖和纤维化标志物表达(COL I、α-SMA)。从机制上讲,AURKA 与 TLR3 相互作用,并与其 K48 连接的多聚泛素化和降解相关。TLR3 沉默消除了 AURKA 敲低的保护作用。在体内,使用持续性 HBV rcccDNA 小鼠模型,AURKA 敲低通过上调 TLR3 改善 HBV 诱导的肝脏炎症和纤维化。值得注意的是,单独纯化的 HBV 病毒体足以剂量依赖性地诱导 HSC 中的 AURKA 表达和下游纤维化反应。这项研究确定了一个新的 AURKA/TLR3 轴,可以调节 HBV 诱导的 HSC 激活和肝纤维化,将 AURKA 定位为 CHB 相关纤维化的潜在治疗靶点。

通过 ROS 和 JC-1 测定评估线粒体功能。

AURKA regulates HBV-induced hepatic stellate cell inflammation and liver fibrosis by modulating K48-linked ubiquitination and proteasomal degradation of TLR3

Authors: Hongjie Wu, Tianbao Wang, Fang Yang, Mingqiang Yue, Aihui Li, Changyun Si, 王天宝, Si Cw
Original Abstract
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB), caused by persistent hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection, continues to pose a major global health threat. Aurora kinase A (AURKA) has been implicated in fibrosis, yet its role in HBV-induced liver fibrosis remains unclear. This study investigated the function and mechanism of AURKA in hepatic stellate cell (HSC) activation and fibrogenesis during CHB. Bioinformatics screening of GSE83148 dataset and validation in human CHB liver specimens, LX-2 HSCs treated with conditioned medium (CM) from HBV-replicating cells, and a persistent HBV recombinant cccDNA (rcccDNA) mouse model established by tail-vein injection of Ad/rcccDNA were performed. Gene and protein expression was analyzed by quantitative PCR and immunoblotting, respectively. Cytokine levels were measured by ELISA. Mitochondrial function was assessed via ROS and JC-1 assays. Protein interactions and ubiquitination were evaluated by co-immunoprecipitation (Co-IP) and protein stability assays. In additional experiments, LX-2 cells were stimulated with purified HBV virions at varying MOIs to assess direct viral effects. AURKA was significantly upregulated in CHB patients and HBV-CM-treated LX-2 HSCs. In LX-2 HSCs, AURKA knockdown attenuated HBV-CM-induced inflammatory cytokine secretion (IL‐1β, IL‐6), mitochondrial dysfunction (mitochondrial ROS, mitochondrial membrane potential), HSC proliferation, and fibrotic marker expression (COL I, α-SMA). Mechanistically, AURKA interacted with TLR3 and was associated with its K48-linked polyubiquitination and degradation. TLR3 silencing abolished the protective effects of AURKA knockdown. In vivo, using a persistent HBV rcccDNA mouse model, AURKA knockdown ameliorated HBV-induced hepatic inflammation and fibrosis by upregulating TLR3. Notably, purified HBV virions alone were sufficient to dose-dependently induce AURKA expression and downstream fibrotic responses in HSCs. This study identifies a novel AURKA/TLR3 axis that regulates HBV-induced HSC activation and liver fibrosis, positioning AURKA as a potential therapeutic target for CHB-associated fibrosis.

Core methods

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

可编程纳米抗体靶向嵌合体可实现细胞内病毒蛋白降解。

Programmable Nanobody-Targeting Chimeras Enable Intracellular Viral Protein Degradation.
作者： Max Yu-Chen Pan, Ting-Hui Lee, Tzu-Ning Peng, Eva Yi-Hsuan Wu, Yu-Ting Chiu, Chih-Chien Chiu, Hong Vinh Nguyen, Yuan-Shao Pao, Ming-Hong Chao, Richard Kuan-Lin Lee
摘要中文翻译摘要:慢性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是全球主要的健康挑战,很大程度上是因为当前的抗病毒治疗很少实现病毒表面抗原 (HBsAg) 的持续清除,而病毒表面抗原 (HBsAg) 是免疫耐受和病毒持久性的核心驱动因素。在这里,我们提出了一个可编程的、基于纳米抗体的靶向蛋白质降解平台,称为纳米抗体靶向嵌合体(Nab-TAC),专为乙型肝炎病毒抗原的细胞内清除而设计。为了系统地识别功能降解模块,我们开发了一个基于分裂荧光素酶互补的定量筛选平台,该平台以降解决定子基序和邻近诱导效应子为基准,从而能够对不同细胞环境和亚细胞区室中的蛋白酶体和溶酶体靶向元件进行全面排名。通过将噬菌体展示衍生的纳米抗体和单链可变片段与这些优化的降解模块耦合,我们设计了一组 Nab-TAC 构建体,可以有效降解肝细胞中的病毒 HBsAg。值得注意的是,在 HBV 水动力注射小鼠模型中,先导构建体 E3-FCGR3B 实现了强大的肝内抗原清除并显着降低了循环 HBsAg 水平。总之,这些发现确立了 Nab-TAC 作为靶向抗病毒蛋白降解的模块化和多功能平台,并强调了其作为慢性 HBV 感染功能性治愈的治疗策略的潜力。

Programmable Nanobody-Targeting Chimeras Enable Intracellular Viral Protein Degradation.

Authors: Max Yu-Chen Pan, Ting-Hui Lee, Tzu-Ning Peng, Eva Yi-Hsuan Wu, Yu-Ting Chiu, Chih-Chien Chiu, Hong Vinh Nguyen, Yuan-Shao Pao, Ming-Hong Chao, Richard Kuan-Lin Lee
Original Abstract
ABSTRACT Chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major global health challenge, largely because current antiviral therapies seldom achieve sustained clearance of the viral surface antigen (HBsAg), a central driver of immune tolerance and viral persistence. Here, we present a programmable, nanobody‐based targeted protein degradation platform, termed Nanobody‐Targeting Chimera (Nab‐TAC), designed for the intracellular clearance of HBV antigens. To systematically identify functional degradation modules, we developed a split‐luciferase complementation‐based quantitative screening platform that benchmarks degron motifs and proximity‐inducing effectors, enabling comprehensive ranking of proteasome‐ and lysosome‐targeting elements across diverse cellular contexts and subcellular compartments. By coupling phage display‐derived nanobodies and single‐chain variable fragments with these optimized degradation modules, we engineered a panel of Nab‐TAC constructs that efficiently degrade viral HBsAg in hepatocytes. Notably, the lead construct, E3‐FCGR3B, achieved robust intrahepatic antigen clearance and markedly reduced circulating HBsAg levels in an HBV hydrodynamic injection mouse model. Together, these findings establish Nab‐TAC as a modular and versatile platform for targeted antiviral protein degradation and highlight its potential as a therapeutic strategy toward a functional cure for chronic HBV infection.

乙型肝炎表面抗原丧失后的生活质量和自我报告的耻辱:加拿大乙型肝炎网络的多中心研究

Quality of life and self-reported stigma following hepatitis B surface antigen loss: A multi-site study by the Canadian Hepatitis B Network
作者： Jordyn Vienneau, Hin Hin Ko, Curtis L Cooper, Karen E Doucette, Magdy Elkashab, Alnoor Ramji, Edward Tam, Scott K Fung, Pam Crotty, Carla S. Coffin
摘要中文翻译摘要背景:慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 会带来不良的健康结果,但也可能导致心理社会和生活质量 (QOL) 挑战。乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 丧失,无论是自发的还是由于治疗(即功能性治愈)导致的,都与临床结果的改善相关,但 HBsAg 清除率对患者报告的结果测量 (PROM) 的影响知之甚少。方法:对有慢性乙型肝炎病史且有来自加拿大六个肝病诊所的 HBsAg 清除记录的成年人进行两项调查(乙型肝炎自我污名和乙型肝炎相关生活质量 [HBQOL])。结果:共有 84 名参与者入组(中位年龄:60 岁;53.6% 为男性)。近四分之三(72.6%)的患者表示从未感到自我耻辱。所有六个领域的 HBQOL 分数均有所改善(每个领域 p < 0.001),其中心理健康(减少 60%)、传播担忧(55%)和耻辱(53%)方面的相对收益最大。结论:HBsAg 消失与患者报告的耻辱感和生活质量的显着改善相关。这些发现强调了将 PROM 纳入乙型肝炎病毒临床护理以更好地获取治疗经验的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 造成临床和社会心理负担,虽然乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 丧失与临床结果改善相关,但其对生活质量 (QOL) 和自我污名等患者报告结果指标 (PROM) 的影响仍不清楚。
设计与对象
这项多中心研究从加拿大六个肝病诊所招募了有 HBsAg 清除记录的成年人,并进行了两项评估乙型肝炎自我耻辱和乙型肝炎相关生活质量的调查。
核心方法
对有慢性乙型肝炎病史并已确认 HBsAg 清除的参与者进行了两项经过验证的调查,一项是测量乙型肝炎的自我污名,另一项是乙型肝炎相关的生活质量。
主要结果
在 84 名参与者(中位年龄 60 岁;53.6% 为男性)中,72.6% 的人表示从未经历过自我耻辱,HBQOL 评分在所有六个领域均显着改善(每个领域 p < 0.001),其中心理健康(减少 60%)、传播担忧(55%)和耻辱(53%)方面的相对收益最大。
解释与创新
作者将 HBsAg 消失解释为与患者报告的耻辱感和生活质量的显着改善相关,这突显了乙型肝炎管理中功能性治愈的先前被低估的益处。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,将 PROM 纳入乙型肝炎临床护理可以更好地获取治疗经验并为以患者为中心的护理策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于摘要报告,缺乏详细的方法论,并且没有解决受调查群体之外的潜在选择偏差或普遍性,需要谨慎解释。

Quality of life and self-reported stigma following hepatitis B surface antigen loss: A multi-site study by the Canadian Hepatitis B Network

Authors: Jordyn Vienneau, Hin Hin Ko, Curtis L Cooper, Karen E Doucette, Magdy Elkashab, Alnoor Ramji, Edward Tam, Scott K Fung, Pam Crotty, Carla S. Coffin
Original Abstract
Abstract Background: Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) imposes adverse health outcomes but may also cause psychosocial and quality-of-life (QOL) challenges. Hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss, whether spontaneous or due to treatment (ie, a functional cure), is associated with improved clinical outcomes, but the impact of HBsAg clearance on patient-reported outcome measures (PROMs) is poorly understood. Methods: Two surveys (hepatitis B self-stigma and hepatitis B–related QOL [HBQOL]) were administered to adults with a history of CHB with documented HBsAg clearance from six Canadian hepatology clinics. Results: Eighty-four participants were enrolled (median age: 60 years; 53.6% male). Nearly three quarters (72.6%) of patients reported never feeling self-stigma. HBQOL scores improved across all six domains ( p < 0.001 for each), with the largest relative gains in psychological well-being (60% reduction), transmission concerns (55%), and stigma (53%). Conclusions: HBsAg loss was associated with significant improvements in patient-reported stigma and QOL. These findings highlight the importance of incorporating PROMs into hepatitis B virus clinical care to better capture treatment experiences.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Chronic hepatitis B (CHB) poses both clinical and psychosocial burdens, and while hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg) loss is linked to improved clinical outcomes, its effect on patient-reported outcome measures (PROMs) such as quality of life (QOL) and self-stigma remains unclear.
Design and population
This multi-site study recruited adults with documented HBsAg clearance from six Canadian hepatology clinics and administered two surveys assessing hepatitis B self-stigma and hepatitis B–related QOL.
Core methods
Two validated surveys—one measuring hepatitis B self-stigma and the other hepatitis B–related QOL—were administered to participants with a history of CHB and confirmed HBsAg clearance.
Main results
Among 84 participants (median age 60 years; 53.6% male), 72.6% reported never experiencing self-stigma, and HBQOL scores improved significantly across all six domains (p < 0.001 for each), with the largest relative gains in psychological well-being (60% reduction), transmission concerns (55%), and stigma (53%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret HBsAg loss as being associated with substantial improvements in patient-reported stigma and QOL, highlighting a previously underappreciated benefit of functional cure in hepatitis B management.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that incorporating PROMs into hepatitis B clinical care could better capture treatment experiences and inform patient-centered care strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to an abstract-only report, lacks detailed methodology, and does not address potential selection bias or generalizability beyond the surveyed cohort, warranting cautious interpretation.

PD-1 阻断释放局部乙型肝炎病毒相关的 B 细胞反应,抑制肝细胞癌。

PD-1 blockade unleashes local hepatitis B virus-related B cell response inhibiting hepatocellular carcinoma.
作者： Shuling Chen, Yuanqi Wang, Jingying Chen, Wenxuan Xie, Guanrui Liao, Xiaoxue Ren, Kai Lei, Yuting Zhang, Shu Wang, Ye Yang
摘要中文翻译普遍的观点是效应 T 细胞激活介导癌症中的抗 PD-1 功效。在这里,我们进行了一项与可切除复发性肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患者围手术期抗 PD-1 治疗 (NCT04615143) 患者的 2 期试验平行的机制研究,以研究其作用机制。通过动态单细胞多组学分析,晚期复发患者呈现出两种不同的亚型,其特征是肿瘤微环境中 T 细胞或 B 细胞占主导地位的反应。克隆抗体库分析和空间配对的 scRNA-seq/BCR-seq 揭示了在这些 B 型晚期复发患者中,体细胞超突变促进肿瘤三级淋巴结构 (TLS) 内针对乙型肝炎病毒核心抗原 (HBcAg) 的抗体结合。从机制上讲,HBcAg 被输出到细胞外空间,触发局部 B 细胞和抗体反应以及补体激活。在小鼠中,这些高亲和力 HBcAg 反应性抗体可产生补体介导的抗肿瘤活性,并增强抗 PD-1 功效。因此,我们发现 TLS 内增强的抗病毒 B 细胞免疫是 HCC 中抗 PD-1 的机制。

PD-1 blockade unleashes local hepatitis B virus-related B cell response inhibiting hepatocellular carcinoma.

Authors: Shuling Chen, Yuanqi Wang, Jingying Chen, Wenxuan Xie, Guanrui Liao, Xiaoxue Ren, Kai Lei, Yuting Zhang, Shu Wang, Ye Yang
Original Abstract
The prevailing notion is that effector T cell activation mediates anti-PD-1 efficacy in cancer. Here, we conducted a mechanistic study parallel to our phase 2 trial of perioperative anti-PD-1 therapy in patients with resectable recurrent hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) (NCT04615143) to study its mechanism of action. Late-recurrence patients present two distinct subtypes characterized by T cell or B cell dominant responses in the tumor microenvironment by dynamic single-cell multi-omics analysis. Clonal antibody repertoire analysis and spatially paired scRNA-seq/BCR-seq reveal somatic hypermutation promoting antibody binding against hepatitis B virus core antigen (HBcAg) within tumor tertiary lymphoid structures (TLSs) in these type B-late recurrence patients. Mechanistically, HBcAg is exported into the extracellular space, triggering local B cell and antibody responses and complement activation. In mice, these high-affinity HBcAg-reactive antibodies lead to complement-mediated antitumor activity with enhanced anti-PD-1 efficacy. Thus, we uncover enhanced anti-virus B cell immunity within the TLS as a mechanism to anti-PD-1 in HCC.

2024 年瓦努阿图医护人员的乙型肝炎知识、态度和实践。

Hepatitis B knowledge, attitudes and practices among health-care workers in Vanuatu, 2024.
作者： Leila Bell, Kaylene Kalmos, Annie Taissets, Florita Toa, Sereana Natuman, Prof Mark Stoove, M. H. Hellard, Nicole Allard, Caroline van Gemert
摘要中文翻译目标:瓦努阿图慢性乙型肝炎的患病率很高,约为 9%。虽然自 1989 年以来婴儿就可以进行免疫接种,但部分成年人口仍然易感,其中包括卫生保健工作者。在计划向医护人员推出乙型肝炎疫苗接种之前,我们进行了一项知识、态度和做法调查,为旨在促进疫苗接种的教育计划提供信息。方法:邀请有乙型肝炎职业暴露风险的临床和非临床卫生保健专业人员于 2024 年 4 月至 6 月完成一项在线调查。该调查寻求乙型肝炎知识(10 个问题)、态度(6 个问题)和临床实践(3 个问题)以及参与者人口统计信息。计算参与者的知识分数,并使用费舍尔精确检验探索与人口统计因素的潜在关联。结果:50 名受访者中大多数是女性 (73%),并且在医院或卫生部工作 (82%)。知识水平较高,中位数为9分(范围:3-10);21名参与者得分为100%。对于与治疗可用性和传播风险相关的问题,错误回答的比例最高。我们没有发现人口因素与知识得分之间存在关联的证据。大多数参与者认为乙型肝炎疫苗有用(88%),并且预防和控制措施可以保护他们免受感染(96%)。讨论:我们的调查显示,人们对乙型肝炎患者和感染控制措施的了解水平较高,普遍持积极态度。虽然我们的受访者不太可能代表瓦努阿图的所有卫生保健工作者,但调查结果提供了有关具体知识差距的有用见解,这些差距可以在疫苗接种推出之前计划的卫生保健工作者教育课程中得到解决。

Hepatitis B knowledge, attitudes and practices among health-care workers in Vanuatu, 2024.

Authors: Leila Bell, Kaylene Kalmos, Annie Taissets, Florita Toa, Sereana Natuman, Prof Mark Stoove, M. H. Hellard, Nicole Allard, Caroline van Gemert
Original Abstract
Objective: The prevalence of chronic hepatitis B in Vanuatu is high, at approximately 9%. While immunization has been available for infants since 1989, subsets of the adult population remain susceptible, including health-care workers. Prior to a planned roll-out of hepatitis B vaccination for health-care workers, we conducted a knowledge, attitudes and practices survey to inform education programmes aimed at promoting vaccine uptake. Methods: Clinical and non-clinical health-care professionals at risk of occupational exposure to hepatitis B were invited to complete an online survey from April to June 2024. The survey sought information on hepatitis B knowledge (10 questions), attitudes (6 questions) and clinical practices (3 questions), as well as participant demographics. Participant knowledge scores were calculated and potential associations with demographic factors explored using Fisher's exact test. Results: Most of the 50 respondents were female (73%) and worked in either hospitals or the Ministry of Health (82%). Knowledge was high, with a median score of 9 (range: 3-10); 21 participants scored 100%. The proportion of incorrect responses was highest for questions related to treatment availability and transmission risks. We found no evidence of associations between demographic factors and knowledge scores. Most participants believed that hepatitis B vaccines were useful (88%) and prevention and control measures would protect them from infection (96%). Discussion: Our survey revealed high levels of knowledge and generally positive attitudes towards people with hepatitis B and infection control practices. While our respondents are unlikely to be representative of all health-care workers in Vanuatu, findings offer useful insights into specific knowledge gaps that could be addressed in planned health-care worker education sessions ahead of the vaccination roll-out.

Core methods

Main results
We found no evidence of associations between demographic factors and knowledge scores.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
