结构性障碍导致中国山东省流动人口乙型肝炎预防的意向与行动差距:一项血清学、空间和行为数据的三角研究。

Structural barriers drive the intention-action gap in hepatitis B prevention among migrants in Shandong Province, China: a triangulated study of serological, spatial, and behavioral data.
作者： Yuan Sun, Bingyang Li, Na Li, Feng Hao, Peng Chen
摘要中文翻译背景:乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是中国的一个重大公共卫生挑战。由于地域流动性大和职业脆弱性,农民工构成高度弱势群体。尽管做出了公共卫生教育努力,但明显的意图与行动差距扰乱了乙肝护理级联。这项研究整合了血清学、空间和行为数据,以调查这种差距是否主要是由认知缺陷造成的,还是被潜在的结构性障碍所中断。方法:在中国烟台进行了一项三角横断面研究,包括结构化问卷调查(N = 1,184)和血清学检测(N = 2,364)。使用地理信息系统 (GIS) 绘制区域抗 HBs 阳性率的空间分布。使用结构方程模型 (SEM) 和鲁棒最大似然估计器对改编后的健康信念模型进行测试,以评估客观结构约束(特别是时间贫困、经济约束和系统性缺乏医疗保险)的破坏性影响。结果:血清学确诊的抗-HBs阳性率为51.10%(1,208/2,364),远低于世界卫生组织推荐的70%阈值。空间分析显示,地理异质性显着(χ2 = 74.38,P < 0.001),免疫力较高的地区集中在规模较大的工业带(67.33%),免疫力较低的地区集中在分散的服务业区(36.81%)。SEM 结果表明,虽然外部线索强烈预测 HBV 知识(β = 0.894),但知识对预防意图只有边际影响(β = 0.192)。预防意图显着预测实际行为(β = 0.655)。然而,这种激励过程受到结构性障碍的严重破坏,主要是时间贫困(β = -0.563,P < 0.001),其次是经济约束(β = -0.278,P < 0.001)和缺乏医疗保险(β = -0.056,P = 0.030)。结论:农民工乙肝预防的意向与行动差距主要是由社会经济结构性障碍(尤其是时间贫困)造成的,而基本的乙肝知识仍然是预防行动的基础但不充分的条件。实现消除病毒性肝炎的目标需要从个人认知说服转向结构性干预,强调基于工作场所的服务提供、有针对性的空间资源分配以及保险改革,以减轻自付费用和机会成本。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)感染仍然是中国的一个重大公共卫生挑战,特别是对于面临高度地域流动性和职业脆弱性的农民工而言。尽管做出了公共卫生教育努力,但明显的意图与行动差距扰乱了乙型肝炎护理级联,促使该研究调查这种差距是否是由认知缺陷或结构性障碍造成的。
设计与对象
在中国烟台进行了一项三角横断面研究,对农民工进行了结构化问卷调查(N = 1,184)和血清学检测(N = 2,364)。该研究整合了血清学、空间和行为数据来检查乙型肝炎预防行为。
核心方法
使用地理信息系统 (GIS) 绘制区域抗 HBs 阳性率的空间分布。使用结构方程模型 (SEM) 和鲁棒最大似然估计器测试了改编后的健康信念模型,以评估客观结构约束的破坏性影响,包括时间贫困、经济约束和缺乏医疗保险。
主要结果
血清学确诊的抗-HBs阳性率为51.10%(1,208/2,364),远低于世界卫生组织建议的70%阈值。空间分析显示,地理异质性显着(χ2 = 74.38,P < 0.001),免疫力较高的地区集中在规模较大的工业带(67.33%),免疫力较低的地区集中在分散的服务业区(36.81%)。SEM 结果表明,虽然外部线索强烈预测 HBV 知识(β = 0.894),但知识对预防意图(β = 0.192)只有边际影响,而预防意图又显着预测实际行为(β = 0.655)。
解释与创新
农民工乙肝预防的意向与行动差距主要是由社会经济结构性障碍造成的——最明显的是时间贫困(β = -0.563,P < 0.001)——而基本的乙肝知识仍然是预防行动的基础但不充分的条件。该研究明确地将认知因素与结构性决定因素进行对比,强调仅以知识为中心的干预措施是不够的。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,实现消除病毒性肝炎需要从个人认知说服转向结构性干预,强调基于工作场所的服务提供、有针对性的空间资源分配以及保险改革,以减轻自付费用和机会成本。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到横断面设计的限制,它排除了因果推断,而且它对单一地理环境(烟台)的依赖,可能限制了对其他流动人口的普遍适用性。由于血清学、空间和行为数据的三角测量,证据强度中等,但由于缺乏纵向随访而受到限制。

Structural barriers drive the intention-action gap in hepatitis B prevention among migrants in Shandong Province, China: a triangulated study of serological, spatial, and behavioral data.

Authors: Yuan Sun, Bingyang Li, Na Li, Feng Hao, Peng Chen
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health challenge in China. Migrant workers constitute a highly vulnerable population due to high geographic mobility and occupational vulnerability. Despite public health education efforts, a notable intention-action gap disrupts the HBV care cascade. This study integrates serological, spatial, and behavioral data to investigate whether this gap is primarily driven by cognitive deficits or interrupted by underlying structural barriers. METHODS: A triangulated cross-sectional study was conducted in Yantai, China, involving structured questionnaire surveys (N = 1,184) and serological testing (N = 2,364). The spatial distribution of regional anti-HBs positive rates was mapped using Geographic Information Systems (GIS). An adapted Health Belief Model was tested using Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) with a Robust Maximum Likelihood estimator to evaluate the disruptive effects of objective structural constraints-specifically, time poverty, economic constraints, and systemic lack of medical insurance. RESULTS: The serologically confirmed anti-HBs positive rate was 51.10% (1,208/2,364), substantially lower than the 70% threshold recommended by the World Health Organization. Spatial analysis revealed significant geographic heterogeneity (χ2 = 74.38, P < 0.001), with higher immunity concentrated in large-scale industrial belts (67.33%) and lower immunity clustered in dispersed service-sector districts (36.81%). SEM results indicated that while external cues strongly predicted HBV knowledge (β = 0.894), knowledge had only a marginal effect on preventive intention (β = 0.192). Preventive intention significantly predicted actual behavior (β = 0.655). However, this motivational process was severely undermined by structural barriers, predominantly time poverty (β = -0.563, P < 0.001), followed by economic constraints (β = -0.278, P < 0.001) and lack of medical insurance (β = -0.056, P = 0.030). CONCLUSIONS: The intention-action gap in HBV prevention among migrant workers is primarily driven by socioeconomic structural barriers-most notably time poverty-while basic HBV knowledge remains a foundational but insufficient condition for preventive action. Achieving the goal of viral hepatitis elimination requires a shift from individual cognitive persuasion to structural interventions, emphasizing workplace-based service delivery, spatially targeted resource allocation, and insurance reforms to alleviate out-of-pocket and opportunity costs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major public health challenge in China, particularly among migrant workers who face high geographic mobility and occupational vulnerability. Despite public health education efforts, a notable intention-action gap disrupts the HBV care cascade, prompting the study to investigate whether this gap is driven by cognitive deficits or structural barriers.
Design and population
A triangulated cross-sectional study was conducted in Yantai, China, involving structured questionnaire surveys (N = 1,184) and serological testing (N = 2,364) among migrant workers. The study integrated serological, spatial, and behavioral data to examine HBV prevention behaviors.
Core methods
The spatial distribution of regional anti-HBs positive rates was mapped using Geographic Information Systems (GIS). An adapted Health Belief Model was tested using Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) with a Robust Maximum Likelihood estimator to evaluate the disruptive effects of objective structural constraints, including time poverty, economic constraints, and lack of medical insurance.
Main results
The serologically confirmed anti-HBs positive rate was 51.10% (1,208/2,364), substantially lower than the 70% threshold recommended by the World Health Organization. Spatial analysis revealed significant geographic heterogeneity (χ2 = 74.38, P < 0.001), with higher immunity concentrated in large-scale industrial belts (67.33%) and lower immunity clustered in dispersed service-sector districts (36.81%). SEM results indicated that while external cues strongly predicted HBV knowledge (β = 0.894), knowledge had only a marginal effect on preventive intention (β = 0.192), which in turn significantly predicted actual behavior (β = 0.655).
Interpretation and novelty
The intention-action gap in HBV prevention among migrant workers is primarily driven by socioeconomic structural barriers—most notably time poverty (β = -0.563, P < 0.001)—while basic HBV knowledge remains a foundational but insufficient condition for preventive action. The study explicitly contrasts cognitive factors with structural determinants, highlighting the inadequacy of knowledge-focused interventions alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that achieving viral hepatitis elimination requires a shift from individual cognitive persuasion to structural interventions, emphasizing workplace-based service delivery, spatially targeted resource allocation, and insurance reforms to alleviate out-of-pocket and opportunity costs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its cross-sectional design, which precludes causal inference, and its reliance on a single geographic setting (Yantai), potentially limiting generalizability to other migrant populations. The evidence strength is moderate due to the triangulation of serological, spatial, and behavioral data but constrained by the absence of longitudinal follow-up.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

libevitug 在慢性乙型肝炎和丁型肝炎病毒合并感染患者中的安全性和有效性:中国的单中心、开放标签、IIa 期试验

Safety and efficacy of libevitug in patients with chronic hepatitis B and D virus coinfection: A single-center, open-label, phase IIa trial in China
作者： Xinrui Wang, Xiumei Chi, Yingyu Zhang, Fei Kong, Xin Zhou, Yumei Gu, Yonghe Qi, Liangfeng Zou, Zhongmin Zhou, Jiaying Wen
摘要中文翻译背景:慢性 HBV 和 HDV 合并感染是最严重的病毒性肝炎。本研究旨在评估 libevitug (HH-003)(一种针对 HBV 大包膜蛋白 PreS1 结构域的新型人单克隆抗体)在慢性 HBV 和 HDV 合并感染患者中的安全性和有效性。方法:这项单中心、开放标签、概念验证 IIa 期临床试验招募了 9 名接受静脉注射的参与者。每2周输注20 mg/kg libevitug,持续24周,然后是24周的随访期。结果:第 24 周时,8 名参与者 (88.9%) 的 HDV RNA 较基线降低≥1 log 10,其中 4 名参与者 (44.4%) 达到不可检测的水平。7 名参与者 (77.8%) 实现了病毒学应答(HDV RNA 检测不到或较基线下降≥2 log 10)。在基线 ALT 升高的 5 名参与者中,3 名 (60.0%) 实现了病毒学和生化综合反应(ALT 正常化)。在第 48 周,6 名参与者 (66.7%) 维持了治疗结束时(第 24 周)所达到的病毒学应答,其中 3 名参与者 (33.3%) 维持了不可检测的 HDV RNA 水平。在基线时具有可量化 HBV DNA 的 7 名参与者中,2 名 (28.6%) 在第 24 周时实现了检测不到 HBV DNA 或 ≥1 log 10 降低。在整个研究期间,血清 HBsAg 水平保持不变。8 名参与者 (88.9%) 报告了治疗引起的不良事件,只有 1 名参与者 (11.1%) 经历了与治疗相关的不良事件。未报告 3 级或严重不良事件,也未发生因不良事件而停药的情况。结论:Libevitug 在慢性 HBV 和 HDV 合并感染的参与者中表现出良好的抗病毒活性,可显着降低 HDV RNA,提高 ALT 正常化率,并具有良好的安全性和耐受性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估 libevitug (HH-003)(一种针对 HBV 大包膜蛋白 PreS1 结构域的新型人单克隆抗体)在慢性 HBV 和 HDV 合并感染患者中的安全性和有效性。
设计与对象
这项单中心、开放标签、概念验证 IIa 期临床试验招募了 9 名接受静脉注射的参与者。
核心方法
7 名参与者 (77.8%) 实现了病毒学应答(HDV RNA 检测不到或较基线下降≥2 log 10)。
主要结果
8 名参与者 (88.9%) 报告了治疗引起的不良事件,只有 1 名参与者 (11.1%) 经历了与治疗相关的不良事件。
解释与创新
在整个研究期间,血清 HBsAg 水平保持不变。
科研与公卫意义
Libevitug 表现出良好的抗病毒活性,在慢性 HBV 和 HDV 合并感染的参与者中实现了 HDV RNA 的大幅降低、ALT 正常化率高以及良好的安全性和耐受性。

这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Safety and efficacy of libevitug in patients with chronic hepatitis B and D virus coinfection: A single-center, open-label, phase IIa trial in China

Authors: Xinrui Wang, Xiumei Chi, Yingyu Zhang, Fei Kong, Xin Zhou, Yumei Gu, Yonghe Qi, Liangfeng Zou, Zhongmin Zhou, Jiaying Wen
Original Abstract
Background: Chronic HBV and HDV coinfection represents the most severe form of viral hepatitis. This study aimed to assess the safety and efficacy of libevitug (HH-003), a novel human monoclonal antibody targeting the PreS1 domain of the HBV large envelope protein, in patients with chronic HBV and HDV coinfection. Methods: This single-center, open-label, proof-of-concept phase IIa clinical trial enrolled 9 participants who received i.v. infusion of 20 mg/kg libevitug every 2 weeks for 24 weeks, followed by a 24-week follow-up period. Results: At week 24, 8 participants (88.9%) exhibited a reduction in HDV RNA ≥1 log 10 from baseline, with 4 (44.4%) achieving undetectable levels. 7 participants (77.8%) achieved virological response (undetectable HDV RNA or ≥2 log 10 decline from baseline). Among the 5 participants with elevated baseline ALT, 3 (60.0%) achieved a combined virological and biochemical response (ALT normalization). At week 48, 6 participants (66.7%) maintained the virological response achieved at the end of treatment (week 24), with 3 (33.3%) maintaining undetectable HDV RNA levels. Among the 7 participants with quantifiable HBV DNA at baseline, 2 (28.6%) achieved undetectable HBV DNA or ≥1 log 10 reduction at week 24. Serum HBsAg levels remained unchanged throughout the study period. Treatment-emergent adverse events were reported in 8 participants (88.9%), with only 1 participant (11.1%) experiencing treatment-related adverse events. No grade 3 or serious adverse events were reported, and no discontinuations due to adverse events occurred. Conclusions: Libevitug demonstrated promising antiviral activity, achieving substantial HDV RNA reduction, a high rate of ALT normalization, and favorable safety and tolerability in participants with chronic HBV and HDV coinfection.

源自 FUBP1-IN-1 的新型化合物 0411 通过上调 DUSP5 对抗乙型肝炎病毒具有抗病毒活性。

Antiviral activity of novel chemical compound 0411, derived from FUBP1-IN-1 against hepatitis B virus by upregulation of DUSP5.
作者： Shuang Zhao, Haisha Wei, Yiyang Liang, Hui Fan
摘要中文翻译目前一线药物很少能对慢性乙型肝炎患者实现功能性治愈,这促使我们探索新的抗乙型肝炎治疗策略。在这项研究中,我们发现新型化合物 0411(FUBP1 抑制剂 FUBPI-IN-1 的衍生物)在体外和体内均显着抑制 HBV 转录。转录组测序表明,0411 处理显着上调了双特异性磷酸酶 5 (DUSP5) 基因的表达,该基因是 MAPK 通路的关键调节因子。进一步的功能研究表明,化合物0411的抗病毒活性依赖于DUSP5。从机制上来说,HepG2-NTCP 细胞中的 0411 处理显着增强了活性组蛋白修饰在 DUSP5 启动子上的沉积。总之,我们的研究结果证明了 0411 作为有效抗 HBV 转录药物的潜力。

Antiviral activity of novel chemical compound 0411, derived from FUBP1-IN-1 against hepatitis B virus by upregulation of DUSP5.

Authors: Shuang Zhao, Haisha Wei, Yiyang Liang, Hui Fan
Original Abstract
Current first-line drugs rarely achieve functional curesfor chronic hepatitis B patients, which urges us to explore novel anti-HBV therapeutic strategies. In this study, we found that the novel chemical compound 0411, a derivative of the FUBP1 inhibitor, FUBPI-IN-1, significantly suppressed HBV transcription both in vitro and in vivo. Transcriptome sequencing demonstrated that 0411 treatment markedly upregulated the expression of the dual-specificity phosphatase 5 (DUSP5) gene, a crucial regulator of the MAPK pathway. Further functional studies illustrated that the antiviral activity of compound 0411 was dependent on DUSP5. Mechanismly, 0411 treatment in HepG2-NTCP cells enhances the deposition of active histone modifications on the promoter of DUSP5 significantly. In conclusion, our findings demonstrate the potential of 0411 as an effective agent against HBV transcription.

艾滋病毒感染者的乙型肝炎疫苗接种:弥合免疫差距

Hepatitis B Vaccination in People Living with HIV: Bridging the Immunological Gap
作者： Christelle Radi, Jana Abu Faraj, Jad Idriss, Daniel J. Gromer, Diane Saint-Victor, Christiane S. Eberhardt, Nadine Rouphael, Suha Kalash
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 感染仍然是艾滋病毒感染者 (PLWH) 肝脏相关发病率和死亡率的主要驱动因素,但疫苗诱导的保护往往不理想。HIV 相关的免疫功能障碍,包括 CD4+ T 细胞耗竭、抗原呈递改变、滤泡辅助 T 细胞支持受损和 B 细胞失调,会降低标准重组 HBV 疫苗后的血清保护作用,并可能限制抗体反应的持久性。疫苗反应进一步受到 HIV 病毒抑制、年龄、合并症、既往疫苗史和基线 HBV 血清学状态的影响,包括分离的乙型肝炎核心抗体(抗 HBc)和隐匿性 HBV 感染 (OBI) 考虑因素。尽管抗逆转录病毒治疗 (ART) 可以提高疫苗反应性,但许多感染者在常规治疗后未能达到保护性乙型肝炎表面抗体 (抗 HBs) 滴度 (≥10 mIU/mL),或者随着时间的推移抗体逐渐减弱。目前的指南建议对所有易感感染者进行乙型肝炎疫苗接种,并进行疫苗接种后血清学检测,并对无反应者重新接种疫苗。持续存在的实施障碍,包括不完整的系列、疫苗犹豫、耻辱和后勤限制,继续限制现实世界的影响。新的临床试验数据支持 CpG 佐剂 HBV 疫苗 (HepB-CpG/Heplisav-B) 和强化剂量和方案(双剂量或四剂量方案),以改善血清保护并产生更高的峰值抗 HBs 滴度,这可能会增强耐久性。本综述综合了指南建议、低反应性的免疫机制、疫苗反应的预测因素以及优化 PLWH 乙型肝炎疫苗接种的实用策略。

所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
HIV 相关的免疫功能障碍,包括 CD4+ T 细胞耗竭、抗原呈递改变、滤泡辅助 T 细胞支持受损和 B 细胞失调,会降低标准重组 HBV 疫苗后的血清保护作用,并可能限制抗体反应的持久性。
解释与创新
新的临床试验数据支持 CpG 佐剂 HBV 疫苗 (HepB-CpG/Heplisav-B) 和强化剂量和方案(双剂量或四剂量方案),以改善血清保护并产生更高的峰值抗 HBs 滴度,这可能会增强耐久性。

Hepatitis B Vaccination in People Living with HIV: Bridging the Immunological Gap

Authors: Christelle Radi, Jana Abu Faraj, Jad Idriss, Daniel J. Gromer, Diane Saint-Victor, Christiane S. Eberhardt, Nadine Rouphael, Suha Kalash
Original Abstract
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) infection remains a major driver of liver-related morbidity and mortality among people living with HIV (PLWH), yet vaccine-induced protection is frequently suboptimal. HIV-associated immune dysfunction, including CD4+ T-cell depletion, altered antigen presentation, impaired T follicular helper cell support, and B-cell dysregulation, reduces seroprotection after standard recombinant HBV vaccines and may limit durability of antibody responses. Vaccine response is further influenced by HIV viral suppression, age, comorbidities, prior vaccine history, and baseline HBV serologic status, including isolated hepatitis B core antibody (anti-HBc) and occult HBV infection (OBI) considerations. Although antiretroviral therapy (ART) improves vaccine responsiveness, many PLWH fail to achieve protective hepatitis B surface antibody (anti-HBs) titers (≥10 mIU/mL) after conventional schedules, or experience antibody waning over time. Current guidelines recommend HBV vaccination for all susceptible PLWH with post-vaccination serologic testing and revaccination for nonresponders. Persistent implementation barriers, including incomplete series, vaccine hesitancy, stigma, and logistical constraints, continue to limit real-world impact. Emerging clinical trial data support CpG-adjuvanted HBV vaccines (HepB-CpG/Heplisav-B) and intensified dosing and schedules (double-dose or four-dose regimens) to improve seroprotection and generate higher peak anti-HBs titers, which may enhance durability. This review synthesizes guideline recommendations, immunologic mechanisms of hyporesponsiveness, predictors of vaccine response, and practical strategies to optimize HBV vaccination in PLWH.

Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

使用维生素 K 诱导蛋白 (PIVKA-II) 监测肝细胞癌 (HCC) 患者:香港的成本效益分析

Surveillance of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) patients using Protein Induced by Vitamin K (PIVKA-II): A cost-utility analysis for Hong Kong
作者： Man‐Fung Yuen, David Wastlund, Tammie Tan Yan Lin, Qishi Zheng, Junqiao Chen, Kwan‐Lung Ko
摘要中文翻译背景与目的:肝细胞癌(HCC)是香港癌症相关死亡的主要原因。常见的危险因素包括慢性乙型肝炎 (CHB) 感染和/或肝硬化 (LC)。建议对患有这些疾病的患者使用超声加甲胎蛋白 (AFP) 进行常规 HCC 监测,但许多患者接受超声检查的机会仍然有限。对于这些情况,使用维生素 K 缺乏或拮抗剂 II (PIVKA-II) 诱导的蛋白质与 AFP 的组合进行基于生物标志物的监测可以提供一种有价值的替代方案。方法:本研究将使用“PIVKA-II + AFP”的常规 HCC 监测与香港推荐的护理标准:使用“US + AFP”的监测进行了成本效益比较。使用马尔可夫微观模拟框架的健康经济模型是根据已发布的当地数据和设置进行调整的。该模型用于比较替代 HCC 监测方法的诊断结果、总体成本、质量调整生命年 (QALY) 和成本效益。结果:与目前推荐的护理标准相比,结果表明使用“PIVKA-II + AFP”进行 HCC 监测可以将早期疾病期间 HCC 的检出率提高 4.6 个百分点,从而使一些患者能够获得更有效的治疗选择。它还可以将假阳性诊断减少 7.2%,从而减少对昂贵的确认性检测的需求。总体而言,PIVKA-II + AFP' 与每人 0.019 QALY 的增益和 4,144 港元的成本节省相关。结果对于非肝硬化慢性乙型肝炎患者尤其有利。结论:对于超声监测机会有限的 CHB 和/或 LC 患者,使用“PIVKA-II + AFP”进行 HCC 监测可能是一种可行的选择。该组合有可能改善早期 HCC 检测,促进及时干预并改善生存结果和患者生活质量。

核心方法

Surveillance of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) patients using Protein Induced by Vitamin K (PIVKA-II): A cost-utility analysis for Hong Kong

Authors: Man‐Fung Yuen, David Wastlund, Tammie Tan Yan Lin, Qishi Zheng, Junqiao Chen, Kwan‐Lung Ko
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND & AIMS: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a leading cause of cancer-related death in Hong Kong. Common risk factors include chronic hepatitis B (CHB) infection and/ or liver cirrhosis (LC). Routine HCC surveillance using ultrasound plus alpha-fetoprotein (AFP) for patients with these conditions is recommended, but many patients still face limited access to ultrasound. For these, biomarker-based surveillance using a combination of protein induced by vitamin K absence or antagonist-II (PIVKA-II) plus AFP could provide a valuable alternative. METHODS: This study compared the cost-utility of routine HCC surveillance using 'PIVKA-II + AFP' to the recommended standard-of-care in Hong Kong: surveillance using 'US + AFP'. A health economic model using a Markov-microsimulation framework was adapted using published local data and settings. The model was used to compare diagnostic outcomes, overall costs, quality-adjusted life years (QALYs), and cost-utility of the alternative HCC surveillance methods. RESULTS: Compared to the current recommended standard-of-care, the results suggested that HCC surveillance using 'PIVKA-II + AFP' could improve detection of HCC during early-stage disease by 4.6 percentage points, thus enabling access to more effective treatment options for some patients. It could also reduce false positive diagnoses by 7.2%, which could alleviate the need for costly confirmatory testing. Overall, PIVKA-II + AFP' was associated with a gain of 0.019 QALYs and cost savings of HK $ 4,144 per individual. The results were particularly favorable for non-cirrhotic CHB patients. CONCLUSIONS: HCC surveillance using 'PIVKA-II + AFP' could be a feasible option for patients with CHB and/ or LC who face limited access to ultrasound-based surveillance. The combination has the potential to improve early-stage HCC detection, facilitating timely intervention and improving both survival outcomes and patient quality of life.

印度南部农村地区乙型肝炎感染的患病率及其相关因素:一项有价值的调查。

Prevalence of hepatitis B infection and its associated factors in rural South India: A valuable investigation.
作者： Jing Zhang, Lin-Jing Wang, Jin-Feng Chi, Zhang J, Wang LJ, Chi JF., Linjing Wang, Junjie Chi

基于剪切波弹性成像的肝内异质性量化可预测乙型肝炎病毒相关急性失代偿性肝硬化患者的预后

Shear wave elastography-based intrahepatic heterogeneity quantification predicts prognosis in patients with hepatitis B virus-related acutely decompensated cirrhosis
作者： Huang Xing-zhi, Xin-jia Jiang, Xin-chun Yuan, Yaohui Li, Song-song Yuan, Pan Xu, Xing-zhi Huang
摘要中文翻译摘要 目的 使用二维 (2D) 剪切波弹性成像 (SWE) 图像量化生物力学和形态学维度上的肝内异质性 (IHH),并将其整合到多模态模型中,以预测乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关急性失代偿性肝硬化 (ADC) 患者的 5 年死亡率。方法 这项回顾性研究纳入了 239 名 HBV-ADC 患者,随机分为训练组 (n = 144) 和验证组 (n = 95)。收集基线临床数据、2D SWE 图像、平均弹性值和 5 年结果。将无监督 K 均值聚类应用于从空间共同配准的 SWE 和 2D-​​SWE 图像的灰度区域中提取的局部放射组学特征,以得出双模态 IHH 分数。使用训练队列,通过多变量 Cox 回归开发多模态模型,整合独立的临床预测因子、平均 SWE 值以及双模态 IHH 和放射组学评分。结果 在验证队列中,SWE 和灰度衍生的 IHH 评分均显示出对 5 年死亡率的稳健预后价值,曲线下面积 (AUC) 分别为 0.826 和 0.795,一致性指数 (C 指数) 分别为 0.736 和 0.692。对于多模式模型,SHAP 分析将这些 IHH 评分确定为个人风险分层的最关键决定因素。值得注意的是,该模型将 AUC 和 C 指数分别提高到 0.904 和 0.801,显着优于所有单峰预测模型(所有 p

Shear wave elastography-based intrahepatic heterogeneity quantification predicts prognosis in patients with hepatitis B virus-related acutely decompensated cirrhosis

Authors: Huang Xing-zhi, Xin-jia Jiang, Xin-chun Yuan, Yaohui Li, Song-song Yuan, Pan Xu, Xing-zhi Huang
Original Abstract
Abstract Purpose To quantify intrahepatic heterogeneity (IHH) across biomechanical and morphological dimensions using two-dimensional (2D) shear wave elastography (SWE) images, and integrate it into a multimodal model for predicting 5-year mortality in patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related acutely decompensated cirrhosis (ADC). Methods This retrospective study included 239 patients with HBV-ADC, randomly allocated into a training (n = 144) and a validation cohort (n = 95). Baseline clinical data, 2D SWE images, mean elasticity values, and 5-year outcomes were collected. Unsupervised K-means clustering was applied to local radiomic features extracted from spatially co-registered SWE and gray-scale regions of 2D-SWE images to derive dual-modality IHH scores. Using the training cohort, a multimodal model was developed via multivariate Cox regression, integrating independent clinical predictors, mean SWE values, and dual-modality IHH and radiomic scores. Result In the validation cohort, both the SWE- and gray-scale-derived IHH scores demonstrated robust prognostic value for 5-year mortality, achieving time-dependent areas under the curve (AUCs) of 0.826 and 0.795, and concordance indices (C-indices) of 0.736 and 0.692, respectively. For the multimodal model, SHAP analysis identified these IHH scores as the most critical determinants for individual risk stratification. Notably, this model elevated the AUC and C-index to 0.904 and 0.801, respectively, significantly outperforming all unimodal predictive models (all p

代谢合并症影响中国乙型肝炎病毒相关肝细胞癌患者的生存:一项倾向评分匹配的回顾性队列研究

Metabolic comorbidities affect the survival of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma in Chinese patients: a retrospective cohort study with propensity score matching
作者： Zheng Zhou, Xiyan Zheng, Zhiqun Lin, Xianqing Chen, Maoyun Xie
摘要中文翻译目的 最近的文献证实了代谢合并症作为肝细胞癌(HCC)发病危险因素的作用。在中国,大多数肝癌病例归因于慢性乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)。然而,代谢合并症与中国乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)相关肝癌患者预后之间的关系仍不确定。方法 2011年至2021年中山大学附属8家医院收治的164例HBV相关HCC患者被分为MC组(代谢合并症:2型糖尿病(T2DM)、高血压、低高密度脂蛋白(HDL)水平中至少一种代谢合并症)和NMC组(无代谢合并症)和NMC组(无代谢合并症)因素)。在本手稿中,“MC”指的是代谢合并症,而不是代谢综合征。使用倾向评分匹配(PSM)和多变量Cox回归来分析总生存期(OS)。结果 PSM 后,MC 组和 NMC 组各 60 名患者纳入研究。PSM 前后,MC 组的总生存率明显低于 NMC 组。T2DM 是 OS 的独立危险因素。T2DM、高血压和低 HDL 水平是诊断时存在肝内多灶性病变的独立危险因素。所有三种代谢合并症的存在均与显着较差的 OS 相关 (p=0.043)。结论 代谢合并症与 HBV 相关 HCC 的不良预后和进展相关。三种代谢合并症(T2DM、高血压和低 HDL 水平)的存在与较差的 OS 相关,这表明一旦一定数量的因素积累,代谢负担可能会对生存产生负面影响。

Metabolic comorbidities affect the survival of hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma in Chinese patients: a retrospective cohort study with propensity score matching

Authors: Zheng Zhou, Xiyan Zheng, Zhiqun Lin, Xianqing Chen, Maoyun Xie
Original Abstract
Objective Recent literature has confirmed the roles of metabolic comorbidities as risk factors for the incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). In China, most HCC cases are attributed to chronic hepatitis B virus (HBV). However, the relationships between metabolic comorbidities and the prognosis of Chinese patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related HCC remain uncertain. Methods A total of 164 patients with HBV-related HCC from eight hospitals affiliated with Sun Yat-sen University were enrolled in this cohort study from 2011 to 2021. The patients were divided into the MC group (metabolic comorbidities: at least one metabolic comorbidity from among type 2 diabetes mellitus (T2DM), hypertension, and low high-density lipoprotein (HDL) levels) and the NMC group (without these factors). In this manuscript, “MC” refers to metabolic comorbidity, rather than metabolic syndrome. Propensity score matching (PSM) and multivariate Cox regression were used to analyse overall survival (OS). Results After PSM, 60 patients each in the MC and NMC groups were included in the study. Overall survival was significantly worse in the MC group than in the NMC group before and after PSM. T2DM was an independent risk factor for OS. T2DM, hypertension, and low HDL levels were independent risk factors for the presence of intrahepatic multifocal lesions at diagnosis. The presence of all three metabolic comorbidities was associated with significantly worse OS (p=0.043). Conclusion Metabolic comorbidities were associated with poor prognosis and the progression of HBV-related HCC. The presence of three metabolic comorbidities (T2DM, hypertension, and low HDL levels) was associated with poor OS, suggesting that metabolic burden may negatively affect survival once a certain number of factors accumulate.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Objective Recent literature has confirmed the roles of metabolic comorbidities as risk factors for the incidence of hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC).
Design and population
Methods A total of 164 patients with HBV-related HCC from eight hospitals affiliated with Sun Yat-sen University were enrolled in this cohort study from 2011 to 2021.
Core methods
Propensity score matching (PSM) and multivariate Cox regression were used to analyse overall survival (OS).
Main results
The presence of all three metabolic comorbidities was associated with significantly worse OS (p=0.043).
Interpretation and novelty
Conclusion Metabolic comorbidities were associated with poor prognosis and the progression of HBV-related HCC.
Scientific and public-health significance
T2DM, hypertension, and low HDL levels were independent risk factors for the presence of intrahepatic multifocal lesions at diagnosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
However, the relationships between metabolic comorbidities and the prognosis of Chinese patients with hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related HCC remain uncertain.

人类急性肝损伤中巨噬细胞相关 YAP1 阳性物质的组织检测:探索性免疫病理活检系列。

Tissue-based detection of macrophage-associated YAP1-positive material in human acute liver injury: an exploratory immunopathological biopsy series.
作者： Hiroteru Kamimura, Kenya Kamimura, Shuji Terai
摘要中文翻译背景:Yes 相关蛋白 1 (YAP1) 是 Hippo 通路的下游效应子,与肝细胞应激、导管反应和组织修复有关。实验研究表明,受损的 YAP1 激活肝细胞可以通过库普弗细胞吞噬作用清除,但相应的人体活检证据仍然有限。方法:我们回顾性分析了 7 名成人,他们在因急性肝损伤首次就诊或转诊入院后 10 天内接受了有临床指征的肝活检。通过苏木精和伊红染色、高碘酸希夫淀粉酶染色、YAP1 免疫组织化学以及 YAP1 和 CD68 双重免疫荧光对肝活检切片进行评估。巨噬细胞相关的 YAP1 阳性结构在操作上被定义为位于门脉或门脉区域 CD68 阳性巨噬细胞内或紧邻 CD68 阳性巨噬细胞的 YAP1 阳性信号。该定义旨在捕捉空间关联,并非旨在证明巨噬细胞的细胞内吞噬作用、分子共表达或内源性 YAP1 表达。作为探索性分析,评估了终末期肝病模型 (MELD) 评分峰值和丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 正常化天数之间的关联。结果:中位年龄为52岁,病因包括自身免疫性肝炎、原发性胆汁性胆管炎-自身免疫性肝炎重叠、药物性肝损伤、急性乙型肝炎和特发性急性肝损伤。初次就诊或转诊入院后平均 5 天(范围为 2-10 天)进行肝活检。每次活检评估了 9 个汇管束和 5 个巨噬细胞相关 YAP1 阳性结构的中位数,得出中位 YAP1/CD68 阳性汇管束指数为 58.3%。平均 26 天后 ALT 恢复正常。该指数与 ALT 正常化天数呈名义负相关(Spearman rho = -0.86,p = 0.014;Pearson r = -0.79,p = 0.034),而 MELD 评分与指数或恢复时间没有显着相关。留一法分析保留了相反的方向,但由于样本量非常小,因此显示出不稳定的显着性。结论:在这个小型探索性活检系列中,在人类早期急性肝损伤中观察到 YAP1 阳性物质与 CD68 阳性巨噬细胞存在空间关联。YAP1/CD68 阳性汇管指数显示与生化恢复存在假设生成关系,但不应被解释为经过验证的预后标志物或巨噬细胞介导的吞噬作用的证据。有必要进行更大规模、针对病因的研究,包括标准化活检时间、谱系标记、多重免疫荧光、高分辨率成像和数字病理学。

问题与背景
Yes 相关蛋白 1 (YAP1) 是 Hippo 通路的下游效应子,与肝细胞应激、导管反应和组织修复有关。
设计与对象
巨噬细胞相关的 YAP1 阳性结构在操作上被定义为位于门脉或门脉区域 CD68 阳性巨噬细胞内或紧邻 CD68 阳性巨噬细胞的 YAP1 阳性信号。
核心方法
作为探索性分析,评估了终末期肝病模型 (MELD) 评分峰值和丙氨酸转氨酶 (ALT) 正常化天数之间的关联。
主要结果
每次活检评估了 9 个汇管束和 5 个巨噬细胞相关 YAP1 阳性结构的中位数,得出中位 YAP1/CD68 阳性汇管束指数为 58.3%。
解释与创新
留一法分析保留了相反的方向,但由于样本量非常小,因此显示出不稳定的显着性。

在这个小型探索性活检系列中,在人类早期急性肝损伤中观察到 YAP1 阳性物质与 CD68 阳性巨噬细胞存在空间关联。

Tissue-based detection of macrophage-associated YAP1-positive material in human acute liver injury: an exploratory immunopathological biopsy series.

Authors: Hiroteru Kamimura, Kenya Kamimura, Shuji Terai
Original Abstract
BACKGROUND: Yes-associated protein 1 (YAP1), a downstream effector of the Hippo pathway, is implicated in hepatocellular stress, ductular reaction, and tissue repair. Experimental studies suggest that injured YAP1-activated hepatocytes can be cleared by Kupffer-cell phagocytosis, but corresponding human biopsy evidence remains limited. METHODS: We retrospectively analyzed seven adults who underwent clinically indicated liver biopsy within 10 days of initial presentation or referral admission for acute liver injury. Liver biopsy sections were assessed by hematoxylin and eosin staining, periodic acid-Schiff-diastase staining, YAP1 immunohistochemistry, and double immunofluorescence for YAP1 and CD68. Macrophage-associated YAP1-positive structures were operationally defined as YAP1-positive signals located within or immediately adjacent to CD68-positive macrophages in portal or periportal areas. The definition was intended to capture spatial association and was not intended to prove intracellular phagocytosis, molecular co-expression, or endogenous YAP1 expression by macrophages. Associations with peak Model for End-Stage Liver Disease (MELD) score and days to alanine aminotransferase (ALT) normalization were evaluated as exploratory analyses. RESULTS: The median age was 52 years, and etiologies included autoimmune hepatitis, primary biliary cholangitis-autoimmune hepatitis overlap, drug-induced liver injury, acute hepatitis B, and idiopathic acute liver injury. Liver biopsy was performed at a median of 5 days (range, 2-10 days) after initial presentation or referral admission. A median of nine portal tracts and five macrophage-associated YAP1-positive structures were evaluated per biopsy, yielding a median YAP1/CD68-positive portal-tract index of 58.3%. ALT normalized after a median of 26 days. The index showed a nominal inverse association with days to ALT normalization (Spearman rho = -0.86, p = 0.014; Pearson r = -0.79, p = 0.034), whereas MELD score was not significantly associated with the index or recovery time. Leave-one-out analyses retained the inverse direction but showed unstable significance because of the very small sample size. CONCLUSIONS: In this small exploratory biopsy series, YAP1-positive material was observed in spatial association with CD68-positive macrophages in early human acute liver injury. The YAP1/CD68-positive portal-tract index showed a hypothesis-generating relationship with biochemical recovery but should not be interpreted as a validated prognostic marker or proof of macrophage-mediated phagocytosis. Larger, etiology-specific studies with standardized biopsy timing, lineage markers, multiplex immunofluorescence, high-resolution imaging, and digital pathology are warranted.

慢加急性肝衰竭患者急性损伤和潜在慢性肝病的病因学趋势:中国西南地区的回顾性研究(2020-2023年)。

Trends in the etiology of acute insult and underlying chronic liver disease in patients with acute-on-chronic liver failure: A retrospective study in Southwest China (2020-2023).
作者： Yi Fang, Haifeng Dai, Qiufen Li, Danni Wang, Jianwen Liu, Yanan Liu
摘要中文翻译近年来,慢性肝衰竭急性损伤和潜在慢性肝病的病因学有所发展。本研究调查了 2020 年至 2023 年中国西南地区的病因模式和趋势。本研究为回顾性分析,纳入2020年至2023年在我院根据亚太肝病研究协会标准诊断为慢性肝衰竭的346例患者,分析2020年至2023年急性损伤和慢性肝病的病因及变化趋势。急性损伤的前5位病因是自发性乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)再激活(45.4%)、核苷类似物戒断引起的HBV再激活(16.2%)、药物性肝损伤(16.2%)、不明原因(6.1%)和酒精性肝炎(4.6%)。慢性肝病病因前5位依次为慢性乙型肝炎(53.2%)、乙型肝炎相关性肝硬化(22.00%)、慢性乙型肝炎合并酒精性肝炎(6.6%)、乙型肝炎合并酒精性肝炎相关性肝硬化(6.4%)、酒精性肝炎相关性肝硬化(3.8%)。2020年至2023年,病毒性肝炎患者比例先下降后上升。酒精性肝炎患者比例呈小幅下降趋势。HBV相关肝硬化的比例从18.8%上升至29.9%。慢性乙型肝炎加酒精性肝炎患者比例分别从11.8%下降至2.6%。病毒性肝炎,尤其是慢性乙型肝炎,仍然是中国西南地区急性损伤和潜在慢性肝病的主要原因。这些发现强调了有针对性的乙肝治疗策略的必要性。

Trends in the etiology of acute insult and underlying chronic liver disease in patients with acute-on-chronic liver failure: A retrospective study in Southwest China (2020-2023).

Authors: Yi Fang, Haifeng Dai, Qiufen Li, Danni Wang, Jianwen Liu, Yanan Liu
Original Abstract
The etiology of acute insults and underlying chronic liver disease in acute-on-chronic liver failure has evolved in recent years. This study examined etiological patterns and trends from 2020 to 2023 in Southwest China. This study is a retrospective analysis that included 346 patients diagnosed with acute-on-chronic liver failure according to the Asian pacific association for the study of liver criteria at our hospital from 2020 to 2023. The etiology of acute insult and chronic liver disease and changing trends from 2020 to 2023 were analyzed. The top 5 etiologies of acute insult were spontaneous hepatitis B virus (HBV) reactivation (45.4%), HBV reactivation due to nucleot(s)ide analog withdrawal (16.2%), drug-induced liver injury (16.2%), unknown (6.1%), and alcoholic hepatitis (4.6%). The top 5 etiologies of chronic liver disease were chronic hepatitis B (53.2%), HBV-associated cirrhosis (22.00%), chronic hepatitis B plus alcoholic hepatitis (6.6%), HBV plus alcoholic hepatitis-associated cirrhosis (6.4%), and alcoholic hepatitis-associated cirrhosis (3.8%). From 2020 to 2023, the percentage of patients with viral hepatitis initially declined and then increased. The percentage of patients with alcoholic hepatitis showed a slight declining trend. The proportion of HBV-associated cirrhosis increased from 18.8% to 29.9%. The percentage of patients with chronic hepatitis B plus alcoholic hepatitis decreased from 11.8% to 2.6%, respectively. Viral hepatitis, especially chronic hepatitis B, remains the leading cause of acute insult and underlying chronic liver disease in Southwest China. These findings underscore the need for targeted HBV management strategies.

优化乙型肝炎筛查:ELISA 与快速检测的比较

Optimizing Hepatitis B screening: A comparison of ELISA and rapid tests
作者： Vero Hanitriniaina HARIJAONA VOLOLONTSOA, Anselme Rakotoarivelo, Andry Rasamindrakotroka, Ny Onja Niaina RAHARIJAONA, Elga VERENAKO
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎仍然是一个主要的全球公共卫生问题,特别是在马达加斯加等高流行地区,其患病率超过 8%。诊断主要依靠 ELISA 免疫酶技术,该技术因其可靠性而得到认可,但在资源有限的实验室中并未广泛使用。快速诊断测试(RDT)提供了一种实用的替代方案,但其在这方面的性能尚未得到评估。这项前瞻性描述性和分析性研究比较了 Virucheck® 快速检测和万泰生物 Pharm ELISA 在检测乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原 (HBsAg) 方面的性能。该样本由来自镰状细胞病患者和 JRA 大学医院随访受试者的 74 份血清样本组成。结果显示,TDR 的血清阳性率为 6.75%,而 ELISA 的血清阳性率为 9.45%。TDR的敏感性为71.41%,特异性为100%,而ELISA证实了其优越性,敏感性超过95%,特异性接近98%。除了技术性能之外,该研究还强调了年轻人口占主导地位,反映了母婴传播的重要性,以及与危险性行为相关的年轻人中显着的血清流行率。这些发现强调了早期和有针对性的筛查以及强化疫苗接种策略的紧迫性。总之,马达加斯加优化乙型肝炎筛查依赖于快速检测(适合基于社区的筛查)和 ELISA 的互补使用,这对于诊断确认和临床随访至关重要。这种方法代表了在乙型肝炎高流行的情况下减少传播、提高疫苗接种覆盖率和加强乙型肝炎防治工作的战略途径。

问题与背景

设计与对象
该样本由来自镰状细胞病患者和 JRA 大学医院随访受试者的 74 份血清样本组成。
核心方法
这项前瞻性描述性和分析性研究比较了 Virucheck® 快速检测和万泰生物 Pharm ELISA 在检测乙型肝炎病毒表面抗原 (HBsAg) 方面的性能。
主要结果
结果显示,TDR 的血清阳性率为 6.75%,而 ELISA 的血清阳性率为 9.45%。

科研与公卫意义
乙型肝炎仍然是一个主要的全球公共卫生问题,特别是在马达加斯加等高流行地区,其患病率超过 8%。
局限与证据强度
这种确定性提取仅基于抽象。该消息来源没有明确报告独立评估的足够局限性,因此可信度较低。

Optimizing Hepatitis B screening: A comparison of ELISA and rapid tests

Authors: Vero Hanitriniaina HARIJAONA VOLOLONTSOA, Anselme Rakotoarivelo, Andry Rasamindrakotroka, Ny Onja Niaina RAHARIJAONA, Elga VERENAKO
Original Abstract
Hepatitis B remains a major global public health problem, particularly in areas of high endemicity such as Madagascar, where prevalence exceeds 8%. Diagnosis relies primarily on the ELISA immunoenzymatic technique, which is recognized for its reliability but is not widely available in resource-limited laboratories. Rapid diagnostic tests (RDTs) offer a practical alternative, but their performance in this context has yet to be evaluated. This prospective descriptive and analytical study compared the performance of the Virucheck® rapid test and the Wantai Bio Pharm ELISA for the detection of hepatitis B virus surface antigen (HBsAg). The sample consisted of 74 serum samples from patients with sickle cell disease and subjects followed at the JRA University Hospital. The results showed a seroprevalence of 6.75% by TDR versus 9.45% by ELISA. The TDR had a sensitivity of 71.41% and a specificity of 100%, while the ELISA confirmed its superiority with sensitivities exceeding 95% and specificities close to 98%. Beyond technical performance, the study highlighted a predominantly young population, reflecting the significance of mother-to-child transmission, as well as a notable seroprevalence among young adults linked to risky sexual behaviors. These findings underscore the urgency of early and targeted screening, combined with a strengthened vaccination strategy. In conclusion, optimizing hepatitis B screening in Madagascar relies on the complementary use of rapid tests—suitable for community-based screening—and ELISA, which is essential for diagnostic confirmation and clinical follow-up. This approach represents a strategic pathway to reduce transmission, improve vaccination coverage, and strengthen the fight against hepatitis B in a context of high endemicity.

新型 HBx 相互作用宿主细胞因子调节乙型肝炎病毒 X 蛋白 (HBx) 的动态核和细胞质定位。

Novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors regulate dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of Hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx).
作者： Yasuo Ariumi
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) X 蛋白 (HBx) 在病毒转录、复制和 HBV 相关肝癌发生中发挥关键作用。鉴定了几种新的 HBx 相互作用宿主细胞因子:DDX1、DDX3、MOV10 RNA 解旋酶、INI1/hSNF5 染色质重塑因子、Rad18 DNA 修复酶和 CPSF6 裂解因子。DDX1、DDX3 和 MOV10 与 HBx 共定位于细胞质灶或聚集体中。相反,INI1/hSNF5 和 CPSF6 与 HBx 一起重新定位于核斑点、染色质间颗粒簇 (IGC) 和/或 PML 核体中。Rad18 将 HBx 隔离在核仁中。重要的是,DDX3、MOV10 和 INI1/hSNF5 限制 HBV 复制,而 CPSF6 促进 HBV 复制。此外,DDX1 增强 HBx 介导的 NF-κB 转录,而 MOV10 和 Rad18 抑制它。总而言之,HBx 相互作用的宿主因子决定 HBx 的动态核和细胞质定位,并调节 HBV 复制和 HBx 介导的转录。

Novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors regulate dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of Hepatitis B virus X protein (HBx).

Authors: Yasuo Ariumi
Original Abstract
The hepatitis B virus (HBV) X protein (HBx) is a key player in viral transcription, replication, and HBV-associated hepatocarcinogenesis. Several novel HBx-interacting host cellular factors: DDX1, DDX3, MOV10 RNA helicases, INI1/hSNF5 chromatin-remodeling factor, Rad18 DNA repair enzyme, and CPSF6 cleavage factor were identified. DDX1, DDX3, and MOV10 colocalized with HBx in cytoplasmic foci or aggregates. In contrast, INI1/hSNF5 and CPSF6 relocalized with HBx in nuclear speckles, interchromatin granule clusters (IGCs), and/or PML-nuclear bodies. Rad18 sequestered HBx in the nucleoli. Importantly, DDX3, MOV10, and INI1/hSNF5 restricted HBV replication, whereas CPSF6 facilitated HBV replication. Furthermore, DDX1 enhanced HBx-mediated NF-κB transcription, whereas MOV10 and Rad18 inhibited it. Altogether, HBx-interacting host factors determine dynamic nuclear and cytoplasmic localization of HBx and regulate HBV replication and HBx-mediated transcription.

联合经皮耳迷走神经刺激和超声引导膈神经阻滞治疗持续性呃逆:病例报告。

Combined percutaneous auricular vagus nerve stimulation and ultrasound-guided phrenic nerve block for persistent hiccups: A case report.
作者： Qinyue Luo, Guipeng Wu, Chuangang Shi, Zongfeng Guo, Jin Wang, Ran Wang, Xiaoqing Xu, Yi-Xiang Wang
摘要中文翻译理由:持续超过48小时的顽固性呃逆很少见,但会显著影响生活质量。常规疗法往往因疗效短暂、副作用大、侵入性强而受限,凸显了寻找更安全有效治疗方案的必要性。患者关注点:一名62岁男性,患有慢性乙型肝炎、高血压、2型糖尿病、既往肺结核病史及胃肠道疾病,出现发热7天,顽固性呃逆4天,频率为每分钟40至50次。症状显著干扰了进食、睡眠及日常活动。诊断:排除中枢神经系统及代谢原因后,患者被诊断为与肺部感染及胃肠道功能障碍相关的顽固性呃逆。干预措施:初始超声引导膈神经阻滞提供约4小时的短暂缓解。随后,连续3天给予每日膈神经阻滞联合每日两次经皮耳廓迷走神经刺激(aVNS)的联合治疗。结果:至第7天,呃逆完全缓解,呃逆评估量表评分从9降至0,匹兹堡睡眠质量指数评分从16改善至2。启动aVNS后,患者出院后随访1个月。随访期间未观察到顽固性呃逆复发、延迟并发症或治疗相关不良事件。经验:本病例提示,超声引导膈神经阻滞联合aVNS可能为对常规治疗无反应的顽固性呃逆提供一种微创、安全且有效的治疗选择。有必要开展更大规模的研究以验证疗效和长期结果。

问题与背景
理由:持续超过 48 小时的持续打嗝很少见,但会严重影响生活质量。
设计与对象
患者疑虑:一名62岁男性,患有慢性乙型肝炎、高血压、2型糖尿病、既往肺结核和胃肠道疾病,发烧7天,持续打嗝4天,频率为每分钟40至50次。
核心方法
所提供的证据没有明确说明核心实验、分析或统计方法。
主要结果
随访期间未观察到持续性打嗝、迟发性并发症或治疗相关不良事件的复发。
解释与创新
开始aVNS后,患者出院后随访1个月。
科研与公卫意义
经验教训:该病例表明,超声引导下膈神经阻滞联合 aVNS 可能为对传统治疗无反应的持续性呃逆提供微创、安全、有效的治疗选择。
局限与证据强度
传统疗法往往受到短暂疗效、副作用、侵袭性的限制,这凸显了对更安全、更有效的选择的需求。

Combined percutaneous auricular vagus nerve stimulation and ultrasound-guided phrenic nerve block for persistent hiccups: A case report.

Authors: Qinyue Luo, Guipeng Wu, Chuangang Shi, Zongfeng Guo, Jin Wang, Ran Wang, Xiaoqing Xu, Yi-Xiang Wang
Original Abstract
Rationale: Persistent hiccups lasting more than 48 hours are rare but can markedly impair quality of life. Conventional therapies are often limited by transient efficacy, side effects, invasiveness, highlighting the need for safer and more effective options. Patient concerns: A 62-year-old male with chronic hepatitis B, hypertension, type 2 diabetes, prior pulmonary tuberculosis, and gastrointestinal disorders presented with fever for 7 days and persistent hiccups for 4 days, occurring at a frequency of 40 to 50 times per minute. Symptoms significantly interfered with eating, sleeping, and daily activities. Diagnoses: After excluding central nervous system and metabolic causes, the patient was diagnosed with persistent hiccups associated with pulmonary infection and gastrointestinal dysfunction. Interventions: An initial ultrasound-guided phrenic nerve block provided transient relief for about 4 hours. Subsequently, combined therapy with daily phrenic nerve block and twice-daily percutaneous auricular vagus nerve stimulation (aVNS) was administered for 3 consecutive days. Outcomes: By day 7, the hiccups had resolved completely, with the hiccups assessment instrument score decreasing from 9 to 0 and the Pittsburgh sleep quality index score improving from 16 to 2. After initiation of aVNS, the patient was followed up for 1 month after discharge. No recurrence of persistent hiccups, delayed complications, or treatment-related adverse events were observed during follow-up. Lessons: This case suggests that ultrasound-guided phrenic nerve block combined with aVNS may offer a minimally invasive, safe, and effective treatment option for persistent hiccups unresponsive to conventional therapy. Larger studies are warranted to validate efficacy and long-term outcomes.

开发用于同时检测鸭乙型肝炎病毒、鸭腺病毒 3 型和溶没食子链球菌亚种的三重 TaqMan 实时 PCR 检测。巴氏杆菌

Development of a Triplex TaqMan Real-Time PCR Assay for Simultaneous Detection of Duck Hepatitis B Virus, Duck Adenovirus Type 3, and Streptococcus gallolyticus subsp. pasteurianus
作者： Mingfa Yang, Wei Yu, Xiaofang Chen, Wei Zhang, 孟庆文, Changwen Li, Jiasen Liu, Changyou Xia, Qingwen Meng
摘要中文翻译鸭乙型肝炎病毒 (DHBV)、鸭腺病毒 3 (DAdV-3) 和溶没食子链球菌亚种。巴氏杆菌 (SGSP) 是与水禽传染病相关的三种不同病原体。由于症状重叠,这些药物之间的临床区分通常很困难,这凸显了需要准确、快速和同步的诊断工具来支持早期检测和有效的疾病监测。在本研究中,我们开发并验证了三重 TaqMan 实时定量 PCR (qPCR) 测定法,用于同时检测和定量 DHBV、DAdV-3 和 SGSP。该测定显示出高分析灵敏度,每个目标的检测限达到 10 拷贝/μL,所有标准曲线均显示出出色的线性(R2 = 0.999)。特异性评估表明,三重 qPCR 检测对三种目标病原体具有高度特异性,因为它没有与其他检查的病原体产生任何交叉反应信号。重组质粒标准品的测定内和测定间变异系数始终<2%,表明高重现性。同时,采用该方法对鸭体内三种病原体的流行病学进行了调查。DHBV、DAdV-3和SGSP的阳性率分别为73.95%(159/215)、11.16%(24/215)和37.21%(80/215)。DHBV和DAdV-3的共感染率为5.58%(12/215),DHBV和SGSP的共感染率为27.91%(60/215),DAdV-3和SGSP的共感染率为2.33%(5/215),三种病原体的共感染率为2.33%(5/215)。总之,该三重 qPCR 检测显示出优异的特异性、敏感性和重复性,可作为当地采样鸭群中三种目标病原体的常规临床诊断、流行病学监测和共感染筛查的可靠检测工具。

Development of a Triplex TaqMan Real-Time PCR Assay for Simultaneous Detection of Duck Hepatitis B Virus, Duck Adenovirus Type 3, and Streptococcus gallolyticus subsp. pasteurianus

Authors: Mingfa Yang, Wei Yu, Xiaofang Chen, Wei Zhang, 孟庆文, Changwen Li, Jiasen Liu, Changyou Xia, Qingwen Meng
Original Abstract
Duck hepatitis B virus (DHBV), Duck adenovirus 3 (DAdV-3) and Streptococcus gallolyticus subsp. pasteurianus (SGSP) are three distinct pathogens associated with infectious diseases in waterfowl. Clinical differentiation among these agents is often difficult due to overlapping signs, underscoring the need for accurate, rapid, and simultaneous diagnostic tools to support early detection and effective disease surveillance. In this study, we developed and validated a triplex TaqMan real-time quantitative PCR (qPCR) assay for the simultaneous detection and quantification of DHBV, DAdV-3, and SGSP. The assay indicated high analytical sensitivity, achieving detection limits of 10 copies/μL for each target, with all standard curves showing excellent linearity (R2 = 0.999). The evaluation of specificity showed that the triplex qPCR assay had high specificity for the three target pathogens, as it did not yield any cross-reactive signals with other pathogens examined. Intra-assay and inter-assay coefficients of variation for recombinant plasmid standards were consistently <2%, indicating high reproducibility. Meanwhile, the assay was employed to investigate the epidemiology of the three pathogens in ducks. The positive rates for DHBV, DAdV-3 and SGSP were 73.95% (159/215), 11.16% (24/215) and 37.21% (80/215), respectively. The co-infection rates were 5.58% (12/215) for DHBV and DAdV-3, 27.91% (60/215) for DHBV and SGSP, 2.33% (5/215) for DAdV-3 and SGSP, and 2.33% (5/215) for all three pathogens. In summary, this triplex qPCR assay displays excellent specificity, sensitivity and repeatability, which may serve as a reliable testing tool for routine clinical diagnosis, epidemiological monitoring and co-infection screening of the three target pathogens in locally sampled duck populations.

pasteurianus (SGSP) are three distinct pathogens associated with infectious diseases in waterfowl.
Design and population

Core methods

Main results
The evaluation of specificity showed that the triplex qPCR assay had high specificity for the three target pathogens, as it did not yield any cross-reactive signals with other pathogens examined.
Interpretation and novelty
The positive rates for DHBV, DAdV-3 and SGSP were 73.95% (159/215), 11.16% (24/215) and 37.21% (80/215), respectively.

Limitations and evidence strength

Letter to the Editor: Origin recognition complex subunit 1 as a biomarker in hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma: Promises and pitfalls

Authors: Lian-Bang Wang, Xi-Ming Wang, Xin-Ya Zhao, Gong-Zheng Wang
Original Abstract
This commentary discusses the recent study by Feng et al published in World Journal of Gastroenterology . Serum origin recognition complex subunit 1 (ORC1) was identified as a potential biomarker for hepatitis B virus-related hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC). The study reported improved diagnostic accuracy when ORC1 was combined with extra spindle pole bodies-like 1 and alpha-fetoprotein, providing an opportunity to contextualize ORC1 within existing multi-marker biomarker strategies for hepatitis B virus-related HCC surveillance. These observations, along with the limitations of the study, are briefly discussed. Several suggestions have also been made regarding prospective validation and the incorporation of ORC1 into broader multi-marker surveillance strategies.

Gambaran Pemeriksaan Hasil HBsAg pada Ibu Hamil 三个月期 Ii dalam Deteksi 乙型肝炎 di Banyumas

Gambaran Pemeriksaan Hasil HBsAg pada Ibu Hamil Trimester Ii dalam Deteksi Hepatitis B di Banyumas
作者： Auliya Khairin, Ratna Wulandari, Anisa Nur Afdila, Shofia Putri Kawiswara, Cahya Putri Utami, Fatra Guhanda, Fu’ad Minan Zuhri
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎是一种病毒感染,仍然是一个公共健康问题,特别是在孕妇中,因为存在感染垂直传播给胎儿的风险。孕妇进行乙型肝炎表面抗原 (HBsAg) 检查对于早期发现预防乙型肝炎从母亲传染给婴儿来说是必要的。本研究旨在描述Banyumas地区妊娠中期孕妇HBsAg检查在乙型肝炎检测中的结果。所使用的研究类型是采用横断面方法的描述性观察。该人群由中期孕妇组成,样本中有 23 名符合纳入和排除标准的受访者。采集静脉血样本并使用 HBsAg 免疫层析法(试纸条测试)进行检查。对获得的数据进行描述性定性分析,以描述 HBsAg 检查的结果。在检查的样本中没有发现妊娠中期孕妇的 HBsAg 结果呈阳性。结果显示,所有受访者 HBsAg 检测结果均为阴性(100%)。由此可见,孕妇未检测到活动性乙型肝炎病毒感染。采用试纸条法进行HBsAg检查可作为孕妇早期发现乙型肝炎的初步筛查方法。这项研究表明,仍然建议在常规 HBsAg 检查中使用试纸条测试,以防止乙型肝炎从母婴传播
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
乙型肝炎仍然是一个公共卫生问题,特别是在孕妇中,因为它有垂直传播给胎儿的风险,因此需要尽早发现,以防止母婴传播。该研究旨在描述巴纽马斯妊娠中期孕妇的 HBsAg 检查结果。
设计与对象
这项采用横断面方法的描述性观察研究针对巴纽马斯的妊娠中期孕妇进行,样本包括 23 名符合纳入和排除标准的受访者。
核心方法
采集静脉血样本并使用 HBsAg 免疫层析法(试纸条测试)进行检查。对数据进行描述性和定性分析,以描述 HBsAg 检查结果。
主要结果
23名受访者的HBsAg检测结果全部为阴性(100%),检查样本中没有发现妊娠中期孕妇的HBsAg结果为阳性。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,在孕妇中未检测到活动性乙型肝炎病毒感染,该研究证明了试纸条检测在常规 HBsAg 筛查中预防传播的效用。
科研与公卫意义
HBsAg检测可作为孕妇早期发现乙型肝炎的初步筛查方法,仍建议在常规检查中使用,以防止母婴传播。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小(n = 23)限制了普遍性,并且没有对照组的描述性设计限制了因果推断。由于这些设计限制,证据强度中等。

Gambaran Pemeriksaan Hasil HBsAg pada Ibu Hamil Trimester Ii dalam Deteksi Hepatitis B di Banyumas

Authors: Auliya Khairin, Ratna Wulandari, Anisa Nur Afdila, Shofia Putri Kawiswara, Cahya Putri Utami, Fatra Guhanda, Fu’ad Minan Zuhri
Original Abstract
Hepatitis B is a viral infection that remains a public health problem, especially in pregnant women due to the risk of vertical transmission of infection to the fetus. Hepatitis B Surface Antigen (HBsAg) examination in pregnant women is necessary as an early detection effort to prevent hepatitis B transmission from mother to baby. This study aims to describe the results of HBsAg examination in second-trimester pregnant women in the detection of hepatitis B in Banyumas. The type of research used is descriptive observational with a cross-sectional approach. The population consisted of secondtrimester pregnant women with a sample of 23 respondents who met the inclusion and exclusion criteria. Venous blood samples were taken and examined using the HBsAg immunochromatography method (strip test). The data obtained were analyzed descriptively qualitatively to describe the results of the HBsAg examination. No second-trimester pregnant women were found with positive HBsAg results in the samples examined. The results showed that all respondents had negative HBsAg test results (100%). It can be concluded that no active hepatitis B virus infection was detected in pregnant women. HBsAg examination using the strip test method can be used as an initial screening method for early detection of hepatitis B in pregnant women. This study shows that the use of strip tests is still recommended for routine HBsAg examinations to prevent transmission of hepatitis B from mother to baby
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Hepatitis B remains a public health problem, particularly in pregnant women due to the risk of vertical transmission to the fetus, necessitating early detection efforts to prevent mother-to-baby transmission. The study aimed to describe the results of HBsAg examination in second-trimester pregnant women in Banyumas.
Design and population
This descriptive observational study with a cross-sectional approach was conducted on second-trimester pregnant women in Banyumas, with a sample of 23 respondents who met the inclusion and exclusion criteria.
Core methods
Venous blood samples were collected and examined using the HBsAg immunochromatography method (strip test). The data were analyzed descriptively and qualitatively to describe the HBsAg examination results.
Main results
All 23 respondents had negative HBsAg test results (100%), and no second-trimester pregnant women were found with positive HBsAg results in the samples examined.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that no active hepatitis B virus infection was detected in the pregnant women, and the study demonstrated the utility of strip tests for routine HBsAg screening to prevent transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
HBsAg examination using the strip test method can be used as an initial screening method for early detection of hepatitis B in pregnant women, and its use is still recommended for routine examinations to prevent mother-to-baby transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's small sample size (n=23) limits generalizability, and the descriptive design without a control group restricts causal inference. The evidence strength is moderate due to these design limitations.

肝细胞癌合并乙型肝炎相关性肝硬化患者肝切除术的安全性评估:专家共识

Safety assessment for hepatectomy in patients with hepatocellular carcinoma combined with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis: Expert consensus
作者： Zhiyong Huang
摘要中文翻译在我国,80%以上的肝细胞癌(HCC)患者因乙型肝炎病毒感染而出现不同程度的肝硬化。与西方国家不同,肝切除术仍然是中国肝癌和肝硬化患者的主要治疗选择之一。肝细胞癌患者的肝硬化严重程度差异很大。尽管在代偿阶段肝功能可能保持在正常范围内,但进行性肝硬化会显着降低肝功能储备和再生能力。因此,肝切除术后术后并发症和肝功能衰竭的发生率增加。因此,对于不同程度的肝硬化患者,需要尽可能保留肝实质的肝切除术以确保手术安全。目前,不同程度的肝硬化对HCC患者肝切除术后肝衰竭风险的影响尚不完全清楚。此外,对于 HCC 和不同严重程度的肝硬化患者的最佳手术方法尚未达成共识。这一差距是影响肝切除术安全性的重要因素。降低肝癌和肝硬化患者术后肝衰竭的发生率和死亡率仍然是需要进一步研究的重要问题。国际肝胰胆协会中国分会、中华外科学会肝脏外科学组邀请国内肝胆外科和肝病学领域顶尖专家,共同探讨肝硬化严重程度在肝癌肝切除安全性评估中的作用以及提高手术安全性的策略。该共识旨在协助肝胆外科医生对HCC合并肝硬化患者做出适当的手术决策,并提高手术安全性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
目前,不同程度的肝硬化对HCC患者肝切除术后肝衰竭风险的影响尚不完全清楚。
设计与对象
在我国,80%以上的肝细胞癌(HCC)患者因乙型肝炎病毒感染而出现不同程度的肝硬化。
核心方法

Safety assessment for hepatectomy in patients with hepatocellular carcinoma combined with hepatitis B-related cirrhosis: Expert consensus

Authors: Zhiyong Huang
Original Abstract
In China, more than 80% of patients with hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) have varying degrees of cirrhosis caused by hepatitis B virus infection. Unlike in Western countries, hepatectomy remains one of the main treatment options for patients with HCC and cirrhosis in China. The severity of cirrhosis varies considerably among individual patients with HCC. Although liver function may remain within the normal range during the compensated stage, progressive cirrhosis significantly reduces hepatic functional reserve and regenerative capacity. As a result, the incidence of postoperative complications and liver failure increases after hepatectomy. Therefore, hepatectomy that preserves as much liver parenchyma as possible is required to ensure surgical safety in patients with different degrees of cirrhosis. At present, the impact of different degrees of cirrhosis on the risk of posthepatectomy liver failure in patients with HCC is not fully understood. In addition, no consensus has been established regarding the optimal surgical approach for patients with HCC and varying severities of cirrhosis. This gap represents an important factor affecting the safety of hepatectomy. Reducing the incidence of postoperative liver failure and mortality in patients with HCC and cirrhosis remains an important issue that requires further investigation. The Chinese Chapter of the International Hepato-Pancreato-Biliary Association, Group of Liver Surgery of the Chinese Society of Surgery, invited leading domestic experts in hepatobiliary surgery and hepatology to discuss the role of cirrhosis severity in the safety assessment of hepatectomy for HCC and strategies to improve surgical safety. This consensus aims to assist hepatobiliary surgeons in making appropriate surgical decisions for patients with HCC and cirrhosis, and to improve surgical safety.

新型 HLA I 类限制性乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 肽的鉴定及其肽编辑器 TAPBPR 的调节

Identification of novel HLA class I-restricted hepatitis B virus (HBV) peptides and their modulation by peptide editor TAPBPR
作者： Ricky Sinharay, Dominic Nolan, Jens Bauer, Andreas Neerincx, Juliane Walz, Will Gelson, Arwen Altenburg, Louise Boyle
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)是肝细胞癌的主要原因,感染仍然是一个重大的全球健康问题,目前尚无治疗方法。由于细胞毒性 CD8+ T 淋巴细胞是自然 HBV 消退的主要效应器,因此人们对鉴定用于免疫识别的 HBV 表位有很大兴趣。目前对 HBV 衍生肽呈递至 CD8+ T 细胞的了解主要是人类白细胞抗原 (HLA)-A*02:01。我们的目的是表征 HLA-B 和 HLA-C 分子上呈现的新型 HBV 衍生肽,特别是在 HBV 感染率高的地区普遍存在的同种异型。为了发现新的 HBV 衍生肽,对表达单个 HLA I 类分子的细胞进行了免疫肽组学分析,这些分子被转导以表达单个 HBV 蛋白。肽结合测定用于验证所选肽的 HLA 结合。此外,我们使用免疫肽组学分析来确定最近发现的主要组织相容性复合物 I 类 (MHC-I) 肽编辑器 TAPBPR 对 HBV 衍生肽呈递的影响。使用 TAPBPR 肽交换测定验证结果。我们鉴定了 28 种呈递于 HLA I 类的新型 HBV 衍生肽。重要的是,在表达 HLA-B*08:01、-B*15:03、-B*35:01、-C*06:02 和 -C*12:03 的细胞上鉴定了新发现的 22 种 HBV 衍生肽。这项工作对免疫表位数据库 (IEDB) 中描述的 HLA-B 和 HLA-C 限制性 HBV 衍生肽进行了实质性扩展。此外,TAPBPRKO HeLa 细胞呈现出四种额外的新型 HBV 衍生肽,这首次表明 TAPBPR 不仅塑造细胞,而且塑造 MHC-I 分子上呈现的病毒肽库。

Identification of novel HLA class I-restricted hepatitis B virus (HBV) peptides and their modulation by peptide editor TAPBPR

Authors: Ricky Sinharay, Dominic Nolan, Jens Bauer, Andreas Neerincx, Juliane Walz, Will Gelson, Arwen Altenburg, Louise Boyle
Original Abstract
Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is the leading cause of hepatocellular carcinoma and infections remain a substantial global health problem for which no curative therapy exists. As cytotoxic CD8+ T lymphocytes are the principal effectors of natural HBV resolution, there is considerable interest in identification of HBV epitopes that are presented for immune recognition. Current knowledge of HBV-derived peptide presentation to CD8+ T cells is largely dominated human leukocyte antigen (HLA)-A*02:01. Our aim was to characterise novel HBV-derived peptides presented on HLA-B and -C molecules, particularly allotypes that are prevalent in regions with high HBV infection rates. To discover new HBV-derived peptides, immunopeptidomic analysis was performed on cells expressing single HLA class I molecules transduced to express individual HBV proteins. Peptide binding assays were used to validate HLA binding of selected peptides. Additionally, we used immunopeptidomic analysis to determine the effect of the recently discovered major histocompatibility complex class I (MHC-I) peptide editor TAPBPR on presentation of HBV-derived peptides. The results were validated using TAPBPR peptide-exchange assays. We identified 28 novel HBV-derived peptides presented on HLA class I. Importantly, 22 of the newly discovered HBV-derived peptides were identified on cells expressing HLA-B*08:01, -B*15:03, -B*35:01, -C*06:02 and -C*12:03. This work presents a substantial expansion of the HLA-B and HLA-C-restricted HBV-derived peptides described in the Immune Epitope Database (IEDB). Additionally, TAPBPRKO HeLa cells presented four additional and novel HBV-derived peptides, representing the first indication that TAPBPR shapes not only the cellular but also the viral peptide repertoire presented on MHC-I molecules.

Hepatitis B virus (HBV) is the leading cause of hepatocellular carcinoma and infections remain a substantial global health problem for which no curative therapy exists.

Core methods
Additionally, we used immunopeptidomic analysis to determine the effect of the recently discovered major histocompatibility complex class I (MHC-I) peptide editor TAPBPR on presentation of HBV-derived peptides.
Main results
We identified 28 novel HBV-derived peptides presented on HLA class I.
Interpretation and novelty
Additionally, TAPBPRKO HeLa cells presented four additional and novel HBV-derived peptides, representing the first indication that TAPBPR shapes not only the cellular but also the viral peptide repertoire presented on MHC-I molecules.
Scientific and public-health significance
Our aim was to characterise novel HBV-derived peptides presented on HLA-B and -C molecules, particularly allotypes that are prevalent in regions with high HBV infection rates.
Limitations and evidence strength

基于 UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS 的麦冬生物活性成分甲基麦冬酮 A 的代谢物鉴定和分析。

UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS-based metabolite identification and profiling of methylophiopogonone A, A bioactive constituent from Ophiopogon japonicus.
作者： Yujie Zhu, Peng Zhang, Mengying Cao, Zheheng Ma
摘要中文翻译甲基麦冬酮 A (MON-A) 是麦冬的一种抗炎成分,具有治疗慢性乙型肝炎和 SARS-CoV-2 感染患者肝纤维化的潜力。本研究旨在利用人类和三种临床前物种(大鼠、狗和猴子)的肝微粒体和冷冻保存的肝细胞来研究其代谢特征。MON-A 与每个系统分别孵育,并通过超高效液相色谱结合四极杆/Orbitrap 高分辨率质谱 (UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS) 在正电喷雾电离模式下对样品进行分析。使用具有质量缺陷过滤功能的Compound Discoverer 软件处理数据。根据精确质量和 MS2 碎片离子对代谢物结构进行表征。总共鉴定出 14 种代谢物。主要代谢途径包括羟基化、氧化亚甲基二氧苯基(MDP)开环、氧化和葡萄糖醛酸化。M11(一种葡萄糖醛酸结合物)是所有物种中最丰富的代谢物。此外,还检测到四种谷胱甘肽(GSH)缀合物,表明形成了反应性代谢物,特别是邻醌和醌甲基化物。醌甲基化物的形成表明C2-C3双键对于A环的代谢活化至关重要。这项系统研究阐明了 MON-A 的代谢命运,为其生物转化途径和反应性代谢物形成的潜力提供了重要见解。结果为评估这种抗炎候选药物在临床前开发过程中的治疗效果和安全性奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
甲基麦冬酮 A (MON-A) 是麦冬的一种抗炎成分,据报道对慢性乙型肝炎和 SARS-CoV-2 感染的肝纤维化具有治疗潜力,但其代谢命运和安全性尚不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究使用人类和三种临床前物种(大鼠、狗和猴子)的肝微粒体和冷冻保存的肝细胞,通过超高效液相色谱结合四极杆/Orbitrap 高分辨率质谱 (UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS) 研究了 MON-A 的代谢特征。
核心方法
MON-A 与每个系统分别孵育,并使用 UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS 在正电喷雾电离模式下分析样品,数据由Compound Discoverer 软件处理,并根据精确质量和 MS2 碎片离子表征代谢物结构。
主要结果
共鉴定出 14 种代谢物,主要代谢途径包括羟基化、氧化亚甲基二氧苯基 (MDP) 开环、氧化和葡萄糖醛酸化,其中 M11(葡萄糖醛酸结合物)是所有物种中最丰富的代谢物。
解释与创新
该研究阐明了 MON-A 的代谢命运,揭示了其生物转化途径和反应性代谢物形成的潜力,包括四种谷胱甘肽缀合物和醌甲基化物种类,这为评估其治疗功效和安全性奠定了基础。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为 MON-A 的代谢激活和解毒提供了重要的见解,为其作为抗炎候选药物的临床前开发提供了信息,并指导未来的安全性评估。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于体外系统,不涉及体内药代动力学、毒性或临床相关性,需要谨慎解释和进一步研究。

UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS-based metabolite identification and profiling of methylophiopogonone A, A bioactive constituent from Ophiopogon japonicus.

Authors: Yujie Zhu, Peng Zhang, Mengying Cao, Zheheng Ma
Original Abstract
Methylophiopogonone A (MON-A), an anti-inflammatory constituent of Ophiopogon japonicus, demonstrates therapeutic potential for liver fibrosis in patients with chronic hepatitis B and SARS-CoV-2 infection. This study aimed to investigate its metabolic profile using liver microsomes and cryopreserved hepatocytes from humans and three preclinical species (rat, dog, and monkey). MON-A was incubated separately with each system, and the samples were analyzed by ultra-high-performance liquid chromatography coupled with quadrupole/Orbitrap high-resolution mass spectrometry (UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS) in positive electrospray ionization mode. Data were processed using Compound Discoverer software with mass defect filtering. Metabolite structures were characterized based on accurate mass and MS2 fragment ions. A total of 14 metabolites were identified. The primary metabolic pathways included hydroxylation, oxidative methylenedioxyphenyl (MDP) ring-opening, oxidation, and glucuronidation. M11 (a glucuronide conjugate) was the most abundant metabolite across all species. Additionally, four glutathione (GSH) conjugates were detected, indicating the formation of reactive metabolites, notably ortho-quinone and quinone methide species. The formation of quinone methide species revealed that C2-C3 double bond is essential for the metabolic activation of the A-ring. This systematic study clarifies the metabolic fate of MON-A, providing important insights into its biotransformation pathways and potential for reactive metabolite formation. The results establish a foundation for evaluating both the therapeutic efficacy and safety profile of this anti-inflammatory candidate during preclinical development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Methylophiopogonone A (MON-A) is an anti-inflammatory constituent of Ophiopogon japonicus with reported therapeutic potential for liver fibrosis in chronic hepatitis B and SARS-CoV-2 infection, but its metabolic fate and safety profile were not well understood.
Design and population
The study investigated the metabolic profile of MON-A using liver microsomes and cryopreserved hepatocytes from humans and three preclinical species (rat, dog, and monkey) with ultra-high-performance liquid chromatography coupled with quadrupole/Orbitrap high-resolution mass spectrometry (UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS).
Core methods
MON-A was incubated separately with each system, and samples were analyzed in positive electrospray ionization mode using UHPLC-Orbitrap-HRMS, with data processed by Compound Discoverer software and metabolite structures characterized based on accurate mass and MS2 fragment ions.
Main results
A total of 14 metabolites were identified, with the primary metabolic pathways including hydroxylation, oxidative methylenedioxyphenyl (MDP) ring-opening, oxidation, and glucuronidation, and M11 (a glucuronide conjugate) was the most abundant metabolite across all species.
Interpretation and novelty
The study clarifies the metabolic fate of MON-A, revealing its biotransformation pathways and potential for reactive metabolite formation, including four glutathione conjugates and quinone methide species, which establishes a foundation for evaluating its therapeutic efficacy and safety profile.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide critical insights into the metabolic activation and detoxification of MON-A, informing its preclinical development as an anti-inflammatory candidate and guiding future safety assessments.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to in vitro systems and does not address in vivo pharmacokinetics, toxicity, or clinical relevance, warranting cautious interpretation and further investigation.

抗 HBsAg 抗体 mAb19-LS 增强慢性乙型肝炎患者的抗病毒免疫力

Anti-HBsAg antibody mAb19-LS enhances antiviral immunity in humans with chronic hepatitis B
作者： Wang, Z., Tenuta, M., Ngoc Le, H., Halling Scensgaard, S. N., Silva Santos, G. S., Reeves, D. B., Breton, G., Igbokwe, V., Moraes Nicola, A., Millard, K.
摘要中文翻译乙型肝炎病毒(HBV)慢性感染的特点是乙型肝炎表面抗原(HBsAg)持续表达,这与深度免疫耐受相关。尽管核(酸)类似物疗法可以有效抑制病毒复制,但它既不能消除乙型肝炎病毒,也不能逆转病毒特异性免疫功能障碍。在这里,我们报告了两项平行的首次人体剂量递增研究的结果,该研究评估了接受核苷(酸)类似物治疗的慢性 HBV 感染个体单次输注 mAb19-LS(一种针对 HBsAg 的长效 IgG1 单克隆抗体)。mAb19-LS 通常是安全的且耐受性良好,并且可使抗原清除率平均增加 11 倍。HBsAg 抑制的程度和持续时间取决于基线抗原水平和 mAb19-LS 剂量,在接受最高剂量的个体中,抑制维持超过 36 周。循环 HBsAg 的减少与单核细胞和树突状细胞摄取 HBsAg-IgG 免疫复合物以及这些抗原呈递细胞的炎症重编程有关。值得注意的是,输注 24 周后,CD4+ 和 CD8+ T 细胞的增殖,以及响应 HBV 抗原而产生的干扰素-{gamma} 和 TNF- 产量均显着增加。总之,这些发现表明 mAb19-LS 通常是安全的,并且可以有效加速 HBsAg 清除,同时激活抗原呈递细胞并增强抗病毒 T 细胞反应。

Anti-HBsAg antibody mAb19-LS enhances antiviral immunity in humans with chronic hepatitis B

Authors: Wang, Z., Tenuta, M., Ngoc Le, H., Halling Scensgaard, S. N., Silva Santos, G. S., Reeves, D. B., Breton, G., Igbokwe, V., Moraes Nicola, A., Millard, K.
Original Abstract
Chronic infection with hepatitis B virus (HBV) is characterized by persistent expression of hepatitis B surface antigen (HBsAg), which is associated with profound immune tolerance. Although nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy effectively suppresses viral replication, it neither eliminates HBV nor reverses virus-specific immune dysfunction. Here, we report the results of two parallel first-in-human, dose-escalation studies evaluating a single infusion of mAb19-LS, a long-acting IgG1 monoclonal antibody targeting HBsAg, in individuals with chronic HBV infection receiving nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy. mAb19-LS was generally safe and well tolerated and induced a mean 11-fold increase in antigen clearance. The magnitude and duration of HBsAg suppression were dependent on both baseline antigen levels and mAb19-LS dose, with suppression maintained for more than 36 weeks in individuals receiving the highest dose. Reduction of circulating HBsAg was associated with uptake of HBsAg-IgG immune complexes by monocytes and dendritic cells and inflammatory reprogramming of these antigen-presenting cells. Notably, proliferation of both CD4+ and CD8+ T cells, as well as interferon-{gamma} and TNF- production in response to HBV antigens, were significantly increased 24 weeks after infusion. Together, these findings demonstrate that mAb19-LS is generally safe and effectively accelerates HBsAg clearance while activating antigen presenting cells and enhancing antiviral T cell responses.

Although nucleos(t)ide analogue therapy effectively suppresses viral replication, it neither eliminates HBV nor reverses virus-specific immune dysfunction.
Design and population
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the study design, setting, or study population.
Core methods
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the core experimental, analytical, or statistical methods.
Main results
Notably, proliferation of both CD4+ and CD8+ T cells, as well as interferon-{gamma} and TNF- production in response to HBV antigens, were significantly increased 24 weeks after infusion.
Interpretation and novelty
Reduction of circulating HBsAg was associated with uptake of HBsAg-IgG immune complexes by monocytes and dendritic cells and inflammatory reprogramming of these antigen-presenting cells.
Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

荷兰乙型肝炎、丙型肝炎和 HIV 血清阳性率的趋势:二十年全国 PIENTER 血清调查的见解。

Trends in Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and HIV Seroprevalence in the Netherlands: Insights From Two Decades of Nationwide PIENTER Serosurveys.
作者： Christine G. J. I. van Straten, Birgit H. B. van Benthem, Fiona R. M. van der Klis, Gaby Smits, Eric R. A. Vos, Tom Woudenberg, Marc van der Valk, Eline L. M. Op de Coul, Smits Gm, van Straten CGJI
摘要中文翻译尽管有有效的治疗和预防措施,乙型肝炎 (HBV)、丙型肝炎 (HCV) 和艾滋病毒仍然是公共卫生挑战。感染通常无症状,导致未确诊病例、进一步传播以及可预防的发病率和死亡率。在荷兰,疾病负担集中在特定人群,有许多感染未被发现。我们使用来自三项全国性横断面血清调查(PIENTER:1995-1996、2006-2007 和 2016-2017)的 14,444 名 15-79 岁参与者的数据估计了 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率,确定了决定因素并评估了 20 年来的趋势。参与者完成了有关人口统计、行为和社会经济因素的调查问卷,并在 2017 年对所有轮次的血清样本进行了 HBV 和 HCV 检测以及 HIV 检测。血清阳性率估计值根据荷兰人群进行加权,使用加权泊松回归分析趋势,并使用多变量泊松回归评估决定因素。加权 HBV 血清阳性率在各项调查中保持稳定(3%-5% 过去或现在的感染;< 0.4% 活动性感染)。第一代流动人口中乙型肝炎病毒感染率最高,年龄、流动背景和性传播感染史是关键决定因素。HCV 血清流行率仍然较低(< 0.4%),并且在非西方移民中最高。艾滋病毒感染率为 0.1%,在非西方移民和男男性行为者中感染率最高。只有 9% 的活动性 HBV 病例知道自己的状况。总体而言,荷兰的 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率仍然较低,但集中在认识有限的特定群体。这些发现支持有针对性的预防、筛查和与护理挂钩的战略,以实现世卫组织 2030 年消除目标,并强调持续进行基于人群的(血清)监测以指导公共卫生政策的重要性。

感染通常无症状,导致未确诊病例、进一步传播以及可预防的发病率和死亡率。
证据基础与方法
参与者完成了有关人口统计、行为和社会经济因素的调查问卷,并在 2017 年对所有轮次的血清样本进行了 HBV 和 HCV 检测以及 HIV 检测。
共识与结论
第一代流动人口中乙型肝炎病毒感染率最高,年龄、流动背景和性传播感染史是关键决定因素。
争议与缺口
总体而言,荷兰的 HBV、HCV 和 HIV 血清流行率仍然较低,但集中在认识有限的特定群体。

Trends in Hepatitis B, Hepatitis C, and HIV Seroprevalence in the Netherlands: Insights From Two Decades of Nationwide PIENTER Serosurveys.

Authors: Christine G. J. I. van Straten, Birgit H. B. van Benthem, Fiona R. M. van der Klis, Gaby Smits, Eric R. A. Vos, Tom Woudenberg, Marc van der Valk, Eline L. M. Op de Coul, Smits Gm, van Straten CGJI
Original Abstract
Hepatitis B (HBV), hepatitis C (HCV), and HIV remain public health challenges despite effective treatments and preventive options. Infections are often asymptomatic, leading to undiagnosed cases, onward transmission, and preventable morbidity and mortality. In the Netherlands, disease burden is concentrated in specific populations, with many undetected infections. We estimated HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence, identified determinants, and assessed trends over 20 years using data from 14,444 participants aged 15-79 years from three nationwide cross-sectional serosurveys (PIENTER: 1995-1996, 2006-2007, and 2016-2017). Participants completed questionnaires on demographics, behaviour, and socio-economic factors, and serum samples were tested for HBV and HCV in all rounds and HIV in 2017. Seroprevalence estimates were weighted to the Dutch population, trends were analysed using weighted Poisson regression, and determinants were evaluated using multivariable Poisson regression. Weighted HBV seroprevalence remained stable across surveys (3%-5% past or present infection; < 0.4% active infection). HBV prevalence was highest among first-generation migrants, with older age, migration background, and STI history as key determinants. HCV seroprevalence remained low (< 0.4%) and was highest among non-Western migrants. HIV prevalence was 0.1% and highest among non-Western migrants and men who have sex with men. Only 9% of active HBV cases were aware of their status. Overall, HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence remain low in the Netherlands but are concentrated in specific groups with limited awareness. These findings support targeted prevention, screening, and linkage-to-care strategies to achieve the 2030 WHO elimination goals and highlight the importance of continued population-based (sero-)monitoring to guide public health policy.

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions
HBV prevalence was highest among first-generation migrants, with older age, migration background, and STI history as key determinants.
Controversies and gaps
Overall, HBV, HCV, and HIV seroprevalence remain low in the Netherlands but are concentrated in specific groups with limited awareness.
Research and practice implications
These findings support targeted prevention, screening, and linkage-to-care strategies to achieve the 2030 WHO elimination goals and highlight the importance of continued population-based (sero-)monitoring to guide public health policy.

回顾伯明翰乙型肝炎病毒 (HBV) 相关死亡率(2018-2023 年):晚期诊断的影响。

Reviewing hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related mortality in Birmingham (2018-2023): The impact of late diagnosis.
作者： S. Santharam, Sara Croxford, D. Mutimer, Sowsan Atabani, S. Degala, A. Elsharkawy, F. Khan, K.D. McKenzie, A. Parsloe, B. Pollard
摘要中文翻译目标:回顾伯明翰与乙型肝炎 (HBV) 相关的死亡情况,了解较高的 HBV 相关死亡率,了解死亡原因,并确定诊断和护理途径中需要改进的领域,以减少可预防的死亡。研究设计:加强监测——对死亡情况进行回顾性审查,并与国家统计局 (ONS) 死亡率数据库联系起来进行验证。方法:临床医生提交了伯明翰(2018-2023 年)在该市接受专科护理的成年人中发生的 HBV 相关死亡数据。死亡原因是从与国家统计局数据相关的医院记录中获得的。结果:提交了 44 名死于 HBV 相关原因的患者的数据;86%为男性。百分之八十不是英国出生的;70% 出生在乙型肝炎病毒中高流行率的国家。大多数人可能在早年感染乙肝病毒(83%)。英国诊断 HBV 的中位年龄为 52 岁(四分位距 (IQR):39-62 岁);72% 在诊断时患有肝硬化。最常见的诊断环境是二级护理(70%)。死亡中位年龄为 59 岁(IQR:46-72 岁);最常见的原因是肝癌(50%;n = 22)和终末期肝病(23%;n = 10)。结论:在伯明翰死于乙型肝炎相关原因的患者中,错过了早期诊断的机会,特别是在初级保健中。大多数死亡者是移民,可能在早年感染了乙型肝炎病毒,但大多数人是在二级保健机构被诊断为中年和晚期疾病。为了实现围绕乙型肝炎死亡率的公共卫生目标,必须改进检测的提供/采用以及与护理的联系,解决高危人群和医疗保健专业人员的障碍和认识。

共识与结论

Reviewing hepatitis B virus (HBV)-related mortality in Birmingham (2018-2023): The impact of late diagnosis.

Authors: S. Santharam, Sara Croxford, D. Mutimer, Sowsan Atabani, S. Degala, A. Elsharkawy, F. Khan, K.D. McKenzie, A. Parsloe, B. Pollard
Original Abstract
OBJECTIVES: Review hepatitis B (HBV)-related deaths in Birmingham to interrogate higher HBV-related mortality rates, understand causes of death and identify areas for improvement in diagnostic and care pathways to reduce preventable deaths. STUDY DESIGN: Enhanced surveillance - retrospective review of deaths, with linkage to Office for National Statistics (ONS) mortality database for validation. METHODS: Clinicians submitted data on HBV-related deaths occurring in Birmingham (2018-2023) among adults attending for specialist care in the city. Cause of death was obtained from hospital records linked to ONS data. RESULTS: Data were submitted on 44 people who died of HBV-related causes; 86% were male. Eighty percent were non-UK-born; 70% were born in countries with intermediate-high HBV prevalence. The majority likely acquired HBV in early life (83%). Median age at HBV diagnosis in the UK was 52 years (interquartile range (IQR): 39-62 years); 72% had cirrhosis at diagnosis. The most common diagnosis setting was secondary care (70%). Median age at death was 59 years (IQR: 46-72 years); the most common causes were liver cancer (50%; n = 22) and end-stage liver disease (23%; n = 10). CONCLUSIONS: Among those dying of HBV-related causes in Birmingham, there were missed opportunities for earlier diagnosis, particularly in primary care. The majority of people who died were migrants and probably acquired HBV in early life, yet most were diagnosed in secondary care, middle-aged and with advanced disease. To achieve public health targets around HBV mortality, testing offer/uptake and linkage to care must be improved, addressing barriers and awareness among at-risk groups and healthcare professionals.

Evidence base and method

Consensus and conclusions

Controversies and gaps

Research and practice implications
