B 常规重要

尽管总体覆盖率很高,但世卫组织和联合国儿童基金会质疑印度新生儿乙型肝炎疫苗数据

WHO, UNICEF question India's newborn hepatitis B vaccine data despite strong overall coverage

中文翻译暂不可用；以下中文显示位置使用已核验英文内容填充。

完整新闻中文摘要2026 年 7 月 15 日,世界卫生组织和联合国儿童基金会发布了 WUENIC 报告,质疑印度报告的 2025 年乙型肝炎出生剂量疫苗覆盖率达 92%,估计实际覆盖率仅为 70%。这些机构指出目标人群分母不一致,并将这种差异标记为“存在挑战”。虽然印度的整体儿童免疫接种率仍然很高(例如,DTP1 为 97%,麻疹为 98%),但乙型肝炎出生剂量差距使近 30% 的新生儿在 24 小时内得不到保护,增加了慢性感染风险。世卫组织和联合国儿童基金会将审查印度的 NFHS-6 调查结果以进行协调。
查看新闻五要素
时间
发布日期:2026-07-15。事件日期:2025(覆盖数据)。WHO-UNICEF WUENIC 报告于 2026-07-15 发布。
地点与对象
目标人群:出生24小时内的新生儿。
事件
作为 2026 年 WUENIC 报告的一部分,世卫组织和联合国儿童基金会对印度官方乙型肝炎出生剂量覆盖率估计(报告为 92%,机构估计为 70%)提出质疑。这些机构指出了覆盖范围分母和目标人口计算中的不一致之处。这是一项初步评估,有待对印度 NFHS-6 调查结果进行审查。
规模、影响与风险
India reported 92% hepatitis B birth-dose coverage in 2025, but WHO-UNICEF estimate suggests only 70% coverage, implying ~30% of newborns missed the vaccine within 24 hours. Chronic hepatitis B risk from mother-to-child transmission remains high for unvaccinated infants. Globally, 13.5 million children were 'zero-dose' in 2025, with 750,000 fewer than in 2024.
应对、状态与不确定性
世界卫生组织和联合国儿童基金会正在审查印度的 NFHS-6 调查结果以进行协调。印度其他疫苗的常规免疫接种率仍然很高(例如 DTP1 为 97%,麻疹疫苗为 98%)。由于对分母的担忧,这些机构已将乙型肝炎出生剂量估计标记为“有争议”。报告中没有提供印度卫生部的官方回应。
B routine priority

WHO, UNICEF question India's newborn hepatitis B vaccine data despite strong overall coverage

Fetched Original Body
WHO, UNICEF question India's newborn hepatitis B vaccine data despite strong overall coverage WHO and UNICEF have challenged India's official estimate for the hepatitis B birth dose given to newborns within 24 hours. The gap has raised concerns over coverage denominators even as India's routine childhood immunisation remains high. The World Health Organisation (WHO) and UNICEF have questioned India’s official estimates on newborn hepatitis B vaccination coverage, saying the country’s reported figures overstate the proportion of babies receiving the critical birth dose within 24 hours of delivery even as India continues to post high coverage for most routine childhood immunisations. The assessment is part of the latest WHO-UNICEF Estimates of National Immunisation Coverage (WUENIC), released on July 15, which said that while global immunisation services have continued to recover, millions of children still miss life-saving vaccines, particularly those given at birth or requiring completion of multi-dose schedules. For India, the sharpest divergence between official administrative data and the WHO-UNICEF estimate relates to the hepatitis B birth dose (HEPBB), which is recommended within the first 24 hours of birth. The vaccine is critical because hepatitis B virus can be transmitted from an infected mother to her baby during childbirth, leading to chronic infection in up to 90 percent of infected infants. Chronic hepatitis B substantially raises the risk of liver cirrhosis and liver cancer later in life, making timely birth-dose vaccination one of the most effective preventive interventions. The Indian government reported 92 percent coverage for the hepatitis B birth dose in 2025, which, like several other vaccines, is offered free of cost to infants under the Centre's Universal Immunisation Programme (UIP). The WHO and UNICEF estimated actual national coverage at only 70 percent, indicating that nearly three in every 10 newborns missed the vaccine within the recommended time window. The agencies noted that the reported 92 percent coverage reflected vaccinations achieved in only 76 percent of the intended target population after annualising coverage across the national birth cohort. The estimate has been marked as “Estimate challenged by: R-”, indicating that WHO and UNICEF consider the nationally reported administrative data inconsistent with their final estimate because of concerns over the reported coverage denominator and target population. The agencies also reported they are aware that India is conducting a new National Family Health Survey (NFHS-6 came out last month but was likely not factored in for the immunisation report card) and they will review its findings once the final results become available. India Today has reached out to the Union health ministry for its comments. This copy will be updated if and when a reply is received. STRONG COVERAGE REPORT Despite the disagreement over the hepatitis B birth dose, India’s routine immunisation programme continued to register high estimated coverage across most childhood vaccines in 2025. The first dose of the diphtheria, tetanus and pertussis (DTP1) vaccine reached an estimated 97 percent of surviving infants, suggesting that only about 3% of children remained “zero-dose” for routine immunisation. Coverage for the first dose of the measles-containing vaccine and the first dose of the rubella-containing vaccine stood at 98 percent each. Coverage was estimated at 97% for the third dose of pneumococcal conjugate vaccine, while both the third dose of the DTP vaccine and the third dose of the hepatitis B vaccine reached 95 percent. The first dose of inactivated polio vaccine also achieved 95% coverage. Bacille Calmette-Guerin (BCG) and the final dose of rotavirus vaccine were each estimated at 91 percent. Among all vaccines, however, the hepatitis B birth dose recorded the largest proportion of missed children, with 30 percent of newborns not receiving the vaccine within 24 hours of birth. Globally, WHO and UNICEF estimated that 13.5 million children remained “zero-dose” in 2025, meaning they did not receive a single vaccine during their first year of life. While that represented nearly 750,000 fewer children than in 2024, the agencies cautioned that increasing numbers of children begin vaccinations but fail to complete the recommended schedule. The report also highlighted encouraging regional trends. Compared with pre-pandemic levels in 2019, the WHO South-East Asia Region, which includes India, has not only recovered but now records the highest immunisation performance among WHO regions. Africa, the Eastern Mediterranean and Europe have improved but remain below their pre-pandemic coverage levels, while the Western Pacific has experienced further declines and is now the furthest below its 2019 baseline

Time
Publication date: 2026-07-15. Event date: 2025 (coverage data). WHO-UNICEF WUENIC report released on 2026-07-15.
Location and population
Target population: newborns within 24 hours of birth.
Event
WHO and UNICEF questioned India’s official hepatitis B birth-dose coverage estimate (92% reported vs. 70% estimated by agencies) as part of the 2026 WUENIC report. The agencies flagged inconsistencies in coverage denominators and target population calculations. This is a preliminary assessment pending review of India’s NFHS-6 survey results.
Scale, impact and risk
India reported 92% hepatitis B birth-dose coverage in 2025, but WHO-UNICEF estimate suggests only 70% coverage, implying ~30% of newborns missed the vaccine within 24 hours. Chronic hepatitis B risk from mother-to-child transmission remains high for unvaccinated infants. Globally, 13.5 million children were 'zero-dose' in 2025, with 750,000 fewer than in 2024.
Response, status and uncertainty
WHO and UNICEF are reviewing India’s NFHS-6 survey results for reconciliation. India’s routine immunisation remains high for other vaccines (e.g., DTP1 at 97%, measles at 98%). The agencies have marked the hepatitis B birth-dose estimate as 'challenged' due to denominator concerns. No official response from India’s health ministry was provided in the report.

验尸官对缺乏关于乙型肝炎再激活处方的国家指导表示担忧 - The Pharmaceutical Journal

Coroner raises concerns over lack of national guidance on prescribing for hepatitis B reactivation - The Pharmaceutical Journal
完整新闻中文摘要英国一名助理验尸官对 41 岁患者 Tung Tran 死亡后缺乏关于乙型肝炎重新激活处方和监测的国家指导表示担忧。Tran 于 2013 年接受了肾移植,并被诊断患有慢性乙型肝炎,并于 2025 年 9 月因无意中止恩替卡韦预防治疗后病毒重新激活的并发症而死亡。验尸官向英国肝脏研究协会和英国健康安全局提出了担忧,并指出通过急诊科筛查发现了“大量”患者,但缺乏专门的委托来维持与服务的接触。BASL 回应称,它不主导指南的制定,慢性乙型肝炎的监测和处方通常由专业肝病服务机构负责,但它承认 NHS 信托机构之间存在差异。
查看新闻五要素
时间
《预防未来死亡》报告于 2026 年 7 月 10 日在线发布 (N3)。该患者于 2025 年 9 月 12 日死亡(N9)。该文章发表于2026年7月16日(N2)。
地点与对象
该事件发生在英国,特别涉及一名在内伦敦北部(N3、N9)接受治疗的患者。验尸官指出,“大量”患者通过急诊科的选择性退出筛查被诊断出患有乙型肝炎(N2、N9)。
事件
内伦敦北区的助理验尸官理查德·布里顿 (Richard Brittain) 在 41 岁的患者 Tung Tran 先生因无意中停用恩替卡韦 (N3、N9) 引起乙型肝炎再激活并发症而死亡后,发布了一份“预防未来死亡”报告。
规模、影响与风险
患者死亡(N9)。验尸官发现,国家缺乏关于乙型肝炎重新激活的处方和监测的指导(N3、N9)。他指出,“大量”乙型肝炎患者选择退出筛查,但缺乏专门的委托来维持与服务的接触(N2、N9)。验尸官表示,这与丙型肝炎和艾滋病毒服务 (N10) 不同。
应对、状态与不确定性
验尸官将关注点转至英国病毒性肝炎组(隶属于英国肝脏研究协会,BASL)和英国卫生安全局(UKHSA)(N9)。BASL于2026年7月6日作出回应,表示其不负责指南制定,且慢性乙型肝炎的监测和处方通常由专科肝病服务负责(N11,N12)。BASL指出,各NHS信托机构在监测工作是由治疗团队还是专科服务提供方面存在差异(N13)。
B routine priority

Coroner raises concerns over lack of national guidance on prescribing for hepatitis B reactivation - The Pharmaceutical Journal

Fetched Original Body
Coroner raises concerns over lack of national guidance on prescribing for hepatitis B reactivation A patient died from complications from reactivation of hepatitis B, after the supply of viral reactivation prophylaxis was inadvertently discontinued. Patient safety 16 July 2026 By Sophie Willis Advertisement Copy link to page Download PDF The coroner wrote that although a “large population” of patients have been diagnosed with hepatitis B through opt-out screening in emergency departments, specialised commissioning is lacking to ensure engagement with services Olga Yastremska / Alamy Stock Photo A coroner has raised concerns over a lack of national guidance on prescribing and monitoring for hepatitis B reactivation, following the death of a patient. In a ‘ Prevention of future deaths’ report ‘, published online on 10 July 2026, Richard Brittain, assistant coroner for Inner London North, stated that patient Tung Tran, aged 41 years, died from complications from reactivation of hepatitis B, “which arose from inadvertent discontinuation of necessary medical treatment”. Tran had a renal transplant in 2013 and was diagnosed with chronic hepatitis B during the process, the coroner wrote. The patient was later discharged from hepatology and continued to receive viral reactivation prophylaxis entecavir from renal transplant services. “Supply of medication changed to home delivery in early 2025 but the entecavir was inadvertently discontinued, as there was a presumption that hepatology would continue to prescribe this. Mr Tran appears to have understood this to have been an intentional change of his medication,” the coroner said. “He attended his local hospital in August 2025 with signs of acute liver disease from reactivation of hepatitis B and was transferred to the Royal Free Hospital. He was too unwell to be considered for liver transplant and sadly died on 12 September 2025.” Directing a concern over the risk of future deaths to the British Viral Hepatitis Group at the British Association for the Study of the Liver (BASL), the coroner noted: “There is a lack of national guidance regarding which services should be responsible for the monitoring and prescribing in relation to hepatitis B reactivation prevention.” He also directed a concern to the UK Health Security Agency that there is a “large population” of patients who have been found to have hepatitis B through opt-out screening in emergency departments, “but there is a lack of specialised commissioning to maintain subsequent engagement with services”. “I heard that this differs from the position with regards to hepatitis C and HIV services, even though these patient populations are smaller,” the coroner added. In its response to the report , sent on 6 July 2026, the BASL said: [The BASL] does not lead the development or publication of clinical guidelines.” Patients with chronic hepatitis B are “usually managed in specialist hepatology services and the responsibility of monitoring and prescribing lies with these services”, it added. The BASL continued that for patients at risk of reactivation of hepatitis B, guidance on antiviral therapy is provided by “the relevant specialist societies and also in local NHS trust guidance”. “There is variation between NHS trusts as to whether this monitoring and prescribing is delivered by the treating team or whether specialist hepatology services are involved,” it said. Read more: Hepatitis B — prevention, diagnosis and management Last updated 16 July 2026 14:33 Citation The Pharmaceutical Journal , PJ July 2026, Vol 320, No 8011;320(8011)::DOI:10.1211/PJ.2026.1.419732 Please leave a comment Cancel reply You must be logged in to post a comment. Time
The Prevention of Future Deaths report was published online on 10 July 2026 (N3). The patient died on 12 September 2025 (N9). The article was published on 16 July 2026 (N2).
Location and population
The event occurred in the UK, specifically involving a patient treated in Inner London North (N3, N9). The coroner noted a 'large population' of patients diagnosed with hepatitis B through opt-out screening in emergency departments (N2, N9).
Event
An assistant coroner for Inner London North, Richard Brittain, issued a 'Prevention of Future Deaths' report following the death of a patient, Mr. Tung Tran, aged 41, due to complications from hepatitis B reactivation caused by the inadvertent discontinuation of entecavir (N3, N9).
Scale, impact and risk
The patient died (N9). The coroner identified a lack of national guidance on prescribing and monitoring for hepatitis B reactivation (N3, N9). He noted a 'large population' of patients with hepatitis B from opt-out screening but a lack of specialised commissioning to maintain engagement with services (N2, N9). The coroner stated that this differs from hepatitis C and HIV services (N10).
Response, status and uncertainty
The coroner directed concerns to the British Viral Hepatitis Group at the British Association for the Study of the Liver (BASL) and the UK Health Security Agency (UKHSA) (N9). The BASL responded on 6 July 2026, stating it does not lead guideline development and that monitoring and prescribing for chronic hepatitis B usually lies with specialist hepatology services (N11, N12). The BASL noted variation between NHS trusts regarding whether monitoring is delivered by the treating team or specialist services (N13).

女性新生儿更有可能错过维生素 K 预防、乙型肝炎出生剂量 - The American Journal of Managed Care® (AJMC®)

Female Newborns More Likely to Miss Vitamin K Prophylaxis, Hepatitis B Birth Dose - The American Journal of Managed Care® (AJMC®)

中文翻译暂不可用；以下中文显示位置使用已核验英文内容填充。

完整新闻中文摘要A retrospective cohort study of 93,163 live births in Philadelphia found that female newborns were more than twice as likely as boys to have their parents decline vitamin K prophylaxis, with rates rising from 9.6 to 19.8 per 1,000 births between 2018 and 2025. While the hepatitis B birth dose gap was smaller, nearly 1 in 6 newborns did not receive it by 2025. The researchers attribute the VK disparity to circumcision incentives for boys and note that a CDC policy shift allowing shared clinical decision-making for HBV may be contributing to declining uptake. The study has limitations regarding generalizability and inability to assess long-term clinical outcomes.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该研究分析了 2018 年 1 月至 2025 年 12 月 (N5) 之间的活产情况。AJMC 文章发表于 2026 年 7 月 14 日 (N32)。
地点与对象
该分析重点关注费城 (N5) 宾夕法尼亚大学卫生系统三所医院的 93,163 名活产婴儿。受影响人群包括新生儿,特别是比较男性和女性新生儿(N1、N8、N12)。
事件
一项回顾性队列研究调查了新生儿性别是否会影响父母在出生时预防维生素 K (VK) 和接种乙型肝炎 (HBV) 疫苗 (N5) 的决定。研究结果表明,父母拒绝 VK 预防的女性新生儿的可能性是男孩的两倍多 (N1、N8)。
规模、影响与风险
在队列中,777名婴儿(每1000例活产中8.3例)未接受VK预防,9400名(每1000例活产中100.9例)未接受HBV出生剂量(N6)。女性新生儿VK下降率从2018年的每1000例活产9.6例上升至2025年的每1000例活产19.8例(N11)。到2025年,近1/6的新生儿未接受HBV出生剂量(N14)。未接受VK预防的婴儿发生危及生命的VKDB(维生素K缺乏出血)的风险增加(N2,N4)。
应对、状态与不确定性
该研究是在单一卫生系统内进行的,可能限制普遍性(N25)。作者无法评估出生住院之后的后续临床结果,例如 VKDB 或新生儿乙型肝炎感染 (N26)。作者呼吁开展更多研究,以更好地了解观察到的差异,并解决全国范围内新生儿 VK 预防和 HBV 给药的性别特异性比率(N27、N28)。
B routine priority

Female Newborns More Likely to Miss Vitamin K Prophylaxis, Hepatitis B Birth Dose - The American Journal of Managed Care® (AJMC®)

Fetched Original Body
Newborn girls in a large Philadelphia health system were more than twice as likely as boys to have their parents decline vitamin K (VK) prophylaxis at birth and modestly more likely to miss the hepatitis B vaccine (HBV), according to a research letter published in JAMA Network Open . 1 Sex-Based Differences in Newborn VK and HBV Vaccine Declines Intramuscular VK prophylaxis is routinely recommended to prevent vitamin K deficiency bleeding (VKDB), a potentially life-threatening condition that can cause intracranial or gastrointestinal hemorrhage. HBV administration within 24 hours of birth has also been recommended to reduce vertical transmission and subsequent chronic liver disease. Because infants who do not receive VK prophylaxis face an increased bleeding risk, many hospitals do not perform circumcision without it. To investigate whether newborn sex influences parental decisions regarding these interventions, researchers conducted a retrospective cohort study of 93,163 live births at 3 University of Pennsylvania health system hospitals in Philadelphia between January 2018 and December 2025. Among these births, 777 infants (8.3 per 1000 births) did not receive intramuscular VK prophylaxis, and 9400 (100.9 per 1000 births) did not receive the HBV birth dose. Notably, 83% of infants whose parents declined VK also went without HBV. After adjustment for birth hospital, birth year, maternal insurance, race, ethnicity, delivery mode, and gestational age, female newborns had more than double the odds of VK nonreceipt compared with male newborns (adjusted OR [aOR], 2.03; 95% CI, 1.74-2.35; P < .001). The sex gap for HBV was narrower but still significant (aOR, 1.06; 95% CI, 1.01-1.10; P = .02). Both trends worsened over the study period. VK decline rates among female newborns rose from 9.6 per 1000 births in 2018 to 19.8 per 1000 in 2025, an annual increase of 1.37 per 1000 female births (95% CI, 0.40-2.36; P = .01). Among male newborns, VK decline increased from 4.0 to 10.1 per 1000 births over the same period. HBV decline rose even more sharply for both sexes, increasing among male newborns from 77.9 per 1000 births in 2018 to 166.3 per 1000 births in 2025 and among female newborns from 86.4 to 173.7 per 1000 births over the same period. By 2025, nearly 1 in 6 newborns in the cohort did not receive the HBV birth dose. Circumcision Incentives May Help Explain Sex-Based Differences The authors pointed to circumcision as a likely driver of the VK disparity. Because VK prophylaxis nonreceipt often precludes circumcision at many birth hospitals due to a 6-fold increased risk of bleeding, parents who want their son circumcised may accept VK even if they are otherwise hesitant. Circumcision rates in the cohort ranged from 72% to 77% annually, and no circumcisions were performed among newborns who did not receive VK prophylaxis. That built-in incentive does not exist for female newborns, which the researchers said may explain why hesitant parents are more likely to decline VK prophylaxis when the infant is a girl. However, no comparable procedural incentive explained the smaller but still significant HBV gap. Although sex disparities in childhood immunization have been documented in low- and middle-income countries, the authors noted that sex-specific differences in HBV uptake are not well described in the literature. HBV Policy Shift Raises Concerns About Declining Uptake At the same time, the rise in HBV decline documented in this cohort occurs amid a broader policy shift. In December 2025, the CDC adopted a policy change allowing shared clinical decision-making, rather than a universal recommendation, for the HBV birth dose in infants born to mothers who are HBV-negative, marking the first departure from universal birth-dose guidance in more than 30 years. 2 That shift has raised concern among public health experts that moving from a universal recommendation to shared clinical decision-making could reduce coverage over time, jeopardizing a strategy that has contributed to a 99% reduction in pediatric HBV infections. 3 The authors of the current study describe the same tension, noting that the policy change conflicts with American Academy of Pediatrics guidance and may reinforce vaccine misinformation at a moment when HBV decline rates are already accelerating. 1 Additional Research Needed to Understand and Address Newborn Care Disparities The authors concluded by acknowledging several limitations of their study, including that the analysis was conducted within a single health system, potentially limiting generalizability. They also were unable to assess subsequent clinical outcomes, such as VKDB or neonatal hepatitis B infection, beyond the birth hospitalization. As a result, they called for additional research to better understand the observed disparities. “Further studies should address sex-specific rates of newborn VK prophylaxis and HBV administration, VKDB, and neonatal hepatitis B infection nationally,” the authors wrote. “Innovative strategies are required to reduce disparities in VK prophylaxis and HBV administration and limit life-threatening morbidity among female newborns.” References Coggins SA, Flannery DD, Mukhopadhyay S, Puopolo KM. Parental decline of newborn vitamin K and hepatitis B vaccine administration by newborn sex. JAMA Netw Open . 2026;9(6):e2618410. doi:10.1001/jamanetworkopen.2026.18410 Fact sheet hepatitis B immunization. CDC. December 16, 2025. Accessed July 14, 2026. https://cdc.gov/media/releases/2025/fact-sheet-hepatitis-b-immunization.html Joszt L. Misinformation, access gaps threaten hepatitis B elimination goals. AJMC . June 18, 2026. View news five-element analysis
Time
The study analyzed live births between January 2018 and December 2025 (N5). The AJMC article was published on July 14, 2026 (N32).
Location and population
The analysis focused on 93,163 live births at three University of Pennsylvania health system hospitals in Philadelphia (N5). The affected population included newborn infants, specifically comparing male and female newborns (N1, N8, N12).
Event
A retrospective cohort study investigated whether newborn sex influences parental decisions regarding vitamin K (VK) prophylaxis and hepatitis B (HBV) vaccine administration at birth (N5). The findings indicated that female newborns were more than twice as likely as boys to have their parents decline VK prophylaxis (N1, N8).
Scale, impact and risk
Among the cohort, 777 infants (8.3 per 1000 births) did not receive VK prophylaxis, and 9400 (100.9 per 1000 births) did not receive the HBV birth dose (N6). VK decline rates among female newborns rose from 9.6 per 1000 births in 2018 to 19.8 per 1000 in 2025 (N11). By 2025, nearly 1 in 6 newborns did not receive the HBV birth dose (N14). The risk of life-threatening VKDB (vitamin K deficiency bleeding) increases for infants who do not receive VK prophylaxis (N2, N4).
Response, status and uncertainty
The study was conducted within a single health system, potentially limiting generalizability (N25). The authors were unable to assess subsequent clinical outcomes, such as VKDB or neonatal hepatitis B infection, beyond the birth hospitalization (N26). The authors called for additional research to better understand the observed disparities and to address sex-specific rates of newborn VK prophylaxis and HBV administration nationally (N27, N28).

卫生局监测23,000名乙肝患者 - 澳门邮报

Health Bureau monitors 23,000 hepatitis B patients - The Macau Post Daily

中文翻译暂不可用；以下中文显示位置使用已核验英文内容填充。

完整新闻中文摘要The Macau Health Bureau (SSM) announced on July 14, 2026, that approximately 23,000 patients with chronic hepatitis B are under medical monitoring. During a press conference on July 13, officials revealed that screening initiatives have expanded to include residents aged 40 and above who have never undergone testing, following a successful program for those aged 50 and older. Data from the 2016 Macao Health Survey indicates a 7.6 percent HBsAg positivity rate among residents aged 18 or above, with an estimated 34,000 infected individuals in the community. The bureau is collaborating with community associations to host public awareness events and will offer free anonymous rapid testing on July 25 as part of World Hepatitis Day activities. Vaccination programs since the 1980s have significantly reduced positivity rates among younger generations.
查看新闻五要素
时间
该新闻报道于2026年7月14日发布,报道了昨天(2026年7月13日)举行的新闻发布会。本文讨论了自去年以来持续进行的监测以及计划于 2026 年 7 月 25 日举行的未来活动。
地点与对象
受影响人群包括约23,000名接受医学监测的慢性乙型肝炎患者,以及根据2016年的数据估计34,000名18岁或以上的受感染当地居民。该举措针对的是40岁及以上从未接受过检测的居民。
事件
卫生局宣布就预防和治疗乙型肝炎举行新闻发布会。活动包括监测23,000名慢性乙型肝炎患者、扩大乙型肝炎表面抗原筛查范围至40岁或以上的居民,以及举办世界肝炎日宣传活动和免费检测活动。
规模、影响与风险
As of the end of last year, about 15,000 residents participated in the screening scheme. The HBsAg positivity rate among local residents aged 18 or above was 7.6 percent, with an estimated 34,000 infected residents. Infections are predominantly among those over 50 (70 percent). The positivity rate among children under 5 was 0.03 percent, meeting WHO standards.
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生局正在积极监测23,000名患者,并将筛查范围扩大到40岁或以上的居民。定于 7 月 25 日进行免费匿名快速检测。该局正在与社区协会合作开展公众意识活动。最新一期澳门健康调查初步结果预计将于年底公布。
B routine priority

Health Bureau monitors 23,000 hepatitis B patients - The Macau Post Daily

Fetched Original Body
Free anonymous rapid tests slated for July 25 The Health Bureau (SSM) said during a press conference yesterday that about 23,000 patients with chronic hepatitis B are currently under medical monitoring and management. The press conference about the prevention and treatment of hepatitis was held at the bureau’s Public Health Emergency Personnel Residence Hall on Guia Hill yesterday. News SSM Tap Seac Health Centre Director Ng Sio Fan pointed out that starting in February of last year Health Centres’ internal system automatically added the HBsAg test* for individuals aged 50 or above who lacked a testing record. Since last month, this initiative has expanded to include individuals aged 40 and above who have never undergone the test. As of the end of last year, about 15,000 residents participated in the screening scheme. SSM Centre for Disease Control and Prevention senior official Chang Fong Nei noted that according to the data from the 2016 Macao Health Survey and the Statistics and Census Bureau’s 2016 Mid-term Population Census, the HBsAg positivity rate among local residents aged 18 or above was 7.6 percent, identifying Macau as a moderate prevalence region, with an estimated 34,000 infected local residents in the community. The age structure of Hepatitis B infections in Macau is predominantly composed of individuals over the age of 50, accounting for about 70 percent of all positive cases, she said. The latest once-in-a-decade Macao Health Survey has been conducted this year, and preliminary results are expected to be announced by the end of the year. Chang pointed out that the positivity rate among children under the age of 5 was 0.03 percent in Macau last year, meeting the World Health Organisation (WHO) international standard of a paediatric positivity rate of less or equal 0.1 percent. Chang said that vaccination has yielded significant epidemic prevention results since Macau introduced the Hepatitis B vaccine in the 1980s and incorporated it into the routine vaccination programme in the early 1990s. Consequently, the Hepatitis B positivity rate among residents born in the 1990s has dropped to about 2 percent. Public awareness & free screening campaigns Meanwhile, the bureau, in collaboration with local community associations, will jointly host “Healthy Community Flash Mob Stations” on Saturday from 2:00 p.m. to 6:00 p.m. across the peninsula, Taipa and Coloane, featuring five designated activity routes, with the aim of correcting knowledge regarding the prevention and treatment of Hepatitis B through interactive games, and participants who complete the games can receive “small gifts”, Chang added. Additionally, the “2026 World Hepatitis Day” Theme Event slated for July 25, from 2:00 p.m. to 5:00 p.m., at the public Activities Centre on Rotunda de Carlos da Maia, colloquially known as “Three Lamps” (三盞燈), will feature interactive booths and anonymous rapid Hepatitis B surface antigen testing services free of charge, aiming to assist residents in checking their infection status and to elevate public focus on Hepatitis B prevention and control. Infectious Diseases World Hepatitis Day, however, takes place annually on July 28. * An HBsAg test (Hepatitis B surface Antigen test) is a blood test used to determine if a person is currently infected with the Hepatitis B virus (HBV). The test looks for Hepatitis B surface antigens, which are proteins present on the outer surface of the virus. It is the primary and most common screening tool for Hepatitis B. – Gemini Health Bureau (SSM) Tap Seac Health Centre Director Ng Sio Fan (centre), SSM Centre for Disease Control and Prevention senior official Chang Fong Nei (right) and Sum Ip Pio from the public Conde de S. Januário Hospital Centre (CHCSJ) address the press conference about the prevention and treatment of hepatitis at the bureau’s Public Health Emergency Personnel Residence Hall on Guia Hill yesterday. – Photo: Ida Cheong Health
View news five-element analysis
Time
The news report was published on July 14, 2026, covering a press conference held yesterday (July 13, 2026). The article discusses ongoing monitoring since last year and future events scheduled for July 25, 2026.
Location and population
The affected population includes approximately 23,000 patients with chronic hepatitis B under medical monitoring, and an estimated 34,000 infected local residents aged 18 or above based on 2016 data. The initiative targets residents aged 40 and above who have never undergone testing.
Event
The Health Bureau (SSM) announced a press conference regarding the prevention and treatment of hepatitis B. The event includes monitoring 23,000 chronic hepatitis B patients, expanding HBsAg screening to residents aged 40 and above, and organizing public awareness campaigns and free testing events for World Hepatitis Day.
Scale, impact and risk
As of the end of last year, about 15,000 residents participated in the screening scheme. The HBsAg positivity rate among local residents aged 18 or above was 7.6 percent, with an estimated 34,000 infected residents. Infections are predominantly among those over 50 (70 percent). The positivity rate among children under 5 was 0.03 percent, meeting WHO standards.
Response, status and uncertainty
The Health Bureau is actively monitoring 23,000 patients and expanding screening to residents aged 40 and above. Free anonymous rapid tests are slated for July 25. The bureau is collaborating with community associations for public awareness events. Preliminary results of the latest Macao Health Survey are expected by the end of the year.

Health Bureau Follows Up 23,000 Hepatitis B Patients - TDM

中文翻译暂不可用；以下中文显示位置使用已核验英文内容填充。

完整新闻中文摘要The Health Bureau of Macau is following up on 23,000 hepatitis B patients following a free screening program. The screening results indicate that approximately 6.8 percent of residents aged 50 and above have tested positive for the virus. This year, the bureau has expanded eligibility for the free screening to include residents from the age of 40. The results are part of a once-a-decade health survey expected to be released in the fourth quarter of 2026.
查看新闻五要素
时间
数据报告时间为2026-07-14 21:51。筛查结果是预计将于2026年第四季度发布的一项每十年一次的健康调查的一部分。
地点与对象
澳门,50岁及以上居民(今年扩大至40岁居民)。
事件
The Health Bureau of Macau is following up on 23,000 hepatitis B patients following a free screening program. The screening results indicate that approximately 6.8 percent of residents aged 50 and above have tested positive for the virus.
规模、影响与风险
The screening covered a significant portion of the elderly population, with a 6.8 percent positivity rate among those aged 50 and above. The bureau has expanded eligibility to include residents aged 40. The results are part of a once-a-decade health survey.
应对、状态与不确定性
卫生局正积极跟进已确诊的23,000名患者。该结果是初步结果,是将于 2026 年第四季度发布的更大规模调查的一部分。来源是 TDM 的完整报告。

Macau, residents aged 50 and above (expanded to include residents aged 40 this year).
Event
The Health Bureau of Macau is following up on 23,000 hepatitis B patients following a free screening program. The screening results indicate that approximately 6.8 percent of residents aged 50 and above have tested positive for the virus.
Scale, impact and risk
The screening covered a significant portion of the elderly population, with a 6.8 percent positivity rate among those aged 50 and above. The bureau has expanded eligibility to include residents aged 40. The results are part of a once-a-decade health survey.
Response, status and uncertainty

世卫组织加强土库曼斯坦医疗专业人员应对乙型病毒性肝炎的能力 - Turkmenportal.com

WHO strengthens medical professionals’ capacity in Turkmenistan on viral hepatitis B - Turkmenportal.com
完整新闻中文摘要世界卫生组织驻土库曼斯坦国家办事处于2025年3月12日至13日举办了“根据世界卫生组织建议和国家方案诊断和治疗乙型病毒性肝炎”培训课程。该课程由世界卫生组织/欧洲顾问Alexander Nersesov教授主持,旨在提高土库曼斯坦医疗专业人员抗击病毒性乙型肝炎的能力。参加者包括医生、初级卫生保健人员、传染病专家以及以国家命名的国立医科大学的代表。迈拉特·卡里耶夫。培训涵盖了乙型肝炎及其诊断、分类和治疗的基本信息,并对慢性病例、妊娠和特殊患者群体以及丁型肝炎提供了具体指导。该举措预计将改善患者的治疗结果,并加强土库曼斯坦控制和管理病毒性肝炎的努力。培训以视频会议模式进行,符合世卫组织指南和国家方案。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该活动发生于2025年3月12日至13日,文章发表于2025年3月18日。
地点与对象
培训在土库曼斯坦举行,特别是在传染病治疗预防中心。参与者包括医生、初级卫生保健提供者、传染病专家以及以迈拉特·卡里耶夫命名的国立医科大学的代表。
事件
世界卫生组织(WHO)驻土库曼斯坦国家办事处举办了“根据世界卫生组织建议和国家方案诊断和治疗乙型病毒性肝炎”的培训课程。该课程由世界卫生组织/欧洲顾问 Alexander Nersesov 教授主持,涵盖了乙型肝炎及其诊断、分类和治疗的基本信息。
规模、影响与风险
此次培训旨在提高土库曼斯坦医疗专业人员抗击乙型病毒性肝炎的能力。该举措预计将改善患者的治疗效果,并加强土库曼斯坦控制和管理病毒性肝炎的努力。没有报告具体病例数、死亡或暴露情况。
应对、状态与不确定性
培训以视频会议方式进行,内容涵盖乙型病毒性肝炎诊断和治疗的最新知识和最佳实践。该倡议符合世卫组织指南和国家议定书。来源是 Turkmenportal.com 的完整文章。

WHO strengthens medical professionals’ capacity in Turkmenistan on viral hepatitis B - Turkmenportal.com

Fetched Original Body
Culture Economics Education In the world Sport Tourism Technologies Health Energy Society Transport WC-2026 Business Politics Culture Economics Education In the world Sport Tourism Technologies Health Energy Society Transport WC-2026 Business Politics Culture Economics Education In the world Sport Tourism Technologies Health All categories Main / News / Health 18.03.2025 13344 Share on social network WHO strengthens medical professionals’ capacity in Turkmenistan on viral hepatitis B The World Health Organization (WHO) Country Office in Turkmenistan held a training course on the "Diagnostics and treatment of viral hepatitis B according to WHO recommendations and national protocol" from March 12 to 13, 2025. The course led by WHO/Europe consultant, Professor Alexander Nersesov, aims to enhance the capacity of Turkmen medical professionals in their effort to combat viral hepatitis B. The skill-building session was conducted at the Infectious disease treatment-prevention center in videoconference mode. The participants of the event were doctors specializing in the diagnosis and treatment of viral hepatitis from the Infectious disease treatment-prevention center of the Directorate of Infectious Diseases Centers, primary health care providers, infectious diseases specialists from the institutions at the district level, and representatives from the department of infectious diseases at the State Medical University named after Myrat Karryev. The training covered essential information on hepatitis B, its diagnosis, classification, and treatment, with specific guidance for chronic cases, pregnancy, and special patient groups, as well as hepatitis D. This training is designed to equip medical professionals with the updated knowledge and best practices in viral hepatitis B diagnosis and treatment, aligning with both WHO guidelines and national protocols. By enhancing the skills of healthcare providers, this initiative will contribute to improved patient outcomes and strengthen Turkmenistan's efforts to control and manage viral hepatitis. ALGS Stock Rallies 16% After-Hours On $25M Cash Boost From China Hepatitis B Drug Deal - Stocktwits

Fetched Original Body
Shares of Aligos Therapeutics (ALGS) rose 21% in after-hours trading on Monday after the company announced it had received a $25-million upfront payment from its Chinese licensing partner. The payment gives Aligos non-dilutive funding as it advances its pipeline of treatments for liver and viral diseases. Aligos Therapeutics had only $54.9 million in cash, cash equivalents, and short-term investments as of March 31, 2026. Under the licensing deal signed earlier this year, Aligos granted Xiamen Amoytop Biotech exclusive rights to develop and sell its experimental hepatitis B treatment, pevifoscorvir sodium, in Greater China. Aligos keeps full rights to the drug in the United States, Europe, Japan, South Korea, and all other markets. Under the deal, Aligos is eligible for up to $420 million in milestone payments tied to clinical, regulatory and sales achievements. It will also earn tiered royalties in the high single digits on net sales in the licensed region. The treatment has already received Breakthrough Therapy Designation from Chinese regulators, which can speed up the approval process. A joint steering committee between the two companies is now overseeing the program. As part of the broader collaboration, the partners also plan to start clinical testing in China of another Aligos drug candidate in the third quarter of this year. On Stocktwits, retail sentiment around ALGS stock jumped from ‘neutral’ to ‘bullish’ over the past 24 hours, while message volume stayed at ‘high’ levels. A Stocktwits user opined that the stock is highly undervalued. “Cash-in now, but the real read is whether this deal de-risks the story and adds validation for the asset. Market will watch follow-through on the platform, not just the headline,” another user wrote. ALGS stock has fallen by about 37% year-to-date. Zeta Global Holdings (ZETA) share price jumped 5.4% on Monday and extended gains by 2.5% after hours, after the company’s CEO said it is evolving from marketing to an AI infrastructure firm. “For years, we've been building what many have viewed as a marketing platform... today marks the beginning of our next chapter,” David Steinberg, CEO of Zeta said in a post on X. “Marketing was our first application and still very important for our clients. Today, we're expanding beyond customer acquisition, retention, and monetization into a fourth use case, business intelligence. We call it the Zeta Business Intelligence (ZBI) Engine, helping organizations make better decisions with AI,” Steinberg added. The company had recently partnered with Palantir Technologies (PLTR) to build an enterprise AI infrastructure layer for agentic marketing. Earlier last week, Zeta announced a partnership with Palantir to build the enterprise AI infrastructure layer that connects operational intelligence, customer intelligence and marketing execution. The partnership pairs two complementary platforms in a deep technical integration. Palantir Foundry brings the ontology, governance and operational infrastructure that enterprise data demands. Zeta's Data Cloud will be rearchitected on Foundry, with Athena, Zeta's AI-powered intelligence layer, turning that data into decisions and measurable outcomes at enterprise scale. “Palantir and Zeta are using Ontology to create a next generation marketing environment, giving Zeta all the advantages of AI while protecting against many of the known dangers,” said Alex Karp, co-founder and CEO of Palantir Technologies. “Bringing together containerized architecture and AI in the context of marketing will transform this industry.” Retail sentiment on Stocktwits was ‘bullish’ with ‘normal’ message volumes. Hertz Global Holdings (HTZ) share price dropped 3.3% on Monday to a record low, marking the ninth straight session of declines. The drop has intensified short-selling pressure on the stock, data from S3 Partners showed. The dramatic drop was set off by a multi-pronged financial update that severely disappointed Wall Street. Hertz issued a profit warning and reported preliminary second-quarter adjusted corporate EBITDA that missed consensus analyst expectations. Following the announcements, the stock plunged a record 41% in a single session—marking its steepest one-day decline since its 2021 post-bankruptcy relisting—before continuing its downward trajectory. As the stock rapidly devalued, bearish traders moved in quickly to maximize their positions. As of Thursday's close, more than 56% of Hertz’s float has been borrowed by traders betting on its stock price falling further, S3 Partners data showed as reported by Bloomberg. “Short interest has increased dramatically,” said Ihor Dusaniwsky, managing director at S3 told Bloomberg. Hertz has seen an additional 13.7 million shares shorted — worth $30 million — since June 24, according to Dusaniwsky. That means about 20% of short positions have been opened in less than two weeks. Hertz stock fell to record lows as concerns about the company’s rising vehicle depreciation costs and weakness in the used-car market have triggered a sell-off. The rental car company also slashed its second-quarter earnings forecast to a range of $50 million to $80 million, citing unexpected weakness in the used car market. Last month, the company also completed a $350 million secured exchangeable notes offering, with an option to raise an additional $50 million, which it said would help boost liquidity and financial flexibility as it advances its turnaround efforts. Retail sentiment on Stocktwits was ‘bullish’ with ‘high’ message volumes. Retail chatter on the stock surged over 1,600% from the previous session. HTZ stock has lost 62% year-to-date. For updates and corrections, email newsroom[at]stocktwits[dot]com. Comments posted here will also appear on symbol pages. Shares of Rivian Automotive Inc. (RIVN) dropped around 9% in after-hours trading on Monday despite the company reporting preliminary second-quarter revenue estimates above Wall Street estimates, as investors focused on the company's intent to sell 75 million shares in a public offering. Depending on the stock’s Monday closing price of $20.41, the base offering represents gross proceeds of about $1.5 billion. Rivian said it intends to use the net proceeds from the offering for general corporate purposes, including funding certain equity contribution requirements tied to its loan arrangement with the U.S. Department of Energy. The equity raise comes as Rivian advances its R2 platform launch, expands manufacturing capacity in Georgia, and progresses on autonomy and commercial vehicle initiatives. In a separate filing, the company provided preliminary consolidated revenue estimates of $1.55 billion to $1.65 billion for Q2 2026, up from $1.30 billion in the year-ago quarter. The increase was driven primarily by higher vehicle volumes, partially offset by a higher mix of lower-priced commercial vans. Rivian also estimated cash, cash equivalents, and short-term investments at approximately $5.3 billion as of June 30, 2026, up from $4.8 billion at the end of the first quarter, owing to investments from partners Volkswagen Group and Uber Technologies. Full second-quarter financials are now slated for July 30. Last week, Rivian reported that it delivered 12,194 vehicles in the second quarter of 2026, exceeding its own guidance range of 9,000–11,000 units. The company produced 12,613 vehicles during the period. Strong demand for R1 trucks and SUVs, continued commercial van sales, and initial customer deliveries of the new R2 SUV contributed to the beat. The company subsequently also raised its full-year 2026 delivery guidance to 65,000–70,000 vehicles, sending shares 8% higher on Thursday. On Stocktwits, retail sentiment around RIVN stock stayed within the ‘bullish’ territory over the past 24 hours, while message volume remained at ‘high’ levels. A Stocktwits user expressed optimism for the upbeat revenue guidance, sending the shares rallying on Tuesday. Another user voiced hopes for the company’s strengthened balance sheet. A third user framed the offering as essential to the company’s plans to advance R2 SUV production. Another, however, raised concerns about dilution. RIVN stock has gained 2% year-to-date. Elon Musk's artificial intelligence startup xAI rebranded as SpaceXAI and unveiled a new logo to reflect its full integration into SpaceX on Monday. The new logo and the official rechristening were announced in a post on X. Shares of SPCX closed down 1% on Monday ahead of the company joining the Nasdaq-100 index before market open on July 7. Musk confirmed in May that his artificial intelligence startup xAI will be dissolved as a standalone company and fully integrated into SpaceX, rebranding its AI efforts as SpaceXAI. SpaceX had already absorbed xAI in a formal acquisition earlier this year, but the new “SpaceXAI” framing highlights that all AI development will now operate directly under the SpaceX umbrella. Musk also confirmed that the company will have a new logo as well. SpaceX’s previous logo was designed by a New Jersey-based creative agency called RO Studio. xAI’s previous logo, the simple ‘xAI’ wordmark, meanwhile, was designed in-house by one of xAI’s co-founders called Toby Pohlen. The new logo retains parts of the older logos. The updated logo has yet to be reflected on the company’s website or in official filings, suggesting the name is more of a rebrand than an official renaming. It is worth noting that SpaceXAI — the rebranded xAI — is a subsidiary of SpaceX, not SpaceX itself. X, the social media app, is itself a subsidiary of SpaceXAI. SpaceX acquired xAI on February 2, in a record-setting all-stock deal that made xAI a wholly owned subsidiary of SpaceX. xAI investors received 0.1433 shares of SpaceX for every share of xAI, consolidating Musk’s empire and fueling further speculation that the billionaire’s EV company, Tesla, will eventually merge with SpaceX as well. In June, SpaceX debuted on Nasdaq, surging nearly 20% on its first day after the largest IPO in history at $135 per share. In its IPO prospectus, the company laid out what it called the largest actionable total addressable market in history at $28.5 trillion, of which $26.5 trillion is attributed to AI — dwarfing its traditional space ($370 billion) and connectivity ($1.6 trillion) segments. The bulk of the AI opportunity is projected to come from enterprise applications, alongside AI infrastructure, consumer subscriptions, and advertising. On Stocktwits, retail sentiment around SPCX remained in the ‘bearish’ range over the past 24 hours, while message volume remained at ‘extremely low’ levels. A Stocktwits user said that SPCX will plummet to $120 per share. Another user expressed anticipation for SpaceX’s upcoming earnings report. “Key levels, volume, and follow-through will determine whether bulls can regain momentum or if weakness continue,” they wrote. SPCX closed at $160.42 on Monday, still implying a significant 7% premium to its debut price of $150 per share. For updates and corrections, email newsroom[at]stocktwits[dot]com. SpaceXAI is the rebranded identity of xAI, Elon Musk's AI startup, which is a subsidiary of SpaceX. SpaceX itself has not rebranded. SSM加强乙型肝炎筛查和预防 - 澳门日报时报

SSM to step up hepatitis B screening and prevention - macau daily times 澳門每日時報
完整新闻中文摘要澳门卫生局 (SSM) 在周二举行的新闻发布会上于 2026 年 7 月 15 日宣布扩大遏制乙型肝炎感染的举措。该活动以 7 月 28 日世界肝炎日为中心,包括更广泛的筛查、扩大治疗范围和社区外展。官方估计,每100名居民中就有7至8人可能被感染,成年人口表面抗原阳性率为7.6%。现在对 50 岁及以上居民(自 2026 年 6 月起降至 40 岁)自动进行筛查,所有九个公共卫生中心都开设了专门的肝脏诊所。该举措旨在更早地识别隐性感染并减少严重肝病的进展,严重肝病占澳门癌症死亡的 10.5%。
查看新闻五要素
时间
该文章发表于 2026 年 7 月 15 日,报道了卫生局 (SSM) 周二宣布的一项举措,并以 2026 年 7 月 28 日世界肝炎日为中心焦点。
地点与对象
该举措针对的是 50 岁及以上的居民,自 2026 年 6 月起筛查门槛降低至 40 岁。它还针对一般成年人口,目标是到 2025 年底覆盖超过 19,000 名 50 岁及以上的居民。
事件
卫生局 (SSM) 宣布加大力度遏制乙型肝炎感染,包括更广泛的筛查、扩大治疗范围和社区外展。这是一项官方公共卫生倡议。
规模、影响与风险
卫生官员估计,每 100 名居民中就有 7 至 8 人可能被感染。成人乙型肝炎表面抗原阳性率为7.6%(根据2016年调查)。肝癌占澳门癌症死亡人数的10.5%。该计划旨在更早地识别隐性感染并减少进展为严重肝病。
应对、状态与不确定性
自2025年2月起,当局对50岁及以上无记录的居民实施乙肝自动检测,自2026年6月起将门槛降低至40岁。所有9个公共卫生中心均于2024年12月开设了专门的肝脏诊所,截至6月,就诊人数为2,866人次。预计更新的调查结果将于 2026 年第四季度发布。计划举办社区活动,包括 7 月 18 日举办的“健康社区快闪站”和 7 月 25 日举办的世界肝炎日活动,提供免费匿名快速检测。
B routine priority

SSM to step up hepatitis B screening and prevention - macau daily times 澳門每日時報

MACAU DAILY TIMES 澳門每日時報 Top Menu Our Team Editorial Statute Code of Ethics Privacy Policy Terms and Conditions Archive PDF Editions Contacts Extra Times Drive In Book It tTunes Features World of Bacchus Taste of Edesia Main Menu Home Macau Photo Shop Advertorial Interview Greater Bay Business Corporate Bits China Asia World Sports Opinion Editorial Our Desk Business Views China Daily Multipolar World The Conversation World Views Our Team Editorial Statute Code of Ethics Privacy Policy Terms and Conditions Archive PDF Editions Contacts Extra Times Drive In Book It tTunes Features World of Bacchus Taste of Edesia Generic selectors Exact matches only Search in title Search in content Post Type Selectors FOUNDER & PUBLISHER Kowie Geldenhuys EDITOR-IN-CHIEF Paulo Coutinho Macau, Sunday, July 19, 2026 MACAU DAILY TIMES 澳門每日時報 Home Macau Photo Shop Advertorial Interview Greater Bay Business Corporate Bits China Asia World Sports Opinion Editorial Our Desk Business Views China Daily Multipolar World The Conversation World Views Trending: Rape cases rise almost 30%, but security chief says figure may be misleading HeadlinesMacau Home›Headlines›SSM to step up hepatitis B screening and prevention Health SSM to step up hepatitis B screening and prevention By Nadia Shaw, MDTJuly 15, 20261940 Share: The Health Bureau (SSM) on Tuesday announced an expanded push to curb hepatitis B infections. The initiative includes broader screening, expanded treatment access, and community outreach, as officials warned that as many as 7 to 8 out of every 100 residents may be infected based on existing data. The campaign comes as liver cancer remains among the leading causes of cancer deaths in the city. At a morning press conference, authorities outlined a series of measures aimed at early detection and long-term disease management, centered around World Hepatitis Day on July 28. Ng Sio Fan, director of the Tap Seac Health Centre, said screening efforts targeting older residents are already gaining traction. “We estimate that by the end of 2025, through this measure, we will have reached more than 19,000 people aged 50 and above. The infection rate among people aged 50 and above is about 6.8%. According to current data, the Health Bureau is following up on more than 23,000 hepatitis B patients,” she said. Health officials said the hepatitis B surface antigen positivity rate in the adult population stands at 7.6%, based on the latest available survey. Chang Fong Nei, a senior technician in infectious disease control, noted that the figure is derived from a 2016 health survey, with updated results expected in the fourth quarter of 2026 following a new 10-year study. Authorities detailed that since February 2025, hepatitis B testing has been automatically added for residents aged 50 and above with no prior record. Since June 2026, the threshold has been lowered to 40 years old. Authorities said the move is intended to identify silent infections earlier and reduce progression to severe liver disease. Expanded treatment access SSM detailed that all nine public health centers launched dedicated liver clinics in December 2024, and since August that year, eligible patients have been able to receive antiviral prescriptions directly at community clinics. As of June, the clinics had recorded 2,866 visits. Moreover, vaccination remains central to disease prevention. Health officials said positivity among those born in the 1990s has fallen to about 2%, while the rate among children under five is projected to reach 0.03% by 2025, meeting the World Health Organization target of 0.1% or below. According to the 2024 Annual Cancer Registry Report, liver cancer accounts for 10.5% of cancer deaths in Macau, with lung, colorectal, breast, and pancreatic cancers also among the top causes. Together, the five account for nearly two-thirds of all cancer mortality. SSM also announced that it will host community events this month, including a “Healthy Community Pop-up Station” on July 18 and a World Hepatitis Day event on July 25 offering free anonymous rapid testing. Officials also urged residents to maintain healthy lifestyles and avoid behaviors that increase liver strain. View news five-element analysis
Time
The article was published on July 15, 2026, reporting on an initiative announced by the Health Bureau (SSM) on Tuesday, with World Hepatitis Day on July 28, 2026, as a central focus.
Location and population
The initiative targets residents aged 50 and above, with screening thresholds lowered to 40 years old since June 2026. It also addresses the general adult population and aims to reach over 19,000 people aged 50 and above by the end of 2025.
Event
The Health Bureau (SSM) announced an expanded push to curb hepatitis B infections, including broader screening, expanded treatment access, and community outreach. This is an official public health initiative.
Scale, impact and risk
Health officials estimate that 7 to 8 out of every 100 residents may be infected. The hepatitis B surface antigen positivity rate in the adult population is 7.6% (based on a 2016 survey). Liver cancer accounts for 10.5% of cancer deaths in Macau. The initiative aims to identify silent infections earlier and reduce progression to severe liver disease.
Response, status and uncertainty
Authorities have implemented automatic hepatitis B testing for residents aged 50 and above with no prior record since February 2025, lowering the threshold to 40 years old since June 2026. All nine public health centers launched dedicated liver clinics in December 2024, with 2,866 visits recorded as of June. Updated survey results are expected in the fourth quarter of 2026. Community events, including a "Healthy Community Pop-up Station" on July 18 and a World Hepatitis Day event on July 25 offering free anonymous rapid testing, are planned.
